
===== FILE backend/tests/test_inspiration_metadata_runtime.py =====
"""The separate metadata prompt must use the normal paid-call path."""
import asyncio
import json
from copy import deepcopy

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.database import Base
from app.doubao import DoubaoResponse
from app.evaluation_component_catalog import canonical_component_hash
from app.inspiration_metadata_generation import EXECUTION_ROLE, GUIDANCE, PROMPT_STAGE, metadata_contract
from app.models import WorkflowModelInvocation
from app.workflow_model_budget import (
    fail_model_invocation, real_execution_budget_for_selection,
    reserve_model_invocation, settle_model_invocation,
)
from app.workflow_model_executor import (
    WorkflowModelExecutorError, execute_prepared_prompt, prepare_prompt_invocation,
)
from test_inspiration_production_fields import fields
from test_workflow_model_executor import _case, _sha256


@pytest.mark.parametrize('extra_score', [False, True])
def test_metadata_real_executor_contract_and_ledger(tmp_path, monkeypatch, extra_score):
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'metadata-runtime.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run, attempt, node, model, _prompt = _case(db, tmp_path, monkeypatch)
            contract = metadata_contract()
            config = node['config']
            config.update(asset_key=EXECUTION_ROLE, execution_role=EXECUTION_ROLE,
                          output_contract=contract)
            content = config['component_snapshot']['content']
            content.update(stage=PROMPT_STAGE, system_prompt=GUIDANCE,
                           user_prompt='只描述当前待评图片，严格按五个字段返回。')
            config['component_snapshot']['content_hash'] = canonical_component_hash(content)
            node['output_schema'] = deepcopy(contract['schema'])
            snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
            manifest = snapshot['workflow']['manifest']
            manifest.update(category_key='inspiration_image', nodes=[deepcopy(node)])
            snapshot['runtime_context']['category_key'] = 'inspiration_image'
            run.category_key = 'inspiration_image'
            run.snapshot_json = canonical_json(snapshot)
            run.snapshot_hash = _sha256(run.snapshot_json)
            db.flush()
            prepared = prepare_prompt_invocation(db, run=run, attempt=attempt,
                node=node, input_manifest=json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json))
            assert prepared.media_dispatch_identities == ('target:0',)
            assert prepared.system_prompt == GUIDANCE
            assert 'truth-must-not-reach-model' not in prepared.user_prompt
            invocation = reserve_model_invocation(db, attempt_id=attempt.id, node=node,
                run_snapshot=snapshot, reserved_images=1, dispatch_identity=prepared.dispatch_identity)
            generated = {key: fields()[key] for key in contract['schema']['required']}
            if extra_score:
                generated['score'] = 100
            calls = []

            class ProviderStub:
                def __init__(self, config):
                    pass

                async def chat_json(self, system_prompt, user_prompt, **options):
                    calls.append(options)
                    return DoubaoResponse(parsed=generated, raw_text=canonical_json(generated),
                        raw_payload={}, upstream_status_code=200, request_correlation_id='metadata-test',
                        attempt_count=1, output_budget=512, reasoning_effort='high', thinking_mode='enabled',
                        input_tokens=123, output_tokens=45, total_tokens=168)

            if extra_score:
                with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelExecutorError) as error:
                    asyncio.run(execute_prepared_prompt(prepared, client_type=ProviderStub))
                assert error.value.code == 'structured_output_schema_mismatch'
                failed = dict(invocation_id=invocation.id, error_code=error.value.code,
                    error_message=str(error.value), evidence=error.value.evidence,
                    request_correlation_id=error.value.request_correlation_id,
                    upstream_status_code=error.value.upstream_status_code,
                    provider_usage=error.value.provider_usage)
                row = fail_model_invocation(db, **failed)
                assert row.status == 'failed' and row.total_tokens == 168
                assert fail_model_invocation(db, **failed).id == row.id
            else:
                result = asyncio.run(execute_prepared_prompt(prepared, client_type=ProviderStub))
                assert result.node_result['output'] == generated
                settlement = dict(invocation_id=invocation.id, request_correlation_id='metadata-test',
                    upstream_status_code=200, input_tokens=result.input_tokens,
                    output_tokens=result.output_tokens, evidence=result.invocation_evidence)
                row = settle_model_invocation(db, **settlement)
                assert row.status == 'succeeded' and row.total_tokens == 168
                assert settle_model_invocation(db, **settlement).id == row.id
            assert 0 < row.actual_cost_micros <= row.estimated_cost_micros
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(WorkflowModelInvocation)) == 1
            assert len(calls) == 1 and calls[0]['output_contract'].strict is True
            assert calls[0]['output_contract'].schema == contract['schema']
            # A third ordinary prompt adds a real budget; excluding C from the
            # score reducer must never exclude it from call/cost accounting.
            ab = []
            for stage in ('A', 'B'):
                other = deepcopy(node)
                other['node_instance_id'] = 'prompt_' + stage.lower()
                other['config']['asset_key'] = other['node_instance_id']
                other['config']['execution_role'] = 'grading'
                other['config']['component_snapshot']['content']['stage'] = stage
                ab.append(other)
            old_budget = real_execution_budget_for_selection({'nodes': ab, 'edges': []}, 2,
                model_configs={model.id: model})
            new_budget = real_execution_budget_for_selection({'nodes': ab + [node], 'edges': []}, 2,
                model_configs={model.id: model})
            assert old_budget.expected_requests == 4 and new_budget.expected_requests == 6
            assert new_budget.max_cost_micros > old_budget.max_cost_micros
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_inspiration_original_retention.py =====
"""灵感图增量原图保留期：只删过了保留期、已评完且不再被需要的原图文件，记录与历史保留。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.database import Base
from app.incremental_material_models import IncrementalMaterialJob
from app.inspiration_original_retention import event_key, expire_originals
from app.models import (
    Asset, AuditEvent, BaselineSet, BaselineSetItem, ContentIngressEvent, ContentRecord,
    IncrementalAdmissionBacklog, ProductionRun, WorkflowDefinition, WorkflowVersion,
)

NOW = datetime(2026, 10, 10, 12, 0, 0)
OLD = NOW - timedelta(days=5)


@pytest.fixture
def db():
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://", connect_args={"check_same_thread": False}, poolclass=StaticPool)
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    session = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    definition = WorkflowDefinition(workflow_key="wf", name="wf", owner="t", created_by="t")
    session.add(definition)
    session.flush()
    session.add(WorkflowVersion(id=1, workflow_definition_id=definition.id, version="v1", status="active",
                                step_manifest_json="[]", edge_manifest_json="[]", input_schema_json="{}",
                                output_schema_json="{}", canonical_hash="0" * 64, created_by="t"))
    session.commit()
    yield session
    session.close()
    engine.dispose()


def _asset(db, tmp_path, index, *, created=OLD, category="inspiration_image", prefix="material-", data=None):
    data = data if data is not None else f"image-{index}".encode()
    name = f"{prefix}{index:04d}.jpg"
    (tmp_path / name).write_bytes(data)
    asset = Asset(original_name="incremental.jpg", stored_name=name, mime_type="image/jpeg", size_bytes=len(data),
                  width=8, height=8, sha256=hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(), category_key=category,
                  created_at=created)
    db.add(asset)
    db.flush()
    return asset


def _evaluated(db, asset, index, *, status="succeeded", updated=OLD, source_run_id=None):
    record = ContentRecord(source_system="lgt", source_content_id=f"c{index}", category_key=asset.category_key,
                           source_version="v1", source_occurred_at=OLD, content_key=f"lgt:c{index}",
                           status="ready", identity_status="verified", asset_id=asset.id)
    db.add(record)
    db.flush()
    event = ContentIngressEvent(event_id=f"evt-{index}", schema_version="content-ingress-v1",
                                event_type="content.created", source_system="lgt", occurred_at=OLD,
                                payload_hash=f"{index:064d}", payload_json=json.dumps({"asset_id": asset.id}),
                                content_record_id=record.id, status="applied")
    db.add(event)
    db.flush()
    run = _run(db, index, source_id=event.id, status=status, updated=updated)
    db.commit()
    return event, run


def _run(db, key, *, source_id=None, status="succeeded", updated=OLD, source_run_id=None):
    run = ProductionRun(run_key=f"run-{key}", idempotency_key=f"idem-{key}",
                        source_type="content_ingress_event" if source_id else "reassessment",
                        source_id=source_id, source_run_id=source_run_id, workflow_definition_id=1,
                        workflow_version_id=1, snapshot_hash="0" * 64, snapshot_json="{}",
                        category_key="inspiration_image", queue_class="production_batch", status=status,
                        blockers_json="[]", requested_by="t", owner="t", reason="", environment="dry_run",
                        total_steps=1, completed_steps=1, failed_steps=0, created_at=updated, updated_at=updated)
    db.add(run)
    db.flush()
    return run


def _expire(db, tmp_path, **kwargs):
    return expire_originals(db, upload_dir=tmp_path, retention_days=3, now=NOW, apply=True, **kwargs)


def _expired_ids(db):
    return {int(e.subject_id) for e in db.scalars(select(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.action == "asset_original_expired"))}


def test_an_old_finished_inspiration_original_is_deleted_and_the_record_kept(db, tmp_path):
    asset = _asset(db, tmp_path, 1)
    _evaluated(db, asset, 1)

    receipt = _expire(db, tmp_path)

    assert receipt["expired"] == 1 and receipt["freed_bytes"] == asset.size_bytes
    assert not (tmp_path / asset.stored_name).exists()
    kept = db.get(Asset, asset.id)
    assert kept is not None and kept.status == "uploaded"
    event = db.scalar(select(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.event_key == event_key(asset.id)))
    assert json.loads(event.payload_json)["sha256"] == asset.sha256
    # 已过期的不会再被扫到，重跑是空转
    assert _expire(db, tmp_path)["scanned"] == 0


def test_dry_run_reports_without_touching_files_or_audit(db, tmp_path):
    asset = _asset(db, tmp_path, 1)
    _evaluated(db, asset, 1)

    receipt = expire_originals(db, upload_dir=tmp_path, retention_days=3, now=NOW, apply=False)

    assert receipt["eligible"] == 1 and receipt["expired"] == 0
    assert (tmp_path / asset.stored_name).exists()
    assert _expired_ids(db) == set()


def test_inside_the_retention_period_or_unfinished_runs_keep_the_original(db, tmp_path):
    young = _asset(db, tmp_path, 1, created=NOW - timedelta(days=1))
    _evaluated(db, young, 1, updated=NOW - timedelta(days=1))
    running = _asset(db, tmp_path, 2)
    _evaluated(db, running, 2, status="retryable")
    touched = _asset(db, tmp_path, 3)
    _evaluated(db, touched, 3, updated=NOW - timedelta(hours=5))   # 失败重试或纠偏重放刚动过
    never_ran = _asset(db, tmp_path, 4)
    db.commit()

    receipt = _expire(db, tmp_path)

    assert _expired_ids(db) == set()
    assert receipt["skipped"] == {"run_not_finished": 1, "run_recent": 1, "no_production_run": 1}
    for asset in (young, running, touched, never_ran):
        assert (tmp_path / asset.stored_name).exists()


def test_a_recent_child_run_such_as_reassessment_keeps_the_original(db, tmp_path):
    asset = _asset(db, tmp_path, 1)
    _, parent = _evaluated(db, asset, 1)
    _run(db, "child", source_run_id=parent.id, status="running", updated=NOW - timedelta(hours=1))
    db.commit()

    receipt = _expire(db, tmp_path)

    assert receipt["skipped"] == {"run_not_finished": 1}
    assert (tmp_path / asset.stored_name).exists()


def test_regression_samples_and_pending_admission_keep_the_original(db, tmp_path):
    sample = _asset(db, tmp_path, 1)
    _evaluated(db, sample, 1)
    baseline = BaselineSet(name="golden", category_key="inspiration_image", default_expected_level="L3", fingerprint="f" * 64, created_by="t")
    db.add(baseline)
    db.flush()
    db.add(BaselineSetItem(baseline_set_id=baseline.id, asset_id=sample.id, expected_level="L3", asset_snapshot_json="{}"))
    deferred = _asset(db, tmp_path, 2)
    event, _ = _evaluated(db, deferred, 2)
    db.add(IncrementalAdmissionBacklog(event_id=event.id, category_key="inspiration_image",
                                       workflow_definition_id=1, status="pending"))
    db.commit()

    receipt = _expire(db, tmp_path)

    assert _expired_ids(db) == set()
    assert receipt["skipped"] == {"baseline_set": 1, "run_not_finished": 1}


def test_a_material_job_still_binding_the_asset_keeps_the_original(db, tmp_path):
    asset = _asset(db, tmp_path, 1)
    _evaluated(db, asset, 1)
    db.add(IncrementalMaterialJob(source_system="lgt", submission_id="s1", category_key="inspiration_image",
                                  source_content_id="c9", source_version="v1", identity_hash="1" * 64,
                                  manifest_hash="2" * 64, manifest_json="{}", status="retry_wait",
                                  resolved_json=json.dumps({"image": {"asset_id": asset.id}}),
                                  occurred_at=NOW, created_by="t"))
    db.commit()

    assert _expire(db, tmp_path)["skipped"] == {"active_material_job": 1}
    assert (tmp_path / asset.stored_name).exists()


def test_only_incremental_inspiration_originals_are_in_scope(db, tmp_path):
    three_d = _asset(db, tmp_path, 1, category="model_3d_su")
    _evaluated(db, three_d, 1)
    uploaded = _asset(db, tmp_path, 2, prefix="upload-")
    _evaluated(db, uploaded, 2)

    assert _expire(db, tmp_path)["scanned"] == 0
    assert (tmp_path / three_d.stored_name).exists() and (tmp_path / uploaded.stored_name).exists()


def test_bytes_that_do_not_match_the_record_are_never_deleted(db, tmp_path):
    asset = _asset(db, tmp_path, 1)
    _evaluated(db, asset, 1)
    (tmp_path / asset.stored_name).write_bytes(b"someone else's bytes")

    receipt = _expire(db, tmp_path)

    assert receipt["skipped"] == {"sha256_mismatch": 1} and receipt["expired"] == 0
    assert (tmp_path / asset.stored_name).exists()


def test_a_file_already_gone_is_recorded_once_without_error(db, tmp_path):
    asset = _asset(db, tmp_path, 1)
    _evaluated(db, asset, 1)
    (tmp_path / asset.stored_name).unlink()

    receipt = _expire(db, tmp_path)

    assert receipt["already_missing"] == 1 and receipt["errors"] == []
    assert _expired_ids(db) == {asset.id}


def test_the_limit_bounds_one_round_and_the_next_round_continues(db, tmp_path):
    assets = [_asset(db, tmp_path, i) for i in range(1, 4)]
    for i, asset in enumerate(assets, start=1):
        _evaluated(db, asset, i)

    assert _expire(db, tmp_path, limit=2)["expired"] == 2
    assert _expire(db, tmp_path, limit=2)["expired"] == 1
    assert _expired_ids(db) == {a.id for a in assets}


def test_retention_days_must_be_sane(db, tmp_path):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        expire_originals(db, upload_dir=tmp_path, retention_days=0, now=NOW)


def test_originals_in_the_store_subdirectory_are_handled_too(db, tmp_path):
    (tmp_path / "originals").mkdir()
    asset = _asset(db, tmp_path, 1, prefix="originals/material-")
    _evaluated(db, asset, 1)

    receipt = _expire(db, tmp_path)

    assert receipt["expired"] == 1
    assert not (tmp_path / asset.stored_name).exists()
    assert (tmp_path / "originals").is_dir()


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Astra 全仓复审 F2：删除前逐张加锁、锁内复核，新增的长期引用不能被旧判断删掉
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #


@pytest.fixture
def file_db(tmp_path):
    """文件库 + 独立 Session：清理器与"加入基准集"是两个真正各自提交的事务。"""
    from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'retention-race.db'}", connect_args={"timeout": 0.5})
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    with sessions() as session:
        definition = WorkflowDefinition(workflow_key="wf", name="wf", owner="t", created_by="t")
        session.add(definition)
        session.flush()
        session.add(WorkflowVersion(id=1, workflow_definition_id=definition.id, version="v1", status="active",
                                    step_manifest_json="[]", edge_manifest_json="[]", input_schema_json="{}",
                                    output_schema_json="{}", canonical_hash="0" * 64, created_by="t"))
        session.commit()
    yield sessions
    engine.dispose()


def _expirable_sample(sessions, tmp_path, index: int) -> tuple[int, int]:
    with sessions() as db:
        sample = _asset(db, tmp_path, index)
        _evaluated(db, sample, index)
        baseline = BaselineSet(name=f"golden-{index}", category_key="inspiration_image", default_expected_level="L3",
                               fingerprint=f"{index}".rjust(64, "f"), created_by="t")
        db.add(baseline)
        db.commit()
        return sample.id, baseline.id


def _pin(session, baseline_id: int, asset_id: int) -> None:
    session.add(BaselineSetItem(baseline_set_id=baseline_id, asset_id=asset_id, expected_level="L3",
                                asset_snapshot_json="{}"))


def test_a_reference_added_after_the_bulk_check_is_seen_under_the_lock(file_db, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """复现：批量预筛读完引用之后，另一会话把素材加入基准集并提交；旧实现仍按旧判断 unlink。"""
    import app.inspiration_original_retention as retention

    asset_id, baseline_id = _expirable_sample(file_db, tmp_path, 1)
    real_recent = retention._recent_model_use
    raced: list[bool] = []

    def racing_recent(db, ids, cutoff):
        found = real_recent(db, ids, cutoff)
        if not raced:  # 只在批量预筛那一次插进来
            raced.append(True)
            with file_db() as other:
                _pin(other, baseline_id, asset_id)
                other.commit()
        return found

    monkeypatch.setattr(retention, "_recent_model_use", racing_recent)
    with file_db() as db:
        receipt = _expire(db, tmp_path)
        assert _expired_ids(db) == set()
    assert raced == [True]
    assert receipt["expired"] == 0 and receipt["skipped"] == {"baseline_set": 1}
    assert (tmp_path / "material-0001.jpg").exists()


def test_a_reference_cannot_be_committed_while_the_original_is_being_deleted(file_db, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """持锁期间另一会话的引用写入只能等清理器提交（SQLite 写锁；MySQL 上是外键检查的共享锁）。"""
    from sqlalchemy.exc import OperationalError

    import app.inspiration_original_retention as retention

    asset_id, baseline_id = _expirable_sample(file_db, tmp_path, 2)
    real_hash = retention._file_sha256
    attempts: list[str] = []

    def hash_while_someone_pins(path):
        with file_db() as other:
            _pin(other, baseline_id, asset_id)
            try:
                other.commit()
                attempts.append("committed")
            except OperationalError:
                other.rollback()
                attempts.append("waited")
        return real_hash(path)

    monkeypatch.setattr(retention, "_file_sha256", hash_while_someone_pins)
    with file_db() as db:
        receipt = _expire(db, tmp_path)
        assert _expired_ids(db) == {asset_id}
    assert attempts == ["waited"]
    assert receipt["expired"] == 1


def test_an_asset_someone_else_holds_is_left_for_the_next_round(db, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    import app.inspiration_original_retention as retention

    asset = _asset(db, tmp_path, 3)
    _evaluated(db, asset, 3)
    monkeypatch.setattr(retention, "_lock_for_expiry", lambda _db, _asset_id: None)

    receipt = _expire(db, tmp_path)

    assert _expired_ids(db) == set()
    assert receipt["skipped"] == {"asset_locked": 1} and receipt["expired"] == 0
    assert (tmp_path / "material-0003.jpg").exists()


def test_a_database_error_after_the_lock_is_reported_with_its_stage_not_as_lock_contention(db, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """复审 N2：取锁之后（复核、删文件、写审计、提交）的数据库错误不能记成 asset_locked；
    回执如实给出失败阶段。文件已删但审计没写成时，下一轮按 already_missing 补记。"""
    from sqlalchemy.exc import OperationalError

    import app.audit as audit

    asset = _asset(db, tmp_path, 4)
    _evaluated(db, asset, 4)
    real_append = audit.append_audit_event

    def lost_connection(*args, **kwargs):
        raise OperationalError("INSERT INTO audit_events", {}, Exception("lost connection"))

    monkeypatch.setattr(audit, "append_audit_event", lost_connection)
    receipt = _expire(db, tmp_path)
    assert receipt["skipped"] == {} and receipt["expired"] == 0
    assert receipt["errors"] == [{"asset_id": asset.id, "error": "OperationalError", "stage": "audit"}]
    assert not (tmp_path / "material-0004.jpg").exists()  # 先删文件后记事件
    assert _expired_ids(db) == set()

    monkeypatch.setattr(audit, "append_audit_event", real_append)
    again = _expire(db, tmp_path)
    assert again["already_missing"] == 1 and again["errors"] == []
    assert _expired_ids(db) == {asset.id}

===== FILE backend/tests/test_inspiration_production_candidate.py =====
"""Candidate preparation freezes only A metadata; no binding/run side effects."""
import importlib.util
import json
from pathlib import Path
import socket
import sys

from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select, func
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker
from app import database
from app.database import Base
from app.models import ModelConfig, ModelRegistryEntry, WorkflowVersion, PromptVersion, ProductionRun, CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding
from app.workflow_registry import persisted_workflow_manifest
from app.workflow_working_copies import create_immutable_evaluation_mechanism_version
from app.strict_prompt_contracts import strict_output_contract, LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE
from app.vision_input_policy import QWEN_205_PROFILE
from incremental_mechanism_fixtures import seed_incremental_versions


def test_prepare_dry_run_and_apply_are_isolated_immutable_and_repeatable(tmp_path,monkeypatch,capsys):
    def forbidden(*a,**kw):raise AssertionError('candidate preparation must be offline')
    monkeypatch.setattr(socket.socket,'connect',forbidden)
    engine=create_engine('sqlite:///'+str(tmp_path/'candidate.sqlite'))
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions=sessionmaker(bind=engine,expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            base=seed_incremental_versions(db,category_key='inspiration_image',count=1)[0]
            for cls in (ModelConfig,ModelRegistryEntry):
                model=db.scalar(select(cls))
                model.provider='local_qwen'
                model.vision_capability_profile_json=json.dumps(dict(QWEN_205_PROFILE))
            manifest=persisted_workflow_manifest(base)
            a=next(n for n in manifest['nodes'] if n['kind']=='prompt' and n['config']['asset_key']=='prompt_a')
            a['config']['output_contract']=strict_output_contract('inspiration_image','A',
                evaluation_contract={},contract_profile=LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE)
            a['output_schema']=a['config']['output_contract']['schema']
            source=create_immutable_evaluation_mechanism_version(db,definition=base.definition,
                version='golden-source',manifest=manifest,created_by='test')
            source_id,source_hash=source.id,source.canonical_hash
            original=persisted_workflow_manifest(source)
            db.commit()
            counts=(db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(WorkflowVersion)),db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(PromptVersion)))
        script=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]/'scripts/prepare-inspiration-production-candidate.py'
        spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('inspiration_prepare',script)
        module=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec);spec.loader.exec_module(module)
        monkeypatch.setattr(database,'SessionLocal',sessions)
        monkeypatch.setenv('LABEL_LAB_BUILD_SHA','isolated-candidate-test')
        monkeypatch.delenv('LABEL_SYSTEM_INSPIRATION_FACTORY_ENABLED',raising=False)
        args=[str(script),'--source-version',str(source_id),'--expected-source-hash',source_hash,
              '--expected-build','isolated-candidate-test']
        monkeypatch.setattr(sys,'argv',args)
        module.main(); preview=json.loads(capsys.readouterr().out)
        assert preview['applied'] is False and preview['runs_started']==0
        with sessions() as db:
            assert counts==(db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(WorkflowVersion)),db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(PromptVersion)))
        monkeypatch.setattr(sys,'argv',[*args,'--apply'])
        module.main(); first=json.loads(capsys.readouterr().out)
        module.main(); second=json.loads(capsys.readouterr().out)
        assert first==second and first['operator_verification_required'] is True
        with sessions() as db:
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(WorkflowVersion))==counts[0]+1
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(PromptVersion))==counts[1]+1
            assert persisted_workflow_manifest(db.get(WorkflowVersion,source_id))==original
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(ProductionRun))==0
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding))==0
    finally:engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_inspiration_production_fields.py =====
from copy import deepcopy
import pytest
from app.inspiration_production_fields import extend_contract,published_fields,METADATA_FIELDS
from app.strict_prompt_contracts import strict_output_contract,LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE
from app.prompt_output_contract import parse_output_contract


def fields():
    return dict(title='现代客厅',seotitle='现代简约客厅空间设计',class_text='室内空间 / 客厅',
        style='现代简约',tags=['客厅','木饰面'],cons='未见明显不足',design='浅色墙面与木饰面形成对比。',
        reason=[],trait='实景照片',image_defects=[])


def test_new_contract_extends_only_metadata_without_mutating_old_scoring_fields():
    old=strict_output_contract('inspiration_image','A',evaluation_contract={},contract_profile=LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE)
    before=deepcopy(old);new=extend_contract(old)
    assert old==before
    assert set(new['schema']['properties'])-set(old['schema']['properties'])==set(METADATA_FIELDS)
    for key,value in old['schema']['properties'].items():assert new['schema']['properties'][key]==value
    restored=deepcopy(new)
    for field in METADATA_FIELDS:
        del restored['schema']['properties'][field]
        restored['schema']['required'].remove(field)
    assert restored==old
    parse_output_contract(new)


def test_protocol_fields_keep_unicode_tags_and_render_only_evidence_arrays():
    original=fields();new=published_fields(original)
    assert new['seo_title']==original['seotitle']
    assert new['reason']==new['image_defects']==''
    assert new['tags']==['客厅','木饰面']
    assert 'score' not in new and 'grade' not in new
    assert original['reason']==[]


@pytest.mark.parametrize('field',METADATA_FIELDS)
def test_missing_publish_field_is_not_silently_filled(field):
    raw=fields();raw.pop(field)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='fields_missing'):published_fields(raw)


@pytest.mark.parametrize('key,value',[('style',''),('tags','客厅'),('reason',[None]),('design',None)])
def test_bad_types_and_blank_descriptions_fail_closed(key,value):
    raw=fields();raw[key]=value
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):published_fields(raw)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_inspiration_quality_metrics.py =====
from app.inspiration_quality_metrics import (
    compute_inspiration_quality_metrics,
    quality_gate,
    three_bucket_fallback_metrics,
)


def test_recommendation_merges_l1_l2_and_three_bucket_fallback():
    metrics = compute_inspiration_quality_metrics([
        {"truth": "L1", "pred": "L2"},
        {"truth": "L2", "pred": "L1"},
        {"truth": "L3", "pred": "L4"},
        {"truth": "L5", "pred": "L4"},
    ])
    assert metrics["recommendation"]["denominator"] == 2
    assert metrics["three_bucket"]["filter"]["recall"] == 0.0
    assert metrics["diagnostics"]["l1_overpromotion_cost"] > 0


def test_quality_gate_rejects_recommendation_share_over_thirty_five_percent():
    metrics = compute_inspiration_quality_metrics(
        [{"truth": "L1", "pred": "L1"}] * 36
        + [{"truth": "L3", "pred": "L3"}] * 64
    )
    failures = quality_gate(metrics)
    assert any(item["gate"] == "recommendation_share" for item in failures)


def test_fallback_metrics_use_three_business_buckets():
    result = three_bucket_fallback_metrics([
        {"truth": "L2", "pred": "L1"},
        {"truth": "L4", "pred": "L3"},
        {"truth": "L5", "pred": "L5"},
    ])
    assert set(result) == {"recommendation", "ordinary", "filter"}
    assert result["filter"]["recall"] == 1.0

===== FILE backend/tests/test_inspiration_quality_repair.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import copy
from pathlib import Path

from app.inspiration_aesthetic_foundation import (
    AESTHETIC_CALL_B_VERSION,
    DIMENSION_KEYS,
    apply_aesthetic_v3_rules,
    build_prompt,
)
from app.inspiration_category_seed import (
    INSPIRATION_SEED_VERSION,
    build_inspiration_classification_map,
    build_inspiration_v3_contract,
)


def _foundation(score: int = 88) -> dict:
    return {
        "contract_version": "inspiration-aesthetic-foundation-v1",
        "aesthetic_score": score,
        "dimensions": {
            key: {
                "grade": 3,
                "evidence": [f"{key} 可见证据"],
                "shortcomings": [f"{key} 可见不足"],
            }
            for key in DIMENSION_KEYS
        },
        "overall_evidence": ["整体可见证据"],
        "confidence": 0.82,
    }


def _precheck(*, reason: str = "无红线", track: str = "建筑设计") -> dict:
    return {
        "classification": {
            "scope_status": "in_scope",
            "primary_category": track,
            "primary_confidence": 0.95,
        },
        "production_fields": {"reason": [reason], "trait": "实景照片"},
        "hard_defects": [],
        "image_defects": [],
        "decisive_signal_validation": {"status": "valid"},
    }


def _apply(precheck: dict, foundation: dict | None) -> dict:
    return apply_aesthetic_v3_rules(
        contract=build_inspiration_v3_contract(),
        classification_map=build_inspiration_classification_map(),
        precheck=precheck,
        foundation=foundation,
    )


def _candidate_contract_with_semantic_soft_cap() -> dict:
    contract = build_inspiration_v3_contract()
    foundation = contract["aesthetic_foundation"]
    foundation["score_thresholds"] = [
        {"min_score": 90, "level": "L1"},
        {"min_score": 80, "level": "L2"},
        {"min_score": 76, "level": "L3"},
        {"min_score": 60, "level": "L4"},
        {"min_score": 0, "level": "L5"},
    ]
    foundation["casual_snapshot_soft_cap"] = {
        "key": "casual_snapshot_soft_cap",
        "signal": "production_fields.reason",
        "match_any": ["是随手拍"],
        "cap_to_level": "L4",
        "filter_escalation": {
            "cap_to_level": "L5",
            "dimensions_at_most": {
                "presentation_integrity": 2,
                "inspiration_reference": 2,
            },
        },
    }
    return contract


def _apply_candidate(precheck: dict, foundation: dict | None) -> dict:
    return apply_aesthetic_v3_rules(
        contract=_candidate_contract_with_semantic_soft_cap(),
        classification_map=build_inspiration_classification_map(),
        precheck=precheck,
        foundation=foundation,
    )


def test_l3_recall_prompt_restores_detailed_anchor_calibration() -> None:
    prompt = build_prompt()
    assert AESTHETIC_CALL_B_VERSION == (
        "inspiration-b-v5-anchor-calibration-evidence-20260807"
    )
    assert INSPIRATION_SEED_VERSION == "inspiration-category-seed-v7-quality-gates"
    assert "2045/L1：螺旋楼梯" in prompt
    assert "747/L2：紫绿床品" in prompt
    assert "1263/L3：荷花与祝福文字" in prompt
    assert "601/L4：居中灯笼记录照" in prompt
    assert "清晰且无明显硬伤不等于高分" in prompt
    assert "75分边界" in prompt
    assert '"contract_version": "inspiration-aesthetic-foundation-v1"' in prompt
    assert '"evidence": ["必须填写至少一条待评图可见证据"]' in prompt


def test_asset_390_casual_blurry_grayish_is_l5_redline() -> None:
    item = _precheck(reason="是随手拍")
    item["hard_defects"] = ["blurry_grayish"]
    result = _apply(item, None)
    assert result["hard_reject"] is True
    assert result["level"] == "L5"
    assert result["hit_rules"] == ["casual_snapshot"]


def test_non_redline_casual_snapshot_is_soft_capped_to_l4() -> None:
    result = _apply(_precheck(reason="是随手拍"), _foundation(61))
    assert result["hard_reject"] is False
    assert result["inspiration_aesthetic_score"] == 61
    assert result["score"] == 59
    assert result["level"] == "L4"
    assert {"rule": "casual_snapshot_soft_cap", "cap_to": 59} in result["caps"]


def test_semantic_soft_cap_keeps_casual_snapshot_in_l4_band_after_threshold_change() -> None:
    result = _apply_candidate(_precheck(reason="是随手拍"), _foundation(88))

    assert result["hard_reject"] is False
    assert result["score"] == 75
    assert result["level"] == "L4"
    assert {
        "rule": "casual_snapshot_soft_cap",
        "cap_to_level": "L4",
        "resolved_cap_to": 75,
    } in result["caps"]


def test_semantic_soft_cap_escalates_to_l5_only_for_two_low_quality_dimensions() -> None:
    foundation = _foundation(88)
    foundation["dimensions"]["presentation_integrity"]["grade"] = 2
    foundation["dimensions"]["inspiration_reference"]["grade"] = 2

    result = _apply_candidate(_precheck(reason="是随手拍"), foundation)

    assert result["score"] == 59
    assert result["level"] == "L5"
    assert {
        "rule": "casual_snapshot_filter_escalation",
        "cap_to_level": "L5",
        "resolved_cap_to": 59,
    } in result["caps"]


def test_semantic_soft_cap_does_not_escalate_with_only_one_low_quality_dimension() -> None:
    foundation = _foundation(88)
    foundation["dimensions"]["presentation_integrity"]["grade"] = 2

    result = _apply_candidate(_precheck(reason="是随手拍"), foundation)

    assert result["score"] == 75
    assert result["level"] == "L4"
    assert not any(
        cap.get("rule") == "casual_snapshot_filter_escalation"
        for cap in result["caps"]
        if isinstance(cap, dict)
    )

    assert "前五张为 Owner 锚图（L1 至 L5），第六张才是待评图" in prompt

def _watermark_precheck(evidence: str) -> dict:
    item = _precheck(track="产品设计")
    item["image_defects"] = ["subject_obscuring_watermark"]
    item["decisive_evidence"] = {
        "image_defects": {"subject_obscuring_watermark": evidence}
    }
    return item


def _clean_brand_wordmark_foundation() -> dict:
    payload = _foundation(88)
    for key in ("detail_completion", "presentation_integrity"):
        payload["dimensions"][key] = {
            "grade": 4,
            "evidence": [f"{key} 主体完整清晰"],
            "shortcomings": [],
        }
    return payload


def test_asset_747_brand_wordmark_is_narrowly_exempt_from_tier_a_cap() -> None:
    item = _watermark_precheck(
        "画面中下偏下位置的白色品牌文字TEKLA位于床品面料上"
    )
    before = copy.deepcopy(item)
    result = _apply(item, _clean_brand_wordmark_foundation())
    assert result["hard_reject"] is False
    assert result["inspiration_aesthetic_score"] == 88
    assert result["score"] == 80
    assert result["level"] == "L2"
    assert {
        "rule": "hard_defect_exemption",
        "key": "subject_obscuring_brand_wordmark",
        "defect_key": "subject_obscuring_watermark",
    } in result["caps"]
    assert item == before


def test_known_real_photo_modifier_cancels_brand_wordmark_exemption() -> None:
    item = _watermark_precheck(
        "画面中下偏下位置的白色品牌文字TEKLA位于床品面料上"
    )
    item["hard_defects"] = ["known_real_photo_defect"]

    result = _apply(item, _clean_brand_wordmark_foundation())

    assert result["score"] == 20
    assert result["level"] == "L4"
    assert not any(
        cap.get("rule") == "hard_defect_exemption"
        for cap in result["caps"]
        if isinstance(cap, dict)
    )
    assert any(
        cap.get("rule") == "hard_defect_exemption_cancelled"
        for cap in result["caps"]
        if isinstance(cap, dict)
    )
    policy = next(
        cap
        for cap in result["caps"]
        if isinstance(cap, dict) and cap.get("rule") == "hard_defect_severity"
    )
    assert policy["modifier_applied"] is True
    assert policy["cancel_exemption"] is True


def test_known_real_photo_modifier_toggle_off_keeps_brand_wordmark_exemption() -> None:
    contract = build_inspiration_v3_contract()
    modifier = next(
        rule
        for rule in contract["common_modifiers"]["high_score_veto"]["rules"]
        if rule.get("kind") == "modifier"
    )
    modifier["cancel_exemption"] = False
    item = _watermark_precheck(
        "画面中下偏下位置的白色品牌文字TEKLA位于床品面料上"
    )
    item["hard_defects"] = ["known_real_photo_defect"]

    result = apply_aesthetic_v3_rules(
        contract=contract,
        classification_map=build_inspiration_classification_map(),
        precheck=item,
        foundation=_clean_brand_wordmark_foundation(),
    )

    assert result["score"] == 80
    assert any(
        cap.get("rule") == "hard_defect_exemption"
        for cap in result["caps"]
        if isinstance(cap, dict)
    )
    assert not any(
        cap.get("rule") == "hard_defect_exemption_cancelled"
        for cap in result["caps"]
        if isinstance(cap, dict)
    )


def test_true_subject_obscuring_watermark_still_keeps_tier_a_cap() -> None:
    payload = _clean_brand_wordmark_foundation()
    payload["dimensions"]["detail_completion"]["shortcomings"] = [
        "半透明商业水印破坏视觉连贯性"
    ]
    result = _apply(
        _watermark_precheck("半透明版权水印覆盖主体内容"),
        payload,
    )
    assert result["hard_reject"] is False
    assert result["score"] == 20
    assert result["level"] == "L4"
    assert any(cap.get("rule") == "hard_defect_severity" for cap in result["caps"])
    assert not any(cap.get("rule") == "hard_defect_exemption" for cap in result["caps"])

===== FILE backend/tests/test_inspiration_quality_rules.py =====
"""灵感图质量规则机制（可配置版）测试。

三类断言：

1. **单一职责守卫**——外来机制键混进本块必须报错，且报错要说明该键归哪。
   这是把"只管两项"钉成机器可查的红线，不靠注释约束。
2. **解锁能力**——旧 ``_validated_quality_rules`` 把内容写死了（关键词必须精确等于
   ``["是随手拍"]``、豁免必须恰好 1 条、key/关键词/维度全部锁定）。新机制必须放行
   运营的这些改动，否则拆分没换来任何能力。
3. **语义等价**——默认块翻译出的内部形状必须与旧路径对生产合同的输出逐字节一致，
   这是执行层零改动的前提。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import copy
import json

import pytest

from app.inspiration_quality_rules import (
    CONTRACT_BLOCK_KEY,
    QualityRulesError,
    assert_quality_rules_isolated,
    default_quality_rules_block,
    load_quality_rules,
    validate_quality_rules_block,
)


def _block() -> dict:
    return default_quality_rules_block()


def _contract(block: dict | None = None) -> dict:
    return {CONTRACT_BLOCK_KEY: block if block is not None else _block()}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. 单一职责守卫
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

FOREIGN_KEYS_WITH_HOME = [
    ("score_thresholds", "level_thresholds"),
    ("level_thresholds", "合同顶层"),
    ("level_scale", "level_scale"),
    ("bands", "level_thresholds"),
    ("anchors", "anchor_mechanism"),
    ("anchor_samples", "anchor_mechanism"),
    ("anchor_mechanism", "独立块"),
    ("dimensions", "dimensions"),
    ("dimension_keys", "dimensions"),
    ("dimension_weights", "dimensions"),
    ("redline_policy", "redline_policy"),
    ("redlines", "redline_policy"),
    ("boundary_policy", "零实现"),
    ("prompt_template", "提示词管理"),
    ("call_b_version", "合同顶层"),
    ("calibration_status", "合同顶层"),
    ("aesthetic_foundation", "被替代"),
]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("key,home_hint", FOREIGN_KEYS_WITH_HOME)
def test_foreign_key_rejected_at_block_level(key: str, home_hint: str) -> None:
    """外来机制键混进块顶层必须被拦，并说明它该去哪。"""
    block = _block()
    block[key] = {"whatever": True}
    with pytest.raises(QualityRulesError) as excinfo:
        assert_quality_rules_isolated(block)
    assert excinfo.value.code == "quality_rules_foreign_key"
    message = str(excinfo.value)
    assert key in message
    assert home_hint in message


@pytest.mark.parametrize("key,_home", FOREIGN_KEYS_WITH_HOME)
def test_foreign_key_rejected_inside_snapshot_limit(key: str, _home: str) -> None:
    """外来键藏进随手拍限分子块同样要被拦。"""
    block = _block()
    block["snapshot_limit"][key] = {"whatever": True}
    with pytest.raises(QualityRulesError) as excinfo:
        assert_quality_rules_isolated(block)
    assert excinfo.value.code == "quality_rules_foreign_key"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("key,_home", FOREIGN_KEYS_WITH_HOME)
def test_foreign_key_rejected_inside_defect_exception(key: str, _home: str) -> None:
    """外来键藏进硬伤例外条目同样要被拦。"""
    block = _block()
    block["defect_exceptions"][0][key] = {"whatever": True}
    with pytest.raises(QualityRulesError) as excinfo:
        assert_quality_rules_isolated(block)
    assert excinfo.value.code == "quality_rules_foreign_key"


def test_unknown_field_rejected() -> None:
    """改个名字夹带也不行——白名单兜住。"""
    block = _block()
    block["mystery_knob"] = 1
    with pytest.raises(QualityRulesError) as excinfo:
        assert_quality_rules_isolated(block)
    assert excinfo.value.code == "quality_rules_unknown_field"
    assert "mystery_knob" in str(excinfo.value)


def test_unknown_field_rejected_in_snapshot_limit() -> None:
    block = _block()
    block["snapshot_limit"]["secret_cap"] = 3
    with pytest.raises(QualityRulesError) as excinfo:
        assert_quality_rules_isolated(block)
    assert excinfo.value.code == "snapshot_limit_unknown_field"


def test_unknown_field_rejected_in_defect_exception() -> None:
    block = _block()
    block["defect_exceptions"][0]["secret_rule"] = True
    with pytest.raises(QualityRulesError) as excinfo:
        assert_quality_rules_isolated(block)
    assert excinfo.value.code == "defect_exception_unknown_field"


def test_block_must_be_object() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(QualityRulesError) as excinfo:
        assert_quality_rules_isolated(["not", "a", "dict"])
    assert excinfo.value.code == "quality_rules_not_object"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. 解锁能力：旧校验拒绝、新机制必须放行
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_unlock_custom_snapshot_keywords() -> None:
    """旧校验要求关键词精确等于 ["是随手拍"]；运营改写法必须放行。"""
    block = _block()
    block["snapshot_limit"]["when_reason_contains"] = ["随手拍", "手机快照", "生活照"]
    soft_cap, _ = load_quality_rules(_contract(block))
    assert soft_cap is not None
    assert soft_cap["match_any"] == ["随手拍", "手机快照", "生活照"]


def test_unlock_custom_cap_score() -> None:
    """运营调整限分值必须放行。"""
    block = _block()
    block["snapshot_limit"]["max_score"] = 45
    soft_cap, _ = load_quality_rules(_contract(block))
    assert soft_cap["cap_to"] == 45


def test_unlock_multiple_defect_exceptions() -> None:
    """旧校验要求恰好 1 条豁免；运营加第二条必须放行。"""
    block = _block()
    second = copy.deepcopy(block["defect_exceptions"][0])
    second["name"] = "logo_edge_crop"
    second["defect"] = "subject_cropped"
    second["when_evidence_contains"] = ["边缘裁切"]
    block["defect_exceptions"].append(second)
    _, exemptions = load_quality_rules(_contract(block))
    assert len(exemptions) == 2
    assert {item["key"] for item in exemptions} == {
        "subject_obscuring_brand_wordmark",
        "logo_edge_crop",
    }


def test_unlock_custom_exception_name_and_evidence() -> None:
    """旧校验把 key 与关键词写死；运营自定义必须放行。"""
    block = _block()
    exception = block["defect_exceptions"][0]
    exception["name"] = "运营自定义豁免"
    exception["when_evidence_contains"] = ["店铺招牌", "门头字"]
    _, exemptions = load_quality_rules(_contract(block))
    assert exemptions[0]["key"] == "运营自定义豁免"
    assert exemptions[0]["evidence_contains_any"] == ["店铺招牌", "门头字"]


def test_unlock_custom_requirement_dimensions() -> None:
    """旧校验锁定两个维度；运营换维度、换档位必须放行。"""
    block = _block()
    block["defect_exceptions"][0]["require_dimensions"] = [
        {"dimension": "composition", "min_grade": 3},
        {"dimension": "lighting", "min_grade": 5, "no_shortcomings": True},
    ]
    _, exemptions = load_quality_rules(_contract(block))
    requirements = exemptions[0]["foundation_requirements"]
    assert requirements == {
        "composition": {"min_grade": 3, "shortcomings_empty": False},
        "lighting": {"min_grade": 5, "shortcomings_empty": True},
    }


def test_unlock_cap_to_level_with_dimension_ceilings() -> None:
    """按等级封顶 + 维度分上限这条路径也要能配。"""
    block = _block()
    block["snapshot_limit"].pop("max_score")
    block["snapshot_limit"]["max_level"] = "L4"
    block["snapshot_limit"]["dimension_ceilings"] = {"composition": 3, "lighting": 2}
    soft_cap, _ = load_quality_rules(_contract(block))
    assert soft_cap["cap_to_level"] == "L4"
    assert soft_cap["filter_escalation"] == {
        "cap_to_level": "L4",
        "dimensions_at_most": {"composition": 3, "lighting": 2},
    }


def test_snapshot_limit_can_be_disabled() -> None:
    """运营可以单独关掉随手拍限分而保留豁免。"""
    block = _block()
    block["snapshot_limit"]["enabled"] = False
    soft_cap, exemptions = load_quality_rules(_contract(block))
    assert soft_cap is None
    assert len(exemptions) == 1


def test_whole_block_can_be_disabled() -> None:
    block = _block()
    block["enabled"] = False
    soft_cap, exemptions = load_quality_rules(_contract(block))
    assert soft_cap is None
    assert exemptions == []


def test_empty_exceptions_allowed() -> None:
    """允许一条豁免都不配。"""
    block = _block()
    block["defect_exceptions"] = []
    soft_cap, exemptions = load_quality_rules(_contract(block))
    assert soft_cap is not None
    assert exemptions == []


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. 语义等价：默认块必须翻译出与生产旧路径一致的内部形状
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_default_block_matches_production_shape() -> None:
    """默认块的翻译结果必须与旧路径对生产合同的输出一致。

    这些期望值抄自生产库 inspiration_image 合同经 ``_validated_quality_rules``
    的实际输出，是执行层零改动的前提。
    """
    soft_cap, exemptions = load_quality_rules(_contract())
    assert soft_cap == {
        "key": "casual_snapshot_soft_cap",
        "signal": "production_fields.reason",
        "match_any": ["是随手拍"],
        "cap_to": 59,
    }
    assert exemptions == [
        {
            "key": "subject_obscuring_brand_wordmark",
            "source": "image_defects",
            "defect_key": "subject_obscuring_watermark",
            "evidence_contains_any": ["品牌文字", "品牌字样"],
            "foundation_requirements": {
                "detail_completion": {"min_grade": 4, "shortcomings_empty": True},
                "presentation_integrity": {"min_grade": 4, "shortcomings_empty": True},
            },
        }
    ]


def test_default_block_equals_legacy_output_on_production_contract() -> None:
    """与旧实现在生产配置上逐字节等价（防止翻译层漂移）。"""
    from backend.app.inspiration_aesthetic_foundation import _validated_quality_rules

    legacy_contract = {
        "aesthetic_foundation": {
            "casual_snapshot_soft_cap": {
                "key": "casual_snapshot_soft_cap",
                "signal": "production_fields.reason",
                "match_any": ["是随手拍"],
                "cap_to": 59,
            },
            "hard_defect_exemptions": [
                {
                    "key": "subject_obscuring_brand_wordmark",
                    "source": "image_defects",
                    "defect_key": "subject_obscuring_watermark",
                    "evidence_contains_any": ["品牌文字", "品牌字样"],
                    "foundation_requirements": {
                        "detail_completion": {
                            "min_grade": 4,
                            "shortcomings_empty": True,
                        },
                        "presentation_integrity": {
                            "min_grade": 4,
                            "shortcomings_empty": True,
                        },
                    },
                }
            ],
        }
    }
    legacy_soft, legacy_exem = _validated_quality_rules(legacy_contract)
    new_soft, new_exem = load_quality_rules(_contract())
    assert json.dumps(new_soft, sort_keys=True) == json.dumps(legacy_soft, sort_keys=True)
    assert json.dumps(new_exem, sort_keys=True) == json.dumps(legacy_exem, sort_keys=True)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. 执行层接线：配了必须真生效，关了必须不崩
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _run_rules(quality_rules_block: dict | None, *, snapshot_reason: bool):
    """跑一遍真实评分链路，返回结果。

    ``quality_rules_block`` 为 ``None`` 时不写入本块，用于验证回落旧基座路径。
    """
    from app.inspiration_aesthetic_foundation import apply_aesthetic_v3_rules
    from test_inspiration_aesthetic_foundation import (
        build_inspiration_classification_map,
        build_inspiration_v3_contract,
        precheck,
        valid_payload,
    )

    contract = build_inspiration_v3_contract()
    if quality_rules_block is not None:
        contract[CONTRACT_BLOCK_KEY] = quality_rules_block

    prepared_precheck = precheck()
    if snapshot_reason:
        prepared_precheck["production_fields"]["reason"] = ["是随手拍"]

    return apply_aesthetic_v3_rules(
        contract=contract,
        classification_map=build_inspiration_classification_map(),
        precheck=prepared_precheck,
        foundation=copy.deepcopy(valid_payload()),
    )


def _unclamped_score() -> int:
    """无限分作用时的原始分，作为"没被压分"的对照值。"""
    return _run_rules(None, snapshot_reason=False)["score"]


def test_legacy_path_still_caps_at_59() -> None:
    """合同不带本块时回落旧基座，生产现状零变化。"""
    assert _run_rules(None, snapshot_reason=True)["score"] == 59


def test_configured_cap_takes_effect_end_to_end() -> None:
    """运营配的限分值必须真的压到那个分数——特征值 37 排除巧合。"""
    block = _block()
    block["snapshot_limit"]["max_score"] = 37
    assert _run_rules(block, snapshot_reason=True)["score"] == 37


def test_configured_keywords_take_effect_end_to_end() -> None:
    """运营自定义关键词命中后按新限分压分。"""
    block = _block()
    block["snapshot_limit"]["when_reason_contains"] = ["手机快照", "是随手拍"]
    block["snapshot_limit"]["max_score"] = 42
    assert _run_rules(block, snapshot_reason=True)["score"] == 42


def test_non_matching_keywords_do_not_cap() -> None:
    """关键词对不上就不该压分。"""
    block = _block()
    block["snapshot_limit"]["when_reason_contains"] = ["完全不匹配的词"]
    assert _run_rules(block, snapshot_reason=True)["score"] == _unclamped_score()


def test_whole_block_disabled_does_not_crash_or_cap() -> None:
    """整块关闭时装载器返回 soft_cap=None，执行层必须容得下（曾在此崩过）。"""
    block = _block()
    block["enabled"] = False
    assert _run_rules(block, snapshot_reason=True)["score"] == _unclamped_score()


def test_snapshot_limit_disabled_does_not_crash_or_cap() -> None:
    """只关限分、保留豁免时同样不能崩。"""
    block = _block()
    block["snapshot_limit"]["enabled"] = False
    assert _run_rules(block, snapshot_reason=True)["score"] == _unclamped_score()


def test_non_snapshot_image_unaffected_by_cap() -> None:
    """不是随手拍就不受限分影响。"""
    block = _block()
    block["snapshot_limit"]["max_score"] = 37
    assert _run_rules(block, snapshot_reason=False)["score"] == _unclamped_score()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_inspiration_rule_priority.py =====
from copy import deepcopy
from itertools import product
import pytest
from app.inspiration_rule_priority import prioritize_inspiration_quality_guard
from app.strict_prompt_release import _inspiration_terminal_rules
from app.workflow_short_circuit import resolve_short_circuit, ShortCircuitConflict


def test_hard_defect_l5_does_not_tie_class_three_l4_or_override_redline():
    rules = _inspiration_terminal_rules()
    defect = next(r for r in rules if r['rule_key'] == 'inspiration-hard-defect-l4-v2')
    defect.update(rule_key='inspiration-hard-defect-l5-v3', target_level='L5')
    before = {'category_key':'inspiration_image','nodes':[{'node_instance_id':'prompt_a','config':{'short_circuit_rules':rules}}]}
    saved = deepcopy(before)
    conflict = {'output':{'decision_status':'complete','track_classification':'class_three','hard_defects':['blurry_grayish'],'redline_triggered':{}}}
    with pytest.raises(ShortCircuitConflict):
        resolve_short_circuit(rules, conflict, ('L1','L2','L3','L4','L5'))
    after = prioritize_inspiration_quality_guard(before)
    assert before == saved
    fixed = after['nodes'][0]['config']['short_circuit_rules']
    expected = deepcopy(rules)
    next(r for r in expected if r['rule_key']=='inspiration-hard-defect-l5-v3')['priority']=950
    assert fixed == expected  # every condition, target and skip role is unchanged
    for track, hard, redline in product(('class_one','class_two','class_three'), (False,True), (False,True)):
        facts = {'output':{'decision_status':'complete','track_classification':track,
            'hard_defects':['blurry_grayish'] if hard else [],'redline_triggered':{'screenshot':redline}}}
        decision = resolve_short_circuit(fixed, facts, ('L1','L2','L3','L4','L5'))
        target = 'L5' if redline or hard else {'class_one':None,'class_two':'L3','class_three':'L4'}[track]
        assert (decision.target_level if decision else None) == target
        if redline: assert decision.rule_key == 'inspiration-redline-l5-v2'

===== FILE backend/tests/test_inspiration_seed_persistence.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace

from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.category_evaluation_contract import validate_category_evaluation_contract
from app.database import Base
from app.dimension_composition import validate_subcategory_dimensions
from app.inspiration_category_seed import (
    INSPIRATION_CALL_A_VERSION,
    INSPIRATION_CALL_B_VERSION,
    INSPIRATION_REV3_CALL_A_VERSION,
    INSPIRATION_SPEC_VERSION,
)
from app.proposal_text_contract import validate_proposal_text_contract
from app.models import (
    CategoryEvaluationV3Config,
    CategoryEvaluationV3Revision,
    EvaluationCategoryProfile,
    PromptVersion,
)
from app.seed import (
    _seed_inspiration_image_prompts,
    _seed_inspiration_image_v3_config,
    seed_defaults,
)
from app.subcategory_resolver import validate_classification_map


PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]


def _engine():
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    return engine


def test_new_b_prompt_is_appended_without_overwriting_old_version() -> None:
    engine = _engine()
    with Session(engine) as db:
        db.add(
            PromptVersion(
                stage="B",
                category_key="inspiration_image",
                pipeline_scope="full_pipeline",
                name="旧灵感图 B",
                version="inspiration-b-v1",
                system_prompt="旧提示词不可覆盖",
                user_prompt="",
                rubric_version="inspiration-rubric-v1",
                status="published",
            )
        )
        db.commit()
        settings = SimpleNamespace(project_root=PROJECT_ROOT)
        _seed_inspiration_image_prompts(db, settings)
        _seed_inspiration_image_prompts(db, settings)
        db.commit()
        prompts = db.scalars(
            select(PromptVersion)
            .where(PromptVersion.category_key == "inspiration_image")
            .order_by(PromptVersion.id)
        ).all()
        assert [prompt.version for prompt in prompts].count(INSPIRATION_CALL_B_VERSION) == 1
        assert [prompt.version for prompt in prompts].count(INSPIRATION_CALL_A_VERSION) == 1
        legacy_a = db.scalar(
            select(PromptVersion).where(
                PromptVersion.version == INSPIRATION_REV3_CALL_A_VERSION
            )
        )
        active_a = db.scalar(
            select(PromptVersion).where(
                PromptVersion.version == INSPIRATION_CALL_A_VERSION
            )
        )
        assert legacy_a is not None and active_a is not None
        assert legacy_a.system_prompt == (
            PROJECT_ROOT / "prompts" / "inspiration_image_call_a_rev3.txt"
        ).read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
        assert active_a.system_prompt != legacy_a.system_prompt
        assert db.scalar(
            select(PromptVersion.system_prompt).where(
                PromptVersion.version == "inspiration-b-v1"
            )
        ) == "旧提示词不可覆盖"

        active_b = db.scalar(
            select(PromptVersion).where(
                PromptVersion.version == INSPIRATION_CALL_B_VERSION
            )
        )
        assert active_b is not None
        assert '"contract_version":"inspiration-aesthetic-foundation-v1"' in (
            active_b.system_prompt
        )
        assert active_b.version == "inspiration-b-v5-anchor-calibration-evidence-20260807"
        assert active_b.pipeline_scope == "shared"
        assert db.scalar(select(PromptVersion).where(
            PromptVersion.version == "inspiration-b-v3-anchor-aesthetic-20260806"
        )) is not None


def test_existing_profile_is_bound_to_rev4_a_and_new_b_atomically() -> None:
    engine = _engine()
    with Session(engine) as db:
        settings = SimpleNamespace(project_root=PROJECT_ROOT)
        _seed_inspiration_image_prompts(db, settings)
        legacy_a = db.scalar(
            select(PromptVersion).where(
                PromptVersion.version == INSPIRATION_REV3_CALL_A_VERSION
            )
        )
        legacy_b = db.scalar(
            select(PromptVersion).where(
                PromptVersion.version == "inspiration-b-v2-human-calibrated-20260805"
            )
        )
        assert legacy_a is not None and legacy_b is not None
        profile = EvaluationCategoryProfile(
            category_key="inspiration_image",
            display_name="灵感图",
            prompt_a_id=legacy_a.id,
            prompt_b_id=legacy_b.id,
            pipeline_revision=2,
        )
        db.add(profile)
        db.commit()

        _seed_inspiration_image_prompts(db, settings)
        db.commit()
        db.refresh(profile)
        assert (
            db.get(PromptVersion, profile.prompt_a_id).version == INSPIRATION_CALL_A_VERSION
        )
        assert (
            db.get(PromptVersion, profile.prompt_b_id).version == INSPIRATION_CALL_B_VERSION
        )
        assert profile.pipeline_revision == 3

        _seed_inspiration_image_prompts(db, settings)
        db.commit()
        db.refresh(profile)
        assert profile.pipeline_revision == 3


def test_existing_inspiration_config_is_replaced_once_and_stays_active() -> None:
    engine = _engine()
    with Session(engine) as db:
        db.add(
            CategoryEvaluationV3Config(
                category_key="inspiration_image",
                display_name="灵感图",
                status="active",
                contract_json=json.dumps({"spec_version": "legacy"}),
                classification_map_json="{}",
                subcategory_dimensions_json="{}",
                dimension_deduction_rules_json="{}",
                media_penalty_enabled=True,
                revision=7,
                contract_hash="legacy",
                created_by="test",
            )
        )
        db.commit()
        _seed_inspiration_image_v3_config(db)
        db.commit()
        row = db.scalar(
            select(CategoryEvaluationV3Config).where(
                CategoryEvaluationV3Config.category_key == "inspiration_image"
            )
        )
        assert row is not None
        assert row.status == "active"
        assert row.revision == 8
        assert row.media_penalty_enabled is False
        assert json.loads(row.contract_json)["spec_version"] == INSPIRATION_SPEC_VERSION

        frozen = row.contract_json
        _seed_inspiration_image_v3_config(db)
        db.commit()
        assert row.revision == 8
        assert row.contract_json == frozen

def test_seed_defaults_clones_active_v3_contract_and_prompts_for_all_categories() -> None:
    engine = _engine()
    expected_categories = {
        "space_image",
        "inspiration_image",
        "material_image",
        "pdf_text",
        "proposal_text_pdf",
        "model_3d_su",
    }
    with Session(engine) as db:
        seed_defaults(db)
        rows = db.scalars(
            select(CategoryEvaluationV3Config).order_by(
                CategoryEvaluationV3Config.category_key
            )
        ).all()
        assert {row.category_key for row in rows} == expected_categories
        assert {row.status for row in rows} == {"active"}

        revisions_before = {row.category_key: row.revision for row in rows}
        prompt_count_before = len(db.scalars(select(PromptVersion)).all())
        for row in rows:
            contract = json.loads(row.contract_json)
            classification_map = json.loads(row.classification_map_json)
            dimensions_by_track = json.loads(row.subcategory_dimensions_json)

            assert contract["category_key"] == row.category_key
            if row.category_key == "proposal_text_pdf":
                validate_proposal_text_contract(contract)
                assert contract["profile_type"] == "text-proposal-additive-v1"
                assert dimensions_by_track["profile_type"] == "text-proposal-additive-v1"
                for stage, version_key in (("A", "call_a_version"), ("B", "call_b_version")):
                    prompt = db.scalar(select(PromptVersion).where(
                        PromptVersion.version == contract[version_key]
                    ))
                    assert prompt is not None
                    assert prompt.stage == stage
                    assert prompt.category_key == row.category_key
                    assert prompt.status == "published"
                    assert prompt.source == "imported"
                continue
            if row.category_key == "inspiration_image":
                assert contract["spec_version"] == INSPIRATION_SPEC_VERSION
            else:
                assert row.category_key.replace("_", "-") in contract["spec_version"]
                assert contract["common_modifiers"]["format_version"] == "common-modifiers-v1"
                assert "authoritative_precheck_contract" not in contract
                assert row.revision == 1
            assert dimensions_by_track
            assert {
                len(config["common_group"]["schema_definition"]["dimensions"])
                for config in dimensions_by_track.values()
            } == ({5} if row.category_key == "model_3d_su" else {5, 6})

            validate_category_evaluation_contract(contract)
            track_keys = {
                track["key"]
                for track in contract["track_classification"]["tracks"]
            }
            validate_classification_map(
                classification_map, valid_track_keys=track_keys
            )
            for config in dimensions_by_track.values():
                validate_subcategory_dimensions(config)

            for stage, binding_key in (
                ("A", "call_a_version"),
                ("B", "call_b_version"),
            ):
                prompt = db.scalar(
                    select(PromptVersion).where(
                        PromptVersion.version
                        == contract["prompt_bindings"][binding_key]
                    )
                )
                assert prompt is not None
                assert prompt.stage == stage
                assert prompt.category_key == row.category_key
                assert prompt.status == "published"
                assert prompt.source == (
                    "imported"
                    if row.category_key in {"inspiration_image", "model_3d_su"}
                    else "v3_seed_clone"
                )

        seed_defaults(db)
        rows_after = db.scalars(select(CategoryEvaluationV3Config)).all()
        assert len(rows_after) == len(expected_categories)
        assert {row.category_key: row.revision for row in rows_after} == revisions_before
        assert len(db.scalars(select(PromptVersion)).all()) == prompt_count_before


def test_inspiration_seed_preserves_operator_candidate_revision() -> None:
    engine = _engine()
    with Session(engine) as db:
        seed_defaults(db)
        projected = db.scalar(
            select(CategoryEvaluationV3Config).where(
                CategoryEvaluationV3Config.category_key == "inspiration_image"
            )
        )
        assert projected is not None and projected.projected_revision_id is not None
        candidate_contract = json.loads(projected.contract_json)
        candidate_contract["common_modifiers"]["media_type_penalty"]["penalties"][
            "ai_image"
        ] = -9
        candidate_json = json.dumps(
            candidate_contract,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            sort_keys=True,
            separators=(",", ":"),
        )
        candidate = CategoryEvaluationV3Revision(
            category_key=projected.category_key,
            display_name="人工候选",
            revision=projected.revision + 1,
            status="candidate",
            parent_revision_id=projected.projected_revision_id,
            contract_json=candidate_json,
            classification_map_json=projected.classification_map_json,
            subcategory_dimensions_json=projected.subcategory_dimensions_json,
            dimension_deduction_rules_json=projected.dimension_deduction_rules_json,
            media_penalty_enabled=projected.media_penalty_enabled,
            contract_hash="candidate-inspiration-hash",
            created_by="operator:test",
        )
        db.add(candidate)
        db.commit()
        candidate_id = candidate.id

        seed_defaults(db)
        db.commit()
        preserved = db.get(CategoryEvaluationV3Revision, candidate_id)
        assert preserved is not None
        assert preserved.status == "candidate"
        assert preserved.contract_json == candidate_json
        assert preserved.contract_hash == "candidate-inspiration-hash"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_inspiration_semantic_consistency.py =====
from copy import deepcopy

import pytest

from app.inspiration_category_seed import build_inspiration_v3_contract
from app.inspiration_semantic_consistency import (
    LATEST_INSPIRATION_SEMANTIC_DIMENSION_KEYS,
    inspiration_semantic_conflicts,
)
from app.strict_prompt_contracts import (
    LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE,
    merged_strict_output_contract,
    strict_output_contract,
)


def _latest_call_a_schema() -> dict:
    evaluation_contract = build_inspiration_v3_contract()
    evaluation_contract["image_defects_enabled"] = False
    return strict_output_contract(
        "inspiration_image",
        "A",
        evaluation_contract=evaluation_contract,
        contract_profile=LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE,
    )["schema"]


def _conflicts(contract_name: str, payload: dict) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    output_schema = _latest_call_a_schema()
    if contract_name == "inspiration_latest_single":
        evaluation_contract = build_inspiration_v3_contract()
        evaluation_contract["image_defects_enabled"] = False
        evaluation_contract["aesthetic_foundation"]["dimension_keys"] = list(
            LATEST_INSPIRATION_SEMANTIC_DIMENSION_KEYS
        )
        output_schema = merged_strict_output_contract(
            "inspiration_image",
            ["A", "B"],
            evaluation_contract=evaluation_contract,
            name="inspiration_latest_single",
            description="semantic test contract",
            contract_profile=LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE,
        )["schema"]
    return inspiration_semantic_conflicts(
        contract_name,
        payload,
        contract_profile=LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE,
        output_schema=output_schema,
    )


def _latest_single_schema() -> dict:
    evaluation_contract = build_inspiration_v3_contract()
    evaluation_contract["image_defects_enabled"] = False
    evaluation_contract["aesthetic_foundation"]["dimension_keys"] = list(
        LATEST_INSPIRATION_SEMANTIC_DIMENSION_KEYS
    )
    return merged_strict_output_contract(
        "inspiration_image",
        ["A", "B"],
        evaluation_contract=evaluation_contract,
        name="inspiration_latest_single",
        description="semantic test contract",
        contract_profile=LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE,
    )["schema"]


def _consistent_payload() -> dict:
    return {
        "redline_triggered": {
            "multi_image_collage": False,
            "large_area_watermark": False,
            "casual_photo": False,
            "screenshot": False,
            "upside_down_or_mirrored": False,
            "text_heavy": False,
            "qr_code_heavy": False,
            "unfinished_hand_sketch": False,
        },
        "reason": [],
        "hard_defects": [],
        "decisive_evidence": {
            "redline_triggered": [],
            "hard_defects": [],
        },
        "decision_status": "complete",
        "uncertain_fields": [],
        "track_classification": "class_one",
        "track_confidence": 0.93,
        "media_type": "3d_render",
        "media_confidence": 0.91,
        "trait": "3D数字效果图",
        "primary_category": "居住空间",
        "secondary_category": "现代客厅",
        "classification_confidence": 0.92,
    }


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "contract_name",
    ("inspiration_call_a", "inspiration_latest_single"),
)
def test_consistent_negative_decisions_have_no_semantic_conflicts(
    contract_name: str,
) -> None:
    assert _conflicts(contract_name, _consistent_payload()) == ()


def test_run_6835_negative_text_evidence_is_rejected_before_finalization() -> None:
    payload = _consistent_payload()
    payload["decisive_evidence"]["redline_triggered"] = [
        {
            "key": "text_heavy",
            "evidence": "画面文字块总面积未达40%，无符合触发条件的大面积文字内容",
        }
    ]

    assert _conflicts("inspiration_call_a", payload) == (
        "evidence_conflict:redline:text_heavy",
    )


def test_run_8099_false_watermark_with_hit_reason_and_evidence_is_rejected() -> None:
    payload = _consistent_payload()
    payload["reason"] = ["有大面积水印"]
    payload["decisive_evidence"]["redline_triggered"] = [
        {
            "key": "large_area_watermark",
            "evidence": "画面左下角存在品牌水印，属于角落小水印未大面积干扰",
        }
    ]

    assert _conflicts("inspiration_latest_single", payload) == (
        "redline_reason_conflict:large_area_watermark",
        "evidence_conflict:redline:large_area_watermark",
    )


def test_run_8363_false_watermark_with_only_hit_evidence_is_rejected() -> None:
    payload = _consistent_payload()
    payload["decisive_evidence"]["redline_triggered"] = [
        {
            "key": "large_area_watermark",
            "evidence": "画面左下角存在“★HY设计”水印，属于角落小水印未干扰主体",
        }
    ]

    assert _conflicts("inspiration_latest_single", payload) == (
        "evidence_conflict:redline:large_area_watermark",
    )


def test_live_six_dimension_schema_is_required_exactly() -> None:
    schema = _latest_single_schema()
    schema["properties"]["dimensions"]["properties"].pop("originality")
    schema["properties"]["dimensions"]["required"].remove("originality")
    payload = _consistent_payload()
    payload["reason"] = ["有大面积水印"]

    assert inspiration_semantic_conflicts(
        "inspiration_latest_single",
        payload,
        contract_profile=LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE,
        output_schema=schema,
    ) == ()


def test_true_redline_with_matching_reason_and_evidence_is_consistent() -> None:
    payload = _consistent_payload()
    payload["redline_triggered"]["screenshot"] = True
    payload["reason"] = ["是截图"]
    payload["decisive_evidence"]["redline_triggered"] = [
        {"key": "screenshot", "evidence": "画面顶部可见浏览器地址栏"}
    ]

    assert _conflicts("inspiration_call_a", payload) == ()


def test_hard_defect_hit_with_matching_evidence_is_consistent() -> None:
    payload = _consistent_payload()
    payload["hard_defects"] = ["fake_material"]
    payload["decisive_evidence"]["hard_defects"] = [
        {"key": "fake_material", "evidence": "木纹与表面反光方向明显矛盾"}
    ]

    assert _conflicts("inspiration_call_a", payload) == ()


def test_hard_defect_evidence_without_hit_is_a_semantic_conflict() -> None:
    payload = _consistent_payload()
    payload["decisive_evidence"]["hard_defects"] = [
        {"key": "fake_material", "evidence": "木纹与表面反光方向明显矛盾"}
    ]

    assert _conflicts("inspiration_call_a", payload) == (
        "evidence_without_hit:hard_defects:fake_material",
    )


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("field", "key", "expected"),
    (
        (
            "redline",
            "screenshot",
            "missing_evidence:redline:screenshot",
        ),
        (
            "hard_defects",
            "fake_material",
            "missing_evidence:hard_defects:fake_material",
        ),
    ),
)
def test_true_decision_without_evidence_is_a_semantic_conflict(
    field: str,
    key: str,
    expected: str,
) -> None:
    payload = _consistent_payload()
    if field == "redline":
        payload["redline_triggered"][key] = True
        payload["reason"] = ["是截图"]
    else:
        payload[field] = [key]

    assert _conflicts("inspiration_call_a", payload) == (expected,)


def test_uncertain_business_decision_is_not_reclassified_as_consistency_error() -> None:
    payload = _consistent_payload()
    payload["decision_status"] = "uncertain"
    payload["uncertain_fields"] = ["media_type"]

    assert _conflicts("inspiration_call_a", payload) == ()


def test_unrelated_output_contract_is_out_of_scope() -> None:
    payload = deepcopy(_consistent_payload())
    payload["reason"] = ["有大面积水印"]

    assert inspiration_semantic_conflicts(
        "model_3d_su_call_a",
        payload,
        contract_profile=LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE,
        output_schema=_latest_call_a_schema(),
    ) == ()
