    payload["redline_triggered"]["screenshot"] = True
    payload["reason"] = ["自定义截图规则"]

    assert inspiration_semantic_conflicts(
        "inspiration_call_a",
        payload,
        contract_profile=None,
        output_schema={
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "redline_triggered": {
                    "type": "object",
                    "properties": {"screenshot": {"type": "boolean"}},
                },
                "reason": {
                    "type": "array",
                    "items": {"type": "string", "enum": ["自定义截图规则"]},
                },
            },
        },
    ) == ()


def test_latest_profile_with_nonmatching_frozen_schema_is_out_of_scope() -> None:
    payload = _consistent_payload()
    payload["redline_triggered"]["screenshot"] = True
    payload["reason"] = ["自定义截图规则"]

    assert inspiration_semantic_conflicts(
        "inspiration_call_a",
        payload,
        contract_profile=LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE,
        output_schema={
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "redline_triggered": {
                    "type": "object",
                    "properties": {"screenshot": {"type": "boolean"}},
                },
                "reason": {
                    "type": "array",
                    "items": {"type": "string", "enum": ["自定义截图规则"]},
                },
            },
        },
    ) == ()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "mutation",
    (
        "missing_decisive_evidence",
        "redline_value_not_boolean",
        "opened_redline_object",
        "reason_not_required",
    ),
)
def test_latest_profile_requires_exact_frozen_strict_schema(
    mutation: str,
) -> None:
    schema = deepcopy(_latest_call_a_schema())
    if mutation == "missing_decisive_evidence":
        schema["properties"].pop("decisive_evidence")
    elif mutation == "redline_value_not_boolean":
        schema["properties"]["redline_triggered"]["properties"]["screenshot"] = {
            "type": "string"
        }
    elif mutation == "opened_redline_object":
        schema["properties"]["redline_triggered"]["additionalProperties"] = True
    elif mutation == "reason_not_required":
        schema["required"].remove("reason")
    else:
        raise AssertionError(mutation)

    payload = _consistent_payload()
    payload["reason"] = ["有大面积水印"]
    assert inspiration_semantic_conflicts(
        "inspiration_call_a",
        payload,
        contract_profile=LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE,
        output_schema=schema,
    ) == ()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_inspiration_terminal_failure.py =====
"""Real ingress identity -> failed runtime -> outbox -> terminal readback."""
import hashlib
import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select, func

from app import inspiration_factory_intake as intake, incremental_materials
from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.connector_contracts import create_connector_contract_version
from app.connector_delivery import deliver_outbox_batch
from app.incremental_input import build_frozen_content_ingress_input
from app.incremental_material_models import IncrementalMaterialJob
from app.models import (ProductionRun, WorkflowVersion, ContentIngressEvent, ContentRecord,
                        RuntimeOutboxEvent, DownstreamPublishLedger)
from app.inspiration_factory_failure import record_terminal_failure, FAILURE_MODE
from app.inspiration_factory_delivery import (InspirationFactoryDownstreamAdapter,
    INSPIRATION_FACTORY_ADAPTER_KEY, INSPIRATION_FACTORY_SCHEMA_FINGERPRINT)
from app.workflow_runtime import terminalize_runtime_run
from app.workflow_snapshot_store import load_snapshot_blob
from test_inspiration_factory_integration import env, URL, IDENTITY
from test_inspiration_factory_failure import FailedFactory
from test_inspiration_factory_delivery import CONFIG


def setup_run(db, *, mode="enabled"):
    incremental_materials.process_due_material_jobs(db, actor="test", now=datetime.now(timezone.utc))
    job = db.scalar(select(IncrementalMaterialJob)); assert job.status == "completed"
    event = db.get(ContentIngressEvent, job.event_id)
    frozen = build_frozen_content_ingress_input(db, event=event, record=db.get(ContentRecord, event.content_record_id))
    target = create_connector_contract_version(db, contract_key="inspiration-failure-output",
        direction="downstream", category_key="inspiration_image", logical_locator="logical.inspiration_image.failure",
        adapter_key=INSPIRATION_FACTORY_ADAPTER_KEY,
        schema_fingerprint=INSPIRATION_FACTORY_SCHEMA_FINGERPRINT,
        field_mapping=CONFIG, cursor_contract={}, idempotency_contract={"field": "business_idempotency_key"},
        batch_size=1, timeout_seconds=30, owner="test", status="active", created_by="test")
    mapping = {"schema_version": "inspiration-factory-result-v1", "terminal_failure_mode": FAILURE_MODE}
    if mode == "no_opt_in": mapping.pop("terminal_failure_mode")
    node = {"kind": "downstream_projection", "node_instance_id": "publish", "config": {
        "mapping": mapping, "connector_key": target.contract_key,
        "connector_snapshot": {"connector_contract_id": target.id, "contract_key": target.contract_key,
            "direction": "downstream", "contract_version": target.version,
            "schema_fingerprint": target.schema_fingerprint}}}
    snapshot = {"workflow": {"manifest": {"category_key": "inspiration_image", "nodes": [node], "edges": []}},
                "runtime_context": {"run_mode": "incremental", "model_execution_mode": "real", "input_manifest": frozen}}
    if mode == "identity_drift": frozen["inspiration_task"]["project_id"] += 1
    if mode == "simulated": snapshot["runtime_context"]["model_execution_mode"] = "simulated"
    version = db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion).order_by(WorkflowVersion.id))
    raw = canonical_json(snapshot)
    run = ProductionRun(run_key="inspiration-terminal-test", idempotency_key="inspiration-terminal-test",
        source_type="content_ingress_event", source_id=event.id, workflow_definition_id=version.workflow_definition_id,
        workflow_version_id=version.id, snapshot_json=raw, snapshot_hash=hashlib.sha256(raw.encode()).hexdigest(),
        category_key="inspiration_image", run_mode="incremental", model_execution_mode="real",
        queue_class="production_batch", status="running", requested_by="test", owner="test")
    db.add(run); db.flush()
    return run, target


def test_terminal_failure_is_atomic_idempotent_and_delivered_as_failure(env, tmp_path):
    path, kwargs, calls = env
    intake.stream_cycle(path, **kwargs)
    with kwargs["sessions"]() as db:
        run, target = setup_run(db)
        frozen = run.snapshot_json
        terminalize_runtime_run(db, run, status="failed", error_code="model_output_invalid",
                                error_message="sensitive provider diagnostic")
        first = db.scalar(select(RuntimeOutboxEvent)); assert first is not None
        assert record_terminal_failure(db, run).id == first.id
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(RuntimeOutboxEvent.id))) == 1
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(DownstreamPublishLedger.id))) == 1
        payload = load_snapshot_blob(first.payload_blob)
        assert payload["result"]["status"] == "failed" and first.max_attempts == 1
        assert "sensitive" not in canonical_json(payload)
        assert run.status == "failed" and run.snapshot_json == frozen
        db.commit()
        fake = FailedFactory(); fake.task.update(IDENTITY)
        private = tmp_path / "delivery"; private.mkdir(mode=0o700)
        adapter = InspirationFactoryDownstreamAdapter(URL, level_mapping=CONFIG["level_mapping"],
            review_result_values=CONFIG["review_result_values"], journal_path=str(private / "journal.sqlite"), send=fake.send)
        outcome = deliver_outbox_batch(db, target, worker_id="failure-test", adapter=adapter)
        assert outcome.delivered_event_ids == (first.id,)
        db.commit()
        assert first.status == "delivered" and run.status == "failed"
        assert fake.task["status"] == 5 and fake.task["score"] is None
        assert calls.count("confirmClaim") == 1 and fake.actions.count("submit") == 1


@pytest.mark.parametrize("mode", ["no_opt_in", "identity_drift", "simulated", "canceled"])
def test_failure_boundaries_never_queue_or_roll_back_terminal_state(env, mode):
    path, kwargs, _ = env; intake.stream_cycle(path, **kwargs)
    with kwargs["sessions"]() as db:
        run, _ = setup_run(db, mode=mode)
        terminal = "canceled" if mode == "canceled" else "failed"
        terminalize_runtime_run(db, run, status=terminal, error_code="model_output_invalid", error_message="private")
        db.commit()
        assert run.status == terminal and db.scalar(select(func.count(RuntimeOutboxEvent.id))) == 0


def test_outer_rollback_preserves_run_and_emits_no_failure_event(env):
    path, kwargs, _ = env; intake.stream_cycle(path, **kwargs)
    with kwargs["sessions"]() as db:
        run, _ = setup_run(db); db.commit(); run_id = run.id
        terminalize_runtime_run(db, run, status="failed", error_code="model_output_invalid", error_message="private")
        db.rollback(); db.expire_all()
        assert db.get(ProductionRun, run_id).status == "running"
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(RuntimeOutboxEvent.id))) == 0


def test_later_failed_run_cannot_create_new_automatic_revision(env):
    path, kwargs, _ = env; intake.stream_cycle(path, **kwargs)
    with kwargs["sessions"]() as db:
        first, _ = setup_run(db)
        terminalize_runtime_run(db, first, status="failed", error_code="model_output_invalid", error_message="private")
        event = db.scalar(select(RuntimeOutboxEvent)); assert event is not None
        original_payload = load_snapshot_blob(event.payload_blob)
        later = ProductionRun(run_key="later-failed-run", idempotency_key="later-failed-run",
            source_type=first.source_type, source_id=first.source_id,
            workflow_definition_id=first.workflow_definition_id, workflow_version_id=first.workflow_version_id,
            snapshot_json=first.snapshot_json, snapshot_hash=first.snapshot_hash,
            category_key=first.category_key, run_mode=first.run_mode, model_execution_mode="real",
            queue_class="production_batch", status="running", requested_by="test", owner="test")
        db.add(later); db.flush()
        terminalize_runtime_run(db, later, status="failed", error_code="model_output_invalid", error_message="private")
        db.commit()
        assert later.status == "failed" and db.scalar(select(func.count(RuntimeOutboxEvent.id))) == 1
        assert load_snapshot_blob(event.payload_blob) == original_payload

===== FILE backend/tests/test_invocation_review.py =====
import json
import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import select,func
from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.models import WorkflowModelInvocation,ModelInvocationReview,AuditEvent,User
from app.invocation_review import fingerprint,review_unknown_invocation,list_unknown_invocations
from app.failure_console import FailureConsoleError
from app.incremental_routing import drain_state
from test_failure_recovery import setup


def args(call):
    return dict(invocation_id=call.id,quote_hash=fingerprint(call),evidence_reference='/evidence/provider-request-123.json',
        reason='供应商回执确认执行已结束，原费用待对账',execution_ended_confirmed=True,liability_retained_confirmed=True,actor='admin-test')


def test_review_releases_historical_gate_without_touching_billing_or_results(setup):
    db,run,node,snapshot,attempt=setup
    call=db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
    persisted=select(WorkflowModelInvocation.__table__).where(WorkflowModelInvocation.id==call.id)
    before=dict(db.execute(persisted).mappings().one())
    scope=dict(category_key=run.category_key,workflow_definition_id=run.workflow_definition_id)
    assert drain_state(db,**scope)['provider_unresolved']==1
    options=args(call)
    assert not review_unknown_invocation(db,**options)['idempotent_replay'];db.commit()
    assert review_unknown_invocation(db,**options)['idempotent_replay']
    db.refresh(call)
    assert before==dict(db.execute(persisted).mappings().one())
    state=drain_state(db,**scope)
    assert state['provider_unresolved']==0 and state['provider_reviewed_billing_pending']==1
    assert list_unknown_invocations(db)['total']==0
    row=list_unknown_invocations(db,reviewed=True)['items'][0]
    assert row['billing_status']=='pending_reconciliation'
    assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(ModelInvocationReview))==1
    assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.action=='historical_execution_reviewed'))==1


@pytest.mark.parametrize('change',[{'execution_ended_confirmed':False},{'liability_retained_confirmed':False},
    {'evidence_reference':' '},{'reason':' '},{'quote_hash':'0'*64}])
def test_missing_evidence_confirmation_or_stale_quote_is_rejected(setup,change):
    db,run,node,snapshot,attempt=setup;call=db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation));options=args(call);options.update(change)
    with pytest.raises(FailureConsoleError):review_unknown_invocation(db,**options)
    assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(ModelInvocationReview))==0


def test_active_run_is_not_closed(setup):
    db,run,node,snapshot,attempt=setup;call=db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
    run.status='running';db.flush()
    with pytest.raises(FailureConsoleError):review_unknown_invocation(db,**args(call))
    assert drain_state(db,category_key=run.category_key,workflow_definition_id=run.workflow_definition_id)['provider_unresolved']==1


def test_reviewer_cannot_register_execution_or_financial_closure():
    from app.main import app,current_user
    from app.database import get_db
    previous=dict(app.dependency_overrides)
    app.dependency_overrides[current_user]=lambda:User(username='reviewer',is_admin=False,role='reviewer')
    def database():yield None
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db]=database
    try:
        response=TestClient(app).post('/api/failures/invocations/1/review',json={
            'quote_hash':'a'*64,'evidence_reference':'evidence','reason':'review',
            'execution_ended_confirmed':True,'liability_retained_confirmed':True})
        assert response.status_code==403
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear();app.dependency_overrides.update(previous)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_job_controls.py =====

def test_jobs_pin_prompts_and_support_pause_resume_cancel() -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    user = User(is_admin=True, role="admin", username="tester", password_hash="unused", display_name="测试员")
    asset = Asset(
        original_name="room.jpg",
        stored_name="room.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=100,
        width=1200,
        height=800,
        sha256="b" * 64,
    )
    prompt_a = PromptVersion(
        stage="A",
        name="分类",
        version="A-2.1",
        system_prompt="system prompt for classification",
        user_prompt="user prompt",
        status="published",
    )
    prompt_b = PromptVersion(
        stage="B",
        name="美感",
        version="B-2.3",
        system_prompt="system prompt for aesthetic scoring",
        user_prompt="user prompt",
        status="draft",
    )
    model = ModelConfig(
        name="自定义主模型",
        provider="custom-compatible",
        base_url="https://model.example.test/v1",
        model_id="vision-model-v1",
        encrypted_api_key="credential-reference-never-snapshot",
    )
    schema = _supported_dimension_schema()
    db.add_all(
        [
            user,
            asset,
            prompt_a,
            prompt_b,
            model,
            schema,
            EvaluationControl(id=1),
        ]
    )
    v3_bundle = add_active_v3_contract(db)
    db.commit()

    def test_db():
        yield db

    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = test_db
    app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: user
    client = TestClient(app)
    try:
        created = client.post(
            "/api/jobs/enqueue",
            json={
                "asset_ids": [asset.id],
                "prompt_a_id": prompt_a.id,
                "prompt_b_id": prompt_b.id,
            },
        )
        assert created.status_code == 200
        job_id = created.json()["job_ids"][0]
        frozen_job = db.get(EvaluationJob, job_id)
        frozen_profile = json.loads(frozen_job.category_profile_snapshot_json)
        assert frozen_profile["schema_version"] == "evaluation-category-profile-v2"
        assert frozen_profile["category_key"] == "space_image"
        assert frozen_profile["prompt_a_id"] == prompt_a.id
        assert frozen_profile["prompt_b_id"] == prompt_b.id
        assert frozen_profile["model_config"]["provider"] == "custom-compatible"
        assert frozen_profile["dimension_contract"] is None
        frozen_v3 = dict(frozen_profile["v3_authoritative_bundle"])
        assert frozen_v3.pop("config_revision") == 1
        assert frozen_v3 == v3_bundle
        assert "credential-reference-never-snapshot" not in (
            frozen_job.category_profile_snapshot_json
        )

        original_snapshot = frozen_job.category_profile_snapshot_json
        profile = db.scalar(
            select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(
                EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key == "space_image"
            )
        )
        profile.status = "retired"
        profile.preprocess_config_json = '{"changed_after_enqueue":true}'
        model.active = False
        model.provider = "changed-after-enqueue"
        db.commit()
        assert frozen_job.category_profile_snapshot_json == original_snapshot
        frozen_contract = _frozen_category_contract(frozen_job, asset)
        assert frozen_contract is not None
        assert frozen_contract["preprocess_config"] == {"preprocess": "image"}
        assert frozen_contract["model_config"]["provider"] == "custom-compatible"
        profile.status = "active"
        db.commit()

        listed = client.get("/api/jobs").json()["items"][0]
        assert listed["prompt_a_version"] == "A-2.1"
        assert listed["prompt_b_version"] == "B-2.3"
        assert listed["updated_at"]

        paused = client.post("/api/jobs/control/pause")
        assert paused.status_code == 200
        assert paused.json()["affected"] == 1
        db.expire_all()
        assert db.get(EvaluationJob, job_id).status == "paused"
        assert db.get(EvaluationJob, job_id).updated_at is not None
        assert client.get("/api/jobs/control").json()["paused"] is True

        resumed = client.post("/api/jobs/control/resume")
        assert resumed.status_code == 200
        db.expire_all()
        assert db.get(EvaluationJob, job_id).status == "queued"

        canceled = client.post("/api/jobs/control/cancel")
        assert canceled.status_code == 200
        db.expire_all()
        assert db.get(EvaluationJob, job_id).status == "canceled"
        assert db.get(Asset, asset.id).status == "uploaded"
        canceled_asset = client.get(
            "/api/assets",
            params={
                "prompt_a_id": prompt_a.id,
                "prompt_b_id": prompt_b.id,
            },
        ).json()["items"][0]
        assert canceled_asset["evaluation_status"] == "failed"

        single = client.post(
            "/api/jobs/enqueue",
            json={"asset_ids": [asset.id], "prompt_id": prompt_a.id},
        )
        assert single.status_code == 200
        single_job = client.get("/api/jobs").json()["items"][0]
        assert single_job["prompt_version"] == "A-2.1"
        assert single_job["prompt_a_version"] is None
        assert single_job["prompt_b_version"] is None

        profile.pipeline_config_json = json.dumps(
            {**default_pipeline("space_image"), "prompt_mode": "single"},
            ensure_ascii=False,
        )
        profile.prompt_a_id = prompt_a.id
        profile.prompt_b_id = None
        db.commit()
        wrong_single = client.post(
            "/api/jobs/enqueue",
            json={
                "asset_ids": [asset.id],
                "prompt_a_id": prompt_a.id,
                "prompt_b_id": prompt_b.id,
            },
        )
        assert wrong_single.status_code == 409
        assert wrong_single.json()["detail"]["code"] == "prompt_mode_mismatch"

        profile.pipeline_config_json = json.dumps(
            {**default_pipeline("space_image"), "prompt_mode": "ab"},
            ensure_ascii=False,
        )
        profile.prompt_a_id = prompt_a.id
        profile.prompt_b_id = prompt_b.id
        db.commit()
        wrong_ab = client.post(
            "/api/jobs/enqueue",
            json={"asset_ids": [asset.id], "prompt_id": prompt_a.id},
        )
        assert wrong_ab.status_code == 409
        assert wrong_ab.json()["detail"]["code"] == "prompt_mode_mismatch"

        incomplete_pair = client.post(
            "/api/jobs/enqueue",
            json={"asset_ids": [asset.id], "prompt_a_id": prompt_a.id},
        )
        assert incomplete_pair.status_code == 422
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_frozen_category_contract_rejects_mime_and_secret_drift() -> None:
    asset = Asset(
        id=1,
        original_name="room.jpg",
        stored_name="room.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=100,
        sha256="c" * 64,
    )
    base_snapshot = {
        "schema_version": "evaluation-category-profile-v1",
        "profile_id": 1,
        "category_key": "space_image",
        "display_name": "空间图片",
        "allowed_mime_types": ["image/png"],
        "preprocess_config": {"preprocess": "image"},
        "prompt_a_id": 11,
        "prompt_b_id": None,
        "model_config_id": 21,
        "model_config": {
            "name": "模型",
            "provider": "custom",
            "base_url": "https://model.example.test/v1",
            "api_path": "/chat/completions",
            "model_id": "vision-v1",
            "temperature": 0.1,
            "max_tokens": 4096,
            "timeout_seconds": 120,
            "max_retries": 1,
            "max_concurrency": 2,
            "structured_output": True,
            "high_risk_review_enabled": True,
        },
        "rubric_version": "rubric-v2.1",
        "dimension_schema_key": None,
        "dimension_schema_version": None,
        "profile_updated_at": None,
    }
    job = EvaluationJob(
        id=1,
        asset_id=1,
        category_key="space_image",
        prompt_a_id=11,
        prompt_b_id=None,
        category_profile_snapshot_json=json.dumps(base_snapshot),
    )
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="MIME"):
        _frozen_category_contract(job, asset)

    base_snapshot["allowed_mime_types"] = ["image/jpeg"]
    base_snapshot["model_config"]["encrypted_api_key"] = "must-not-survive"
    job.category_profile_snapshot_json = json.dumps(base_snapshot)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="模型快照"):
        _frozen_category_contract(job, asset)


def test_frozen_category_contract_rejects_dimension_selection_drift() -> None:
    asset = Asset(
        id=1,
        original_name="room.jpg",
        stored_name="room.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=100,
        sha256="f" * 64,
    )
    pipeline = default_pipeline("space_image")
    pipeline["dimensions"] = {
        "enabled": True,
        "mode": "selected",
        "selected_keys": ["composition_viewpoint"],
    }
    profile = EvaluationCategoryProfile(
        id=1,
        category_key="space_image",
        display_name="空间图片",
        status="active",
        allowed_mime_types_json='["image/jpeg"]',
        preprocess_config_json="{}",
        pipeline_config_json=canonical_json(pipeline),
        prompt_a_id=11,
        prompt_b_id=None,
        rubric_version="rubric-v2.1",
    )
    model = ModelConfig(
        id=21,
        name="模型",
        provider="custom-compatible",
        base_url="https://model.example.test/v1",
        api_path="/chat/completions",
        model_id="vision-v1",
    )
    snapshot = json.loads(
        _category_execution_snapshot(
            profile,
            prompt_a_id=11,
            prompt_b_id=None,
            model_config=model,
        )
    )
    job = EvaluationJob(
        id=1,
        asset_id=1,
        category_key="space_image",
        prompt_a_id=11,
        prompt_b_id=None,
        category_profile_snapshot_json=json.dumps(snapshot),
    )
    assert _frozen_category_contract(job, asset)["dimension_selection"][
        "effective_keys"
    ] == ["composition_viewpoint"]

    snapshot["dimension_selection"]["effective_keys"] = [
        "presentation_integrity"
    ]
    job.category_profile_snapshot_json = json.dumps(snapshot)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="维度选择与流水线不一致"):
        _frozen_category_contract(job, asset)


def test_frozen_category_contract_accepts_selected_key_from_custom_schema() -> None:
    definition = json.loads(
        json.dumps(space_schema_definition_for_version(ACTIVE_V13_VERSION))
    )
    old_key = definition["dimensions"][-1]["key"]
    custom_key = "material_authenticity"
    definition["dimensions"][-1]["key"] = custom_key
    definition["dimensions"][-1]["label"] = "材质真实性"
    definition["output_contract"]["dimension_output_keys"][-1] = custom_key
    definition["risk_review"]["dimension_keys"][-1] = custom_key
    definition["core_dimension_keys"] = [
        custom_key if key == old_key else key
        for key in definition["core_dimension_keys"]
    ]
    definition_hash = canonical_hash(definition)
    dimension_contract = SimpleNamespace(
        schema_id=99,
        schema_key="material.managed",
        version="1.0.0",
        canonical_hash=definition_hash,
        definition=definition,
    )
    pipeline = default_pipeline("material_image")
    pipeline["prompt_mode"] = "single"
    pipeline["dimensions"] = {
        "enabled": True,
        "mode": "selected",
        "selected_keys": [custom_key],
    }
    profile = EvaluationCategoryProfile(
        id=1,
        category_key="material_image",
        display_name="材质图",
        status="active",
        allowed_mime_types_json='["image/jpeg"]',
        preprocess_config_json="{}",
        pipeline_config_json=canonical_json(pipeline),
        prompt_a_id=11,
        prompt_b_id=None,
        rubric_version="material-v1",
        dimension_schema_key=dimension_contract.schema_key,
        dimension_schema_version=dimension_contract.version,
    )
    model = ModelConfig(
        id=21,
        provider="custom-compatible",
        base_url="https://model.example.test/v1",
        api_path="/chat/completions",
        model_id="vision-v1",
    )
    snapshot = json.loads(
        _category_execution_snapshot(
            profile,
            prompt_a_id=11,
            prompt_b_id=None,
            model_config=model,
            dimension_contract=dimension_contract,
        )
    )
    assert snapshot["dimension_selection"]["effective_keys"] == [custom_key]
    asset = Asset(
        id=1,
        original_name="material.jpg",
        stored_name="material.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=100,
        sha256="a" * 64,
    )
    job = EvaluationJob(
        id=1,
        asset_id=1,
        category_key="material_image",
        prompt_a_id=11,
        prompt_b_id=None,
        category_profile_snapshot_json=canonical_json(snapshot),
    )
    frozen = _frozen_category_contract(job, asset)
    assert frozen is not None
    assert frozen["dimension_selection"]["effective_keys"] == [custom_key]
    assert frozen["dimension_contract"]["canonical_hash"] == definition_hash


def test_worker_uses_frozen_contract_after_live_configuration_is_retired(
    monkeypatch,
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    asset = Asset(
        original_name="missing.jpg",
        stored_name="missing.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=100,
        sha256="d" * 64,
    )
    prompt = PromptVersion(
        stage="A",
        name="单提示词",
        version="single-v1",
        system_prompt="return the complete evaluation contract",
        user_prompt="evaluate {{image_metadata}}",
        rubric_version="rubric-v2.1",
        status="published",
    )
    model = ModelConfig(
        provider="custom-compatible",
        model_id="vision-v1",
        encrypted_api_key="credential-reference",
    )
    profile = EvaluationCategoryProfile(
        category_key="space_image",
        display_name="空间图片",
        status="active",
        allowed_mime_types_json='["image/jpeg"]',
        preprocess_config_json='{"preprocess":"image"}',
        prompt_a_id=None,
        prompt_b_id=None,
        model_config_id=None,
        rubric_version="rubric-v2.1",
    )
    schema = _supported_dimension_schema()
    profile.dimension_schema_key = schema.schema_key
    profile.dimension_schema_version = schema.version
    db.add_all([asset, prompt, model, profile, schema])
    db.flush()
    v3_bundle = add_active_v3_contract(db)
    snapshot = _category_execution_snapshot(
        profile,
        prompt_a_id=prompt.id,
        prompt_b_id=None,
        model_config=model,
        v3_authoritative_bundle=v3_bundle,
    )
    job = EvaluationJob(
        asset_id=asset.id,
        category_key="space_image",
        category_profile_snapshot_json=snapshot,
        prompt_a_id=prompt.id,
        prompt_b_id=None,
        status="processing",
    )
    db.add(job)
    db.commit()
    profile.status = "retired"
    profile.preprocess_config_json = '{"changed_after_enqueue":true}'
    model.active = False
    db.commit()

    @contextmanager
    def test_scope():
        try:
            yield db
            db.commit()
        except Exception:
            db.rollback()
            raise

    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "session_scope", test_scope)
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "settings", SimpleNamespace(upload_dir=tmp_path))
    try:
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="原始素材文件不存在"):
            asyncio.run(worker.evaluate_job(job.id))
    finally:
        db.close()
    engine.dispose()


def test_worker_rejects_frozen_non_published_dimension_before_asset_access(
    monkeypatch,
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://", connect_args={"check_same_thread": False}, poolclass=StaticPool
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    schema = _dimension_schema(status="candidate", version="worker-candidate-v1")

        original_name="missing.jpg", stored_name="missing.jpg", mime_type="image/jpeg",

    )
    prompt = PromptVersion(
        stage="A", name="单提示词", version="worker-candidate-prompt-v1",

        rubric_version="rubric-v2.1", status="published",

    model = ModelConfig(
        provider="custom-compatible", model_id="worker-candidate-model-v1",

    )
    profile = EvaluationCategoryProfile(
        category_key="space_image", display_name="空间图片", status="active",
        allowed_mime_types_json='["image/jpeg"]',
        preprocess_config_json='{"preprocess":"image"}',
        rubric_version="rubric-v2.1", dimension_schema_key=schema.schema_key,
        dimension_schema_version=schema.version,
    )
    db.add_all([schema, asset, prompt, model, profile])
    db.flush()
    v3_bundle = add_active_v3_contract(db)
    job = EvaluationJob(
        asset_id=asset.id, category_key="space_image", prompt_a_id=prompt.id,
        status="processing",
        category_profile_snapshot_json=_category_execution_snapshot(
            profile, prompt_a_id=prompt.id, prompt_b_id=None, model_config=model,
            v3_authoritative_bundle=v3_bundle,
        ),
    )
    db.add(job)
    db.commit()

    @contextmanager
    def test_scope():
        try:
            yield db
            db.commit()
        except Exception:
            db.rollback()
            raise

    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "session_scope", test_scope)
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "settings", SimpleNamespace(upload_dir=tmp_path))
    try:
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="原始素材文件不存在"):
            asyncio.run(worker.evaluate_job(job.id))
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def _dimension_schema(*, status: str, version: str) -> DimensionSchema:
    definition = {
        "format_version": "dimension-schema-definition-v1",
        "dimensions": [{"key": "quality", "required": True}],
    }
    published = status in {"published", "retired"}
    return DimensionSchema(
        schema_key="test.production",
        version=version,
        schema_type="family_pack",
        family_key="space",
        display_name=f"测试维度 {version}",
        status=status,
        definition_json=canonical_json(definition),
        canonical_hash=canonical_hash(definition),
        created_by="test",
        published_by="test" if published else None,
        published_at=worker.datetime.now(worker.timezone.utc) if published else None,
        retired_at=worker.datetime.now(worker.timezone.utc) if status == "retired" else None,
    )


def _supported_dimension_schema() -> DimensionSchema:
    definition = space_schema_definition_for_version(ACTIVE_V13_VERSION)
    return DimensionSchema(
        schema_key=SPACE_SCHEMA_KEY,
        version=ACTIVE_V13_VERSION,
        schema_type="family_pack",
        family_key="space",
        display_name="空间现役维度",
        status="published",
        definition_json=canonical_json(definition),
        canonical_hash=canonical_hash(definition),
        created_by="test",
        published_by="test",
        published_at=worker.datetime.now(worker.timezone.utc),
    )


def _dimension_enqueue_dependencies(db: Session, *, suffix: str):
    asset = Asset(
        original_name=f"dimension-{suffix}.jpg",
        stored_name=f"dimension-{suffix}.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=100,
        sha256=(suffix[0] if suffix else "e") * 64,
        category_key="space_image",
    )
    prompt_a = PromptVersion(
        stage="A", name="维度 A", version=f"dimension-a-{suffix}",
        system_prompt="classify image completely", user_prompt="evaluate",
        rubric_version="rubric-v2.1", status="published",
    )
    prompt_b = PromptVersion(
        stage="B", name="维度 B", version=f"dimension-b-{suffix}",
        system_prompt="score image completely", user_prompt="evaluate",
        rubric_version="rubric-v2.1", status="published",
    )
    model = ModelConfig(
        name="维度测试模型", provider="custom-compatible",
        model_id=f"dimension-model-{suffix}", active=True,
    )
    db.add_all([asset, prompt_a, prompt_b, model])
    db.flush()
    return asset, prompt_a, prompt_b, model


@pytest.mark.parametrize("status", ["draft", "candidate", "retired"])
def test_enqueue_rejects_non_published_dimension_contract(status: str) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://", connect_args={"check_same_thread": False}, poolclass=StaticPool
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    schema = _dimension_schema(status=status, version=f"{status}-v1")
    user = User(
        username="dimension-admin",
        password_hash="unused",
        display_name="维度管理员",
        role="admin",
        is_admin=True,
    )
    db.add_all([schema, user])
    db.commit()
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = lambda: db
    app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: user
    try:
        client = TestClient(app)
        client.get("/api/evaluation-categories")
        profile = db.scalar(
            select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(
                EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key == "space_image"
            )
        )
        asset, prompt_a, prompt_b, model = _dimension_enqueue_dependencies(
            db, suffix=status
        )
        profile.prompt_a_id = prompt_a.id
        profile.prompt_b_id = prompt_b.id
        profile.model_config_id = model.id
        profile.rubric_version = "rubric-v2.1"
        profile.dimension_schema_key = schema.schema_key
        profile.dimension_schema_version = schema.version
        db.commit()
        response = client.post(
            "/api/jobs/enqueue", json={"asset_ids": [asset.id]}
        )
        assert response.status_code == 409
        assert response.json()["detail"]["code"] == "v3_active_config_missing"
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()

def test_production_dimension_contract_rejects_missing_and_invalid_definition() -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://", connect_args={"check_same_thread": False}, poolclass=StaticPool
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with pytest.raises(ProductionDimensionContractError) as missing:
            resolve_published_dimension_contract(
                db, schema_key="missing", version="v1"
            )
        assert missing.value.code == "dimension_contract_missing"
        assert (
            resolve_published_dimension_contract(
                db, schema_key=None, version=None
            )
            is None
        )

        schema = _dimension_schema(status="published", version="tampered-v1")
        schema.canonical_hash = "f" * 64
        db.add(schema)
        db.commit()
        with pytest.raises(ProductionDimensionContractError) as invalid:
            resolve_published_dimension_contract(
                db, schema_key=schema.schema_key, version=schema.version
            )
        assert invalid.value.code == "dimension_contract_invalid"

        invalid_definition = _dimension_schema(
            status="published", version=ACTIVE_V13_VERSION
        )
        invalid_definition.schema_key = SPACE_SCHEMA_KEY
        db.add(invalid_definition)
        db.commit()
        with pytest.raises(ProductionDimensionContractError) as not_executable:
            resolve_published_dimension_contract(
                db,
                schema_key=invalid_definition.schema_key,
                version=invalid_definition.version,
            )
        assert not_executable.value.code == "dimension_contract_not_executable"

        custom_definition = space_schema_definition_for_version(
            ACTIVE_V13_VERSION
        )
        custom_definition["package_version"] = "custom-v1"
        custom_schema = DimensionSchema(
            schema_key="test.custom-executable",
            version="v1",
            schema_type="family_pack",
            family_key="space",
            display_name="尚未接入 Bundle 的可执行维度",
            status="published",
            definition_json=canonical_json(custom_definition),
            canonical_hash=canonical_hash(custom_definition),
            created_by="test",
            published_by="test",
            published_at=worker.datetime.now(worker.timezone.utc),
        )
        db.add(custom_schema)
        db.commit()
        resolved_custom = resolve_published_dimension_contract(
            db,
            schema_key=custom_schema.schema_key,
            version=custom_schema.version,
        )
        assert resolved_custom is not None
        assert resolved_custom.canonical_hash == custom_schema.canonical_hash

        custom_bundle = SimpleNamespace(
            dimension_schema_set_snapshot=json.dumps(
                {
                    "schemas": [
                        {
                            "schema_key": custom_schema.schema_key,
                            "version": custom_schema.version,
                            "canonical_hash": custom_schema.canonical_hash,
                            "definition": custom_definition,
                        }
                    ]
                }
            )
        )
        resolved_from_bundle = resolve_published_dimension_contract(
            db,
            schema_key=custom_schema.schema_key,
            version=custom_schema.version,
            bundle=custom_bundle,
        )
        assert resolved_from_bundle is not None
        assert resolved_from_bundle.definition == custom_definition
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_explicit_category_dimension_overrides_model_scoring_profile_route() -> None:
    rows = materialized_space_schema_rows()
    bundle = SimpleNamespace(
        strategy_schema_version="strategy-bundle-v2",
        dimension_schema_set_snapshot=json.dumps(
            {
                "schemas": [
                    {
                        "schema_key": row["schema_key"],
                        "version": row["version"],
                        "canonical_hash": row["canonical_hash"],
                        "definition": json.loads(row["definition_json"]),
                    }
                    for row in rows
                ]
            }
        ),
    )
    routed = resolve_frozen_dimension_entry(
        bundle=bundle,
        aesthetic={"scoring_profile": "not-v1.3"},
        schema_key=SPACE_SCHEMA_KEY,
        version=ACTIVE_V13_VERSION,
    )
    assert routed["version"] == ACTIVE_V13_VERSION
    assert routed["version"] != HISTORICAL_DEFAULT_VERSION


def test_dimension_contract_error_preserves_worker_error_code() -> None:
    error = ProductionDimensionContractError(
        "dimension_contract_not_executable",
        "维度合同不可执行",
    )

    failure = worker._technical_failure_from_exception(error)

    assert failure.error_type == "dimension_contract_not_executable"
    assert failure.retryable is False

===== FILE backend/tests/test_jobs_listing_paging.py =====
"""The evaluation-progress list must reach past its first page.

It asked for ``/api/jobs?limit=200`` and rendered whatever came back, so on a
day with thousands of materials everything after the newest 200 was simply
invisible from the page. The list now pages, and reports how many there are in
total so an operator can see what they have not looked at yet.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.database import Base, get_db
from app.main import app, current_user
from app.models import Asset, EvaluationJob, User


@pytest.fixture
def client():
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://", connect_args={"check_same_thread": False}, poolclass=StaticPool)
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    user = User(username="tester", password_hash="unused", display_name="测试员")
    db.add(user)
    for index in range(1, 8):
        db.add(Asset(original_name=f"a{index}.jpg", stored_name=f"a{index}.jpg",
                     mime_type="image/jpeg", size_bytes=10, width=8, height=8,
                     sha256=f"{index:064d}"))
    db.flush()
    for index in range(1, 8):
        db.add(EvaluationJob(asset_id=index, category_key="space_image",
                             status="completed" if index % 2 else "queued",
                             stage="done", progress=100))
    db.commit()

    def _db():
        yield db

    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = _db
    app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: user
    try:
        yield TestClient(app)
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_a_page_reports_the_whole_size_not_just_what_it_returned(client):
    body = client.get("/api/jobs?limit=3").json()
    assert len(body["items"]) == 3
    assert body["total"] == 7 and body["offset"] == 0 and body["limit"] == 3
    assert body["has_more"] is True


def test_walking_the_pages_sees_every_job_exactly_once(client):
    seen, offset = [], 0
    while True:
        body = client.get(f"/api/jobs?limit=3&offset={offset}").json()
        seen.extend(item["id"] for item in body["items"])
        if not body["has_more"]:
            break
        offset += len(body["items"])
    assert sorted(seen) == list(range(1, 8)) and len(set(seen)) == 7


def test_every_row_carries_the_asset_so_the_list_can_show_a_thumbnail(client):
    for item in client.get("/api/jobs?limit=200").json()["items"]:
        assert isinstance(item["asset_id"], int) and item["asset_id"] > 0
        assert item["asset_name"]


def test_filtering_narrows_the_total_as_well_as_the_page(client):
    body = client.get("/api/jobs?status=queued&limit=200").json()
    assert body["total"] == 3 and len(body["items"]) == 3
    assert {item["status"] for item in body["items"]} == {"queued"}
    assert client.get("/api/jobs?category_key=nope&limit=200").json()["total"] == 0


def test_the_page_size_stays_bounded_however_large_a_limit_is_asked_for(client):
    assert client.get("/api/jobs?limit=100000").json()["limit"] == 200
    assert client.get("/api/jobs?limit=0").json()["limit"] == 1


def test_a_negative_offset_is_refused_rather_than_wrapping_around(client):
    assert client.get("/api/jobs?offset=-5").status_code == 422


def test_reading_past_the_end_is_an_empty_page_not_an_error(client):
    body = client.get("/api/jobs?limit=3&offset=99").json()
    assert body["items"] == [] and body["has_more"] is False and body["total"] == 7

===== FILE backend/tests/test_joint_model_workflow.py =====
from copy import deepcopy
import json
import pytest
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from app.joint_model_workflow import joint_output_contract, validate_joint_knowledge, create_joint_candidate, validate_joint_input
from app.knowledge_graph_contracts import knowledge_graph_fixture_catalog, resolve_knowledge_graph_fixture
from app.prompt_output_contract import parse_output_contract, validate_output_payload
from app.workflow_registry import persisted_workflow_manifest
from tests.test_model_3d_su_strict_release import _call_a_payload
from tests.test_strict_prompt_release import _prepare_database


@pytest.mark.parametrize('fixture,key', list(knowledge_graph_fixture_catalog('knowledge_graph').items()))
def test_four_route_joint_contract(fixture, key):
    fields = resolve_knowledge_graph_fixture(fixture, key, expected_asset_id='asset-1')
    wire = {'ll_id': '', 'res_id': '', 'title': '', **fields}
    payload = {**_call_a_payload(), 'knowledge_graph': {'contract_key': key, 'fields': wire}}
    validate_output_payload(parse_output_contract(joint_output_contract()), payload)
    assert validate_joint_knowledge(payload)['fields'] == fields
    bad = deepcopy(payload)
    bad['knowledge_graph']['fields'].pop(next(iter(fields)))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        validate_joint_knowledge(bad)


def test_new_category_candidate_preserves_source(tmp_path):
    from app.native_workflow_migration import ensure_native_category_workflows
    from app.model_3d_su_strict_release import attach_model_3d_su_strict_contracts
    from app.models import WorkflowVersion, WorkflowDefinition, CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding
    from app.workflow_working_copies import get_or_create_workflow_working_copy, editable_workflow_template
    from sqlalchemy import select
    engine = _prepare_database(tmp_path)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        ensure_native_category_workflows(db, 'model_3d_su', actor='test')
        definition = db.scalar(select(WorkflowDefinition).where(WorkflowDefinition.workflow_key=='model_3d_su.evaluation'))
        template = editable_workflow_template(db, definition.id)
        get_or_create_workflow_working_copy(db, workflow_definition_id=definition.id, initial_manifest=template.manifest, actor='test')
        receipt = attach_model_3d_su_strict_contracts(db, actor='test', apply=True)
        source = db.get(WorkflowVersion, receipt['workflow_version_id'])
        original = persisted_workflow_manifest(source)
        candidate = create_joint_candidate(db, source=source, actor='test')
        assert candidate.status == 'draft'
        assert create_joint_candidate(db, source=source, actor='test').id == candidate.id
        manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(candidate)
        assert manifest['category_key'] == 'model_3d_su_joint'
        assert original == persisted_workflow_manifest(source)
        a = next(n for n in manifest['nodes'] if n['node_instance_id'] == 'prompt_a')
        assert 'knowledge_graph' in a['config']['output_contract']['schema']['required']
        assert not db.scalar(select(CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding).where(CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding.category_key=='model_3d_su_joint'))


def test_joint_reducer_preserves_knowledge_on_short_circuit(monkeypatch):
    from app import workflow_result_finalizer as finalizer
    key = 'kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1'
    fields = resolve_knowledge_graph_fixture('kg.domestic.whole.v1', key, expected_asset_id='id-1')
    payload = {**_call_a_payload(), 'knowledge_graph': {'contract_key': key, 'fields': fields}}
    monkeypatch.setattr(finalizer, '_short_circuit_level_result', lambda *args: {'level':'L2','score':None})
    manifest = {'category_key':'model_3d_su_joint', 'nodes':[{'node_instance_id':'prompt_a','kind':'prompt','config':{}}]}
    result = finalizer.reduce_native_workflow_result(manifest, {'prompt_a':{'status':'succeeded','output':payload}}, model_execution_mode='simulated')
    assert result['level']=='L2'
    assert result['knowledge_graph']['fields']==fields
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        finalizer.reduce_native_workflow_result(manifest, {}, model_execution_mode='simulated')


def test_route_and_identity_must_match_frozen_input():
    key = 'kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1'
    fields = resolve_knowledge_graph_fixture('kg.domestic.whole.v1', key, expected_asset_id='id-1')
    payload = {'knowledge_graph': {'contract_key':key,'fields':fields}}
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        validate_joint_knowledge(payload, expected_input={'source':'overseas','is_single':1,'res_id':'id-1'})
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        validate_joint_knowledge(payload, expected_input={'source':'domestic','is_single':0,'ll_id':'id-2'})


@pytest.mark.parametrize('value', [{}, {'source':'domestic','is_single':True,'ll_id':'id'},
    {'source':'domestic','is_single':0,'ll_id':'id','res_id':'other'}])
def test_invalid_input_stops_before_model(value):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        validate_joint_input(value)


@pytest.mark.parametrize('short_circuit', [False, True, 'upstream'])
def test_joint_native_runtime_completes_with_both_results(tmp_path, monkeypatch, short_circuit):
    from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker
    from app.native_workflow_migration import ensure_native_category_workflows
    from app.model_3d_su_strict_release import attach_model_3d_su_strict_contracts
    from app.models import WorkflowVersion, WorkflowDefinition, ProductionRun, ProductionStepAttempt
    from app.workflow_working_copies import get_or_create_workflow_working_copy, editable_workflow_template
    from app.workflow_registry import create_workflow_version
    from app.workflow_runtime import ProductionRunRequest, create_production_run
    from app.workflow_node_executors import _deterministic_schema_value
    from sqlalchemy import select
    import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor
    engine = _prepare_database(tmp_path)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    with sessions() as db:
        ensure_native_category_workflows(db, 'model_3d_su', actor='test')
        definition = db.scalar(select(WorkflowDefinition).where(WorkflowDefinition.workflow_key=='model_3d_su.evaluation'))
        template = editable_workflow_template(db, definition.id)
        get_or_create_workflow_working_copy(db, workflow_definition_id=definition.id, initial_manifest=template.manifest, actor='test')
        if short_circuit == 'upstream':
            from app.model_3d_su_upstream_gate_release import attach_model_3d_su_upstream_gate
            attach_model_3d_su_upstream_gate(db, actor='test', apply=True)
        receipt = attach_model_3d_su_strict_contracts(db, actor='test', apply=True)
        source = db.get(WorkflowVersion, receipt['workflow_version_id'])
        # This isolated seed has no anchor files. Make the fixture's no-anchor
        # policy explicit in a NEW source version; never alter an existing version.
        sm = persisted_workflow_manifest(source)
        projection = sm['resource_policy']['legacy_projection_snapshot']
        projection['workflow_fields'].setdefault('contract', {}).setdefault('aesthetic_foundation', {})['use_anchors'] = False
        from app.workflow_result_contract import hash_manifest
        projection['workflow_fields_hash'] = hash_manifest(projection['workflow_fields'])
        source = create_workflow_version(db, definition=definition, version='fixture-no-anchors', manifest=sm, created_by='test')
        candidate = create_joint_candidate(db, source=source, actor='test')
        m = persisted_workflow_manifest(candidate)
        if short_circuit is True:
            a = next(n for n in m['nodes'] if n['node_instance_id']=='prompt_a')
            a['config']['short_circuit_rules'] = [{
                'rule_version':'short-circuit-rule-v1', 'rule_key':'fixture-direct-l2',
                'when':{'path':'output.classification.doc_group','op':'eq','value':'软装配景'},
                'target_level':'L2','priority':10,'skip_roles':[],
                'skip_nodes':[n['node_instance_id'] for n in m['nodes'] if n['node_instance_id'] not in {'upstream','prompt_a','downstream'}]}]
            candidate = create_workflow_version(db, definition=candidate.definition, version='fixture-short-circuit', manifest=m, created_by='test')
        key = 'kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1'
        fields = resolve_knowledge_graph_fixture('kg.domestic.whole.v1',key,expected_asset_id='joint-1')
        a_payload = {**_call_a_payload(), 'knowledge_graph':{'contract_key':key,'fields':{'res_id':'','title':'',**fields}}}
        outputs = {}
        for n in m['nodes']:
            if n['kind']=='prompt':
                outputs[n['config']['asset_key']] = a_payload if n['node_instance_id']=='prompt_a' else _deterministic_schema_value(n['config']['output_contract']['schema'])
                if n['node_instance_id']=='prompt_b':
                    b=outputs[n['config']['asset_key']]
                    b.update(aesthetic_score=62,aesthetic_evidence=['比例协调、材质细节一般'],aesthetic_confidence=.8)
                    for value in b['dimensions'].values():
                        value['hit_rules']=[]
                        value['evaluation']='可见细节清晰'
        candidate.status='active'  # Isolated fixture DB only; creation service keeps candidates draft.
        db.flush()
        run = create_production_run(db, ProductionRunRequest(
            workflow_version_id=candidate.id,idempotency_key='joint-test',category_key='model_3d_su_joint',queue_class='validation',
            runtime_context={'run_mode':'evaluation'},input_manifest={'source':'domestic','is_single':0,'ll_id':'joint-1',
                'material_type':'全景图' if short_circuit == 'upstream' else '家具',
                'workflow_fixture':{'schema_version':'native-prompt-fixtures-v1','prompt_outputs':outputs}},
            owner='test',reason='联合链路零费用验证',environment='dry_run'),actor='test')
        run_id=run.id
        db.commit()
    monkeypatch.setattr(executor,'SessionLocal',sessions)
    for _ in range(30):
        if not executor.process_runtime_step_once('joint-worker'):
            break
    with sessions() as db:
        run=db.get(ProductionRun,run_id)
        attempts=db.scalars(select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id==run_id)).all()
        assert run.status=='succeeded', [(a.step_key,a.status,a.last_error_code,a.last_error_message) for a in attempts]
        projection=json.loads(next(a for a in attempts if a.step_key=='downstream').output_manifest_json)['output']
        assert projection['normalized_result']['knowledge_graph']['fields']['ll_id']=='joint-1'
        from app.workflow_result_finalizer import reduce_native_workflow_result
        result=reduce_native_workflow_result(m,{a.step_key:json.loads(a.output_manifest_json) for a in attempts if a.output_manifest_json},model_execution_mode='simulated')
        assert result['knowledge_graph']['fields']['ll_id']=='joint-1'
        assert 'level' in result
        if short_circuit:
            assert next(a for a in attempts if a.step_key=='prompt_b').status=='skipped'
            assert result['level']=='L2'
            assert next(a for a in attempts if a.step_key=='prompt_a').status=='succeeded'


def test_upstream_short_circuit_cannot_skip_joint_a():

    manifest={'category_key':'model_3d_su_joint','nodes':[

        {'node_instance_id':'prompt_b','kind':'prompt','config':{'execution_role':'grading'}}],

===== FILE backend/tests/test_json_extra_closer.py =====
import pytest

from app.doubao import DoubaoParseError, parse_json_text


def test_one_redundant_object_closer_preserves_object_and_records_repair():
    repairs = []
    assert parse_json_text('{"score":80,"evidence":"keep } inside text"}}', repairs=repairs) == {
        "score": 80, "evidence": "keep } inside text"}
    assert repairs == ["$:drop_redundant_object_closer"]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("prefix", ['', 'JSON result:\n'])
@pytest.mark.parametrize("suffix", ['{}', '{"score":20}', '[]', ',"other":1}', '}}'])
def test_extra_json_data_is_not_silently_discarded(prefix, suffix):
    with pytest.raises(DoubaoParseError):
        parse_json_text(prefix + '{"score":80}' + suffix, repairs=[])


def test_extra_data_error_is_not_misreported_as_token_truncation():
    with pytest.raises(DoubaoParseError) as error:
        parse_json_text('{"score":80}{}')
    assert error.value.technical_error_type == "transient_parse"
    assert error.value.evidence["json_parse"] == {"reason": "Extra data", "offset": 12,
        "document_chars": 14, "single_redundant_closer": False}


@pytest.mark.parametrize("score,accepted", [('80', True), ('"wrong"', False)])
def test_client_retains_original_response_and_still_validates_schema(score, accepted):
    import asyncio
    from test_doubao_multi_image_text import _client
    from app.doubao import DoubaoStructuredOutputError
    client = _client()
    original = '{"score":' + score + '}}'
    async def post(_payload):
        return {"choices": [{"message": {"content": original}, "finish_reason": "stop"}],
                "usage": {"prompt_tokens": 100, "completion_tokens": 10, "total_tokens": 110}}
    client._post = post
    contract = {"mode": "json_schema", "name": "score", "strict": True,
        "schema": {"type": "object", "properties": {"score": {"type": "integer"}},
                   "required": ["score"], "additionalProperties": False}}
    operation = client.chat_json("system", "user", max_attempts=1, output_contract=contract)
    if not accepted:
        with pytest.raises(DoubaoStructuredOutputError):
            asyncio.run(operation)
    else:
        response = asyncio.run(operation)
        assert response.parsed == {"score": 80}
        assert response.raw_text == original
        assert response.raw_payload["choices"][0]["message"]["content"] == original
        assert response.coercions == ("$:drop_redundant_object_closer",)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_judgement_correction_nodes.py =====
"""硬缺陷与调用B美感分必须是可纠偏节点。

这两项都是模型判断、都真实决定分数，但此前没有可提交的合同节点：
- 硬缺陷会压分或封顶（`hard_defects` -> `_apply_hard_defect_penalty`）；
- 美感分是等级撮合器的初始分（`aesthetic_score` -> `initial_score`）。

运营看到不同意的判断却改不了，只能绕道改别的字段，纠偏理由也就归错了因。
这批用例锁住三件事：节点存在且能过合同校验、写入后分数确定性重算、非法输入
fail-closed 被拒。

顺带钉住一个容易回退的实现细节：美感基座要求非空可见证据，人工纠偏时必须把
纠偏理由写成新分数的证据，否则重放会直接失败。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import itertools
import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.category_evaluation_aggregator import aggregate_category_evaluation
from app.correction_contract import (
    assert_correction_contract_complete,
    freeze_contract_from_execution_snapshot,
    validate_correction_contract,
    validate_node_value,
)
from app.database import Base
from app.dimension_deduction_bridge import (
    compose_rule_deductions,
    empty_deduction_output,
)
from app.inspiration_category_seed import (
    build_inspiration_classification_map,
    build_inspiration_subcategory_dimensions,
    build_inspiration_v3_contract,
)
from app.models import EvaluationResult
from app.node_correction_api import CorrectNodeRequest, apply_node_correction
from app.worker_v3_authoritative import build_v3_authoritative_scoring

MODEL_AESTHETIC_SCORE = 86
_job_ids = itertools.count(1)


def _v3_bundle() -> dict:
    return {
        "contract": build_inspiration_v3_contract(),
        "classification_map": build_inspiration_classification_map(),
        "subcategory_dimensions": build_inspiration_subcategory_dimensions(),
        "config_revision": 3,
    }


def _precheck() -> dict:
    return {
        "classification": {
            "scope_status": "in_scope",
            "primary_category": "建筑设计",
            "primary_confidence": 0.96,
        },
        "production_fields": {"reason": [], "trait": "实景照片"},
        "hard_defects": [],
    }


def _dimension_output(config: dict) -> dict:
    output = empty_deduction_output(config)
    output.update(
        {
            "aesthetic_score": MODEL_AESTHETIC_SCORE,
            "aesthetic_evidence": ["构图均衡，主体清晰"],
            "aesthetic_confidence": 0.9,
            "raw_payload": {"provider": "fake"},
        }
    )
    output.pop("warning", None)
    return output


def _contract() -> dict:
    v3 = _v3_bundle()
    return freeze_contract_from_execution_snapshot(
        category_key="inspiration_image",
        execution_snapshot={
            "rubric_version": "r1",
            "v3_authoritative_bundle": v3,
            "pipeline_config": {"production_fields": {}},
            "dimension_contract": {"definition": {}},
        },
    )


def _nodes_by_key(contract: dict) -> dict[str, dict]:
    return {str(node["node_key"]): node for node in contract["nodes"]}


@pytest.fixture()
def stored_result():
    """一条已落库的正常评测：86 分 / L2，带完整调用B输出。"""
    v3 = _v3_bundle()
    config = v3["subcategory_dimensions"]["class_one"]
    precheck = _precheck()
    dimension_output = _dimension_output(config)
    composed = compose_rule_deductions(
        config=config, dimension_output=dimension_output, require_foundation=True
    )
    aggregate = aggregate_category_evaluation(
        v3["contract"],
        precheck,
        composed,
        track_key="class_one",
        initial_score=composed.get("aesthetic_score"),
    )
    scoring = build_v3_authoritative_scoring(aggregate, precheck=precheck)
    scoring.pop("_dimension_deduction_raw_payload", None)
    scoring["v3_context"] = v3

    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://", connect_args={"check_same_thread": False}, poolclass=StaticPool
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    with sessions() as db:
        row = EvaluationResult(
            asset_id=1,
            job_id=next(_job_ids),
            precheck_json=json.dumps(precheck, ensure_ascii=False),
            aesthetic_json=json.dumps(dimension_output, ensure_ascii=False),
            scoring_json=json.dumps(scoring, ensure_ascii=False),
            correction_history_json="[]",
            raw_response_a="{}",
            score=scoring.get("score"),
            level=scoring.get("level"),
            confidence=scoring.get("confidence"),
            needs_review=False,
            model_id="fake",
            prompt_a_version="a",
            rubric_version="r",
            engine_version="e",
        )
        db.add(row)
        db.commit()
        yield db, row


def _current_aesthetic_score(row: EvaluationResult) -> int | None:
    return json.loads(row.aesthetic_json).get("aesthetic_score")


def test_contract_exposes_hard_defect_and_aesthetic_nodes() -> None:
    contract = _contract()
    nodes = _nodes_by_key(contract)

    assert not validate_correction_contract(contract)
    assert_correction_contract_complete(contract)

    hard_defects = nodes["call_a.hard_defects"]
    assert hard_defects["layer"] == "A", "硬缺陷是调用A的判断"
    assert hard_defects["path"] == "precheck.hard_defects"
    assert hard_defects["metadata"]["node_type"] == "precheck_field"

    aesthetic = nodes["call_b.aesthetic_score"]
    assert aesthetic["layer"] == "B", "美感分是调用B的判断"
    assert aesthetic["metadata"]["node_type"] == "aesthetic_score"
    assert (aesthetic["minimum"], aesthetic["maximum"]) == (0, 100)


def test_hard_defect_options_come_from_frozen_contract() -> None:
    """选项必须取自冻结合同，机制增删缺陷时前端无需改代码。"""
    contract = _contract()
    node = _nodes_by_key(contract)["call_a.hard_defects"]

    options = node["options"]
    labels = node["metadata"]["option_labels"]
    assert "blurry_grayish" in options
    assert labels["blurry_grayish"], "每个缺陷都要有中文说明供运营识别"
    assert "subject_obscuring_watermark" not in options, (
        "水印属于 image_defects，不应混进硬缺陷选项"
    )
    assert set(labels) == set(options)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("value", "accepted"),
    [(0, True), (60, True), (100, True), (200, False), (-1, False), ("60", False)],
)
def test_aesthetic_score_node_value_bounds(value: object, accepted: bool) -> None:
    node = _nodes_by_key(_contract())["call_b.aesthetic_score"]
    if accepted:
        validate_node_value(node, value)
        return
    with pytest.raises(Exception):
        validate_node_value(node, value)


def test_correcting_aesthetic_score_recomputes_downstream(stored_result) -> None:
    db, row = stored_result
    assert row.score == MODEL_AESTHETIC_SCORE

    apply_node_correction(
        db,
        result=row,
        payload=CorrectNodeRequest(
            correction_key="aesthetic-1",
            node_type="aesthetic_score",
            node_path="aesthetic.aesthetic_score",
            old_value=MODEL_AESTHETIC_SCORE,
            new_value=60,
            evidence=[],
            reason="人工判定构图松散，美感分应为60",
        ),
        corrector="operator",
    )
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(row)

    assert row.score == 60, "美感分是撮合器初始分，改了必须带动最终分数"
    assert row.level == "L3"

    stored = json.loads(row.aesthetic_json)
    assert stored["aesthetic_score"] == 60
    assert stored["manual_aesthetic_score"] is True
    assert stored["aesthetic_confidence"] == 1.0, "人工真值不应沿用模型置信度"
    assert stored["aesthetic_evidence"][0] == "人工判定构图松散，美感分应为60", (
        "美感基座要求非空证据，人工理由必须写成新分数的证据，否则重放会失败"
    )
    assert "构图均衡，主体清晰" in stored["aesthetic_evidence"], "模型原证据要保留供对照"


def test_correcting_hard_defects_caps_the_score(stored_result) -> None:
    db, row = stored_result
    before = row.score

    apply_node_correction(
        db,
        result=row,
        payload=CorrectNodeRequest(
            correction_key="defect-1",
            node_type="precheck_field",
            node_path="precheck.hard_defects",
            old_value=[],
            new_value=["blurry_grayish"],
            evidence=[],
            reason="画面整体发灰，暗部层次丢失",
        ),
        corrector="operator",
    )
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(row)

    assert row.score < before, "新增硬缺陷必须压分"
    assert json.loads(row.precheck_json)["hard_defects"] == ["blurry_grayish"]


def test_aesthetic_score_can_be_corrected_repeatedly_with_full_history(
    stored_result,
) -> None:
    """运营可以反复改同一项；每次都留痕，且需带上真实当前值。"""
    db, row = stored_result

    for value in (60, 75, 92):
        apply_node_correction(
            db,
            result=row,
            payload=CorrectNodeRequest(
                correction_key=None,
                node_type="aesthetic_score",
                node_path="aesthetic.aesthetic_score",
                old_value=_current_aesthetic_score(row),
                new_value=value,
                evidence=[],
                reason=f"人工判定美感分为 {value}",
            ),
            corrector="operator",
        )
        db.commit()
        db.refresh(row)
        assert row.score == value

    history = json.loads(row.correction_history_json)
    assert len(history) == 3, "每次纠偏都必须追加可审计历史"
    assert row.level == "L1"


def test_stale_aesthetic_value_is_rejected(stored_result) -> None:
    """乐观并发校验：拿着过期的当前值提交必须被拒，避免覆盖他人纠偏。"""
    db, row = stored_result

    with pytest.raises(Exception) as excinfo:
        apply_node_correction(
            db,
            result=row,
            payload=CorrectNodeRequest(
                correction_key=None,
                node_type="aesthetic_score",
                node_path="aesthetic.aesthetic_score",
                old_value=11,  # 与实际存储的 86 不一致
                new_value=60,
                evidence=[],
                reason="拿着过期值提交",
            ),
            corrector="operator",
        )
    assert "node_value_conflict" in str(getattr(excinfo.value, "detail", excinfo.value))


@pytest.mark.parametrize("bad_value", [200, -1, "60", True, 60.5])
def test_invalid_aesthetic_score_fails_closed(stored_result, bad_value) -> None:
    db, row = stored_result

    with pytest.raises(Exception) as excinfo:
        apply_node_correction(
            db,

                node_type="aesthetic_score",
                node_path="aesthetic.aesthetic_score",

                new_value=bad_value,
                evidence=[],
                reason="非法输入",
            ),
            corrector="operator",
        )
    assert "aesthetic_score_invalid" in str(
        getattr(excinfo.value, "detail", excinfo.value)
    )

def test_final_level_node_generated_from_level_scale() -> None:
    """新合同用 level_scale（旧 level_thresholds 为 null）时也必须给出等级节点。

    只认旧字段会让「最终等级」那一行没有纠偏按钮——运营反馈的
    「纠偏改不了等级」正是这个根因。
    """
    from app.correction_contract import _v3_nodes

    bundle = {
        "contract": {
            "spec_version": "1",
            "level_thresholds": None,
            "level_scale": {
                "version": "category-level-scale-v1",
                "levels": [
                    {"level": "L1", "min_score": 90, "enabled": True},
                    {"level": "L2", "min_score": 75, "enabled": True},
                    {"level": "L3", "min_score": 61, "enabled": True},
                    {"level": "L4", "min_score": 50, "enabled": True},
                    {"level": "L5", "min_score": 0, "enabled": True},
                ],
            },
        }
    }
    by_type = {
        (node.get("metadata") or {}).get("node_type"): node
        for node in _v3_nodes(bundle)
    }
    final_level = by_type.get("final_level")
    assert final_level is not None, "level_scale 合同必须生成最终等级纠偏节点"
    assert final_level["node_key"] == "v3.final_level"
    assert final_level["path"] == "scoring.level"
    assert final_level["options"] == ["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"]
    assert by_type.get("v3_thresholds") is not None, "阈值只读节点同样要给出"


def test_final_level_node_skips_disabled_levels() -> None:
    """关闭的档位不进选项，避免运营纠偏到一个机制已停用的等级。"""
    from app.correction_contract import _v3_nodes

    bundle = {
        "contract": {
            "spec_version": "1",
            "level_scale": {
                "version": "category-level-scale-v1",
                "levels": [
                    {"level": "L1", "min_score": 90, "enabled": True},
                    {"level": "L2", "min_score": 75, "enabled": False},
                    {"level": "L3", "min_score": 0, "enabled": True},
                ],
            },
        }
    }
    final_level = next(
        node
        for node in _v3_nodes(bundle)
        if (node.get("metadata") or {}).get("node_type") == "final_level"
    )
    assert final_level["options"] == ["L1", "L3"]

===== FILE backend/tests/test_kg_angle_contract.py =====
from copy import deepcopy

import pytest

from app.knowledge_graph_contracts import (
    KnowledgeGraphContractError,
    adapt_knowledge_graph_model_output,
    get_knowledge_graph_contract,
    resolve_knowledge_graph_fixture,
    validate_knowledge_graph_output,
)


@pytest.mark.parametrize('site', ['domestic', 'overseas'])
@pytest.mark.parametrize('scope', ['whole', 'single'])
def test_angle_survives_v2_validation_and_semantic_mapping(site, scope):
    key = f'kg.knowledge_graph.{site}.{scope}.v2'
    contract = get_knowledge_graph_contract(key)
    assert contract is not None
    assert 'view_angle' in contract.output_schema['properties']
    payload = resolve_knowledge_graph_fixture(
        f'kg.{site}.{scope}.v1', key, expected_asset_id='42')
    payload['view_angle'] = '正视' if site == 'domestic' else 'front view'
    result = adapt_knowledge_graph_model_output(
        key, payload, expected_asset_id='42', evidence_prefix='test')
    assert result['raw_output']['view_angle'] == payload['view_angle']
    assert result['semantic']['view_angle'][0]['value'] == payload['view_angle']
    old = get_knowledge_graph_contract(key.replace('.v2', '.v1'))
    assert 'view_angle' not in old.field_order
    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeGraphContractError):
        validate_knowledge_graph_output(old.contract_key, payload, expected_asset_id='42')
    payload['view_angle'] = 'invalid-angle'
    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeGraphContractError):
        validate_knowledge_graph_output(key, payload, expected_asset_id='42')


def test_explicit_extra_field_is_rejected_before_freezing_old_contract():
    from app.workflow_v3 import _validate_knowledge_graph_bindings
    key = 'kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1'
    schema = deepcopy(get_knowledge_graph_contract(key).output_schema)
    schema['properties']['view_angle'] = {'type': 'string'}
    schema['required'].append('view_angle')
    errors = []
    _validate_knowledge_graph_bindings(errors, [{
        'kind': 'prompt', 'node_instance_id': 'prompt_domestic_whole',
        'config': {'output_contract_key': key, 'output_contract': {
            'mode': 'json_schema', 'name': 'kg', 'strict': True, 'schema': schema}},
    }])
    assert any(e.code == 'workflow_kg_explicit_schema_mismatch' for e in errors)


def test_export_keeps_new_model_field_when_reference_is_v1():
    from app.baseline_export import build_rows, export_columns
    old = 'kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1'
    new = old.replace('.v1', '.v2')
    columns = export_columns([old, new])
    rows, _ = build_rows([{
        'truth_contract_key': old, 'output_contract_key': new,
        'normalized_result': {'ll_id': '42', 'view_angle': '正视'},
        'expected_result': {'ll_id': '42'},
    }], contract_keys=[old, new])
    assert rows[0][columns.index('视角（模型）')] == '正视'
    assert rows[0][columns.index('视角（真值）')] == ''
