def test_v2_simulated_node_uses_same_route_without_fabricating_angle():
    from app.workflow_node_executors import _knowledge_graph_output
    key = 'kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v2'
    output = _knowledge_graph_output(get_knowledge_graph_contract(key).snapshot(), {
        'source': 'domestic', 'is_single': 0, 'll_id': '42',
        'fixture_key': 'kg.domestic.whole.v1',
    }, category_key='knowledge_graph')
    assert output['view_angle'] == ''


def test_old_frozen_mechanism_with_conflicting_schema_cannot_start_new_regression():
    from app.knowledge_graph_contracts import knowledge_graph_route_issues
    key = 'kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1'
    contract = get_knowledge_graph_contract(key)
    schema = contract.output_schema
    schema['properties']['view_angle'] = {'type': 'string'}
    issues = knowledge_graph_route_issues({'nodes': [{
        'kind': 'prompt', 'node_instance_id': 'whole', 'config': {
            'output_contract_key': key, 'output_contract_snapshot': contract.snapshot(),
            'output_contract': {'mode': 'json_schema', 'schema': schema},
        },
    }]})
    assert any('结构化输出' in issue for issue in issues)


def test_freeze_materializes_provider_schema_without_mutating_draft(tmp_path):
    from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
    from app.models import ModelConfig, ModelRegistryEntry
    from app.workflow_v3 import freeze_component_references
    from test_workflow_working_copies import _engine, _seed_managed_dependencies
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            _, draft = _seed_managed_dependencies(db)
            prompt = next(n for n in draft['nodes'] if n['kind'] == 'prompt')
            key = 'kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v2'
            prompt['config']['output_contract_key'] = key
            model = db.get(ModelRegistryEntry, prompt['config']['model_registry_id'])
            model.protocol = 'openai_chat'
            db.get(ModelConfig, model.source_model_config_id).protocol = 'openai_chat'
            frozen = freeze_component_references(db, draft)
            node = next(n for n in frozen['nodes'] if n['kind'] == 'prompt')
            assert node['config']['output_contract']['schema'] == node['output_schema']
            assert node['output_schema'] == node['config']['output_contract_snapshot']['output_schema']
            assert 'view_angle' in node['output_schema']['required']
            assert 'output_contract' not in prompt['config']
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_kg_qwen_format_candidate.py =====
from copy import deepcopy
import pytest
from scripts.kg_qwen_format_candidate import constrained_contract
from app.knowledge_graph_contracts import knowledge_graph_strict_output_contract
from app.prompt_output_contract import parse_output_contract, validate_output_payload, PromptOutputContractError
from app.knowledge_graph_contracts import validate_knowledge_graph_output, KnowledgeGraphContractError


def config(scope):
    key = f'kg.knowledge_graph.overseas.{scope}.v1'
    return {'output_contract_key':key, 'output_contract':knowledge_graph_strict_output_contract(key)}


def output(contract):
    result = {k:'' for k in contract['schema']['properties']}
    result.update(res_id='asset-42', is_single=contract['schema']['properties']['is_single']['enum'][0])
    return result


@pytest.mark.parametrize('field,value,scope', [
    ('object','display stand_0.7,seating_0.5,poster_0.3,table_0.1','whole'),
    ('soft_decoration','rug,curtain,cushion,painting','whole'),
    ('object','chair,table','single'),
])
def test_run406_invalid_values_are_rejected_before_semantic_adapter(field,value,scope):
    old=config(scope); new=constrained_contract(old); payload=output(new);payload[field]=value
    validate_output_payload(parse_output_contract(old['output_contract']),payload)
    with pytest.raises(PromptOutputContractError):
        validate_output_payload(parse_output_contract(new),payload)


@pytest.mark.parametrize('scope,value', [('whole',''),('whole','display stand_0.7'),('whole','sofa_0.7,coffee table_0.5,floor lamp_0.3'),('single','coffee table')])
def test_valid_values_and_title_commas_remain_supported_without_mutation(scope,value):
    original=config(scope); copy=deepcopy(original); new=constrained_contract(original)
    payload=output(new);payload.update(object=value,title='Living Room, With Sofa',material='wood,fabric,metal')
    validate_output_payload(parse_output_contract(new),payload)
    assert original==copy
    assert set(new['schema']['properties'])==set(copy['output_contract']['schema']['properties'])


@pytest.mark.parametrize('site,scope', [(s,a) for s in ('domestic','overseas') for a in ('whole','single')])
def test_final_candidate_limits_all_routes_and_keeps_native_weight_validation(site,scope):
    key=f'kg.knowledge_graph.{site}.{scope}.v1'
    source={'output_contract_key':key,'output_contract':knowledge_graph_strict_output_contract(key)}
    original=deepcopy(source)
    result=constrained_contract(source)
    contract=parse_output_contract(result)
    payload={k:'' for k in result['schema']['properties']}
    identity='ll_id' if site=='domestic' else 'res_id'
    payload.update({identity:'42','is_single':result['schema']['properties']['is_single']['enum'][0]})
    payload['object']='沙发_0.7' if scope=='whole' else '沙发'
    payload['architectural_element']='门,窗,柱'
    validate_output_payload(contract,payload)
    validate_knowledge_graph_output(key,payload,expected_asset_id='42')
    payload['architectural_element']='门,窗,柱,楼梯'
    with pytest.raises(PromptOutputContractError):
        validate_output_payload(contract,payload)
    payload['architectural_element']=''
    payload['object']='沙发' if scope=='whole' else '沙发_0.7'
    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeGraphContractError):
        validate_knowledge_graph_output(key,payload,expected_asset_id='42')
    assert source==original

===== FILE backend/tests/test_kg_route_field_correction.py =====
"""Operators can finish human labels after changing whole/single routing."""
import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker

from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.database import Base
from app.knowledge_graph_contracts import get_knowledge_graph_contract
from app.models import Asset, BaselineSet, BaselineSetItem, BaselineRegressionRun


@pytest.mark.parametrize("site,scope", [(site, scope) for site in ("domestic", "overseas") for scope in ("whole", "single")])
def test_route_then_fields_preserves_frozen_results_and_uses_new_truth(monkeypatch, tmp_path, site, scope):
    from app.seed import seed_defaults
    from app.native_workflow_migration import ensure_knowledge_graph_workflow
    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run
    from app.correction_view import build_correction_view, submit_correction_nodes
    from app.baseline_truth import resolve_effective_baseline_truth
    from app.workflow_correction import analyze_workflow_corrections
    import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'route.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    monkeypatch.setattr(executor, "SessionLocal", sessions)
    old = get_knowledge_graph_contract(f"kg.knowledge_graph.{site}.{scope}.v1")
    new = get_knowledge_graph_contract(f"kg.knowledge_graph.{site}.{'single' if scope == 'whole' else 'whole'}.v1")
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            seed_defaults(db)
            workflow = ensure_knowledge_graph_workflow(db, actor="test", category_key="knowledge_graph")
            asset = Asset(original_name="route.jpg", stored_name="route.jpg", mime_type="image/jpeg", size_bytes=100, sha256="a" * 64, category_key="knowledge_graph")
            baseline = BaselineSet(category_key="knowledge_graph", name="route test", description="", default_expected_level="L1", fingerprint="b" * 64, created_by="test")
            db.add_all([asset, baseline]); db.flush()
            truth = {field: "" for field in old.field_order}
            truth.update({old.identity_field: "asset-route", "is_single": old.is_single})
            source = BaselineSetItem(baseline_set_id=baseline.id, asset_id=asset.id, expected_level=None, expected_result_json=canonical_json(truth), truth_contract_key=old.contract_key,
                asset_snapshot_json=canonical_json({"asset_id": asset.id, "sha256": asset.sha256, "workflow_input": {"source": site, "is_single": old.is_single, old.identity_field: "asset-route", "fixture_key": f"kg.{site}.{scope}.v1"}}))
            db.add(source); db.flush()
            run = create_native_baseline_run(db, baseline_set=baseline, workflow_version=workflow, actor="test", idempotency_key="original")
            run_id, workflow_id = run.id, workflow.id
            db.commit()
        while executor.process_runtime_step_once("route-test"):
            pass
        with sessions() as db:
            run = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, run_id)
            assert run.status == "completed"
            item = run.items[0]
            view = build_correction_view(db, run=run, item=item)
            prior = int(scope == "whole")
            if prior:
                view = submit_correction_nodes(db, run=run, item=item, contract_hash=view["contract"]["contract_hash"], review_revision=0,
                    idempotency_key="old-whole-style", actor="operator",
                    nodes=[{"node_key": f"workflow.prompt_{site}_{scope}.style", "human_value": "modern,classic", "reason": "原整体两种风格", "evidence": [{"text": "test"}]}])
            frozen = (run.execution_snapshot_json, item.result_snapshot_json, item.evaluation.normalized_result_json)
            requests = []
            for field, value in [("is_single", new.is_single), ("color", "gray"), ("style", "custom-style")]:
                node_key = f"workflow.prompt_{site}_{scope}.{field}"
                request = dict(run=run, item=item, contract_hash=view["contract"]["contract_hash"], review_revision=view["review_revision"], idempotency_key=f"save-{field}", actor="operator",
                    nodes=[{"node_key": node_key, "human_value": value, "reason": "人工看图核对", "evidence": [{"text": "核对画面"}]}])
                requests.append(request)
                view = submit_correction_nodes(db, **request)
                assert view["review_revision"] == prior + len(requests)
                assert view["effective_truth"]["expected_result"][field] == value
                if field == "is_single":
                    color_node = next(node for node in view["nodes"] if node["node_key"] == f"workflow.prompt_{site}_{scope}.color")
                    assert color_node["current_value"] == ""
                    assert "human_value" not in color_node
                    if prior:
                        style_node = next(node for node in view["nodes"] if node["node_key"] == f"workflow.prompt_{site}_{scope}.style")
                        assert style_node["current_value"] == ""
                        assert "human_value" not in style_node  # Old human multi-style was not transferable.
            replay = submit_correction_nodes(db, **requests[-1])
            assert replay["idempotent_replay"] is True
            assert replay["truth_revision"] == prior + 3
            assert replay["route_field_confirmation"]["fields"]
            from app.correction_view import CorrectionViewError
            for invalid_field, invalid_value in [("color", ["gray"]), ("object", "chair_0.7" if new.asset_scope == "single" else "chair")]:
                bad = dict(requests[-1])
                bad.update(idempotency_key=f"invalid-{invalid_field}", review_revision=prior + 3,
                    nodes=[{"node_key": f"workflow.prompt_{site}_{scope}.{invalid_field}", "human_value": invalid_value, "reason": "无效格式", "evidence": [{"text": "test"}]}])
                with pytest.raises(CorrectionViewError):
                    submit_correction_nodes(db, **bad)
                assert resolve_effective_baseline_truth(db, item.baseline_set_item).revision == prior + 3
            stale = dict(requests[-1], idempotency_key="stale", review_revision=0)
            with pytest.raises(CorrectionViewError) as conflict:
                submit_correction_nodes(db, **stale)
            assert conflict.value.code == "CORRECTION_REVIEW_STALE"
            assert (run.execution_snapshot_json, item.result_snapshot_json, item.evaluation.normalized_result_json) == frozen
            effective = resolve_effective_baseline_truth(db, item.baseline_set_item)
            assert effective.truth_contract_key == new.contract_key
            assert effective.expected_result["color"] == "gray"
            assert effective.expected_result["style"] == "custom-style"
            history = json.loads(item.evaluation.correction_history_json)
            assert history[-1]["route_field_confirmation"]["to_contract_key"] == new.contract_key
            analysis = analyze_workflow_corrections(db, run.id, [item.id])
            assert not any(sample["new_value"] in ("gray", "custom-style") for group in analysis.groups for sample in group.samples)
            assert "human_truth_route_requires_regression" in analysis.blockers
            from app.models import WorkflowVersion
            later = create_native_baseline_run(db, baseline_set=item.baseline_set_item.baseline_set, workflow_version=db.get(WorkflowVersion, workflow_id), actor="test", idempotency_key="later")
            frozen_truth = json.loads(later.execution_snapshot_json)["truth_snapshot"][0]
            assert frozen_truth["truth_revision"] == prior + 3
            assert frozen_truth["truth_contract_key"] == new.contract_key
            assert frozen_truth["expected_result"]["color"] == "gray"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_kg_schema_extension.py =====
from copy import deepcopy

import pytest

from app.knowledge_graph_contracts import (
    KnowledgeGraphContractError, extend_knowledge_graph_contract,
    get_knowledge_graph_contract, resolve_knowledge_graph_fixture,
    validate_knowledge_graph_output,
)

KEY = "kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1"


def extended():
    schema = get_knowledge_graph_contract(KEY).output_schema
    schema["properties"]["review_required"] = {"type": "boolean"}
    schema["required"].append("review_required")
    return extend_knowledge_graph_contract(KEY, schema)


def test_frozen_fields_are_independent_and_detect_tampering():
    base = get_knowledge_graph_contract(KEY)
    contract = extended()
    frozen = contract.snapshot()
    assert get_knowledge_graph_contract(KEY, frozen) == contract
    assert get_knowledge_graph_contract(KEY) == base
    frozen["output_schema"]["properties"]["review_required"]["type"] = "string"
    assert get_knowledge_graph_contract(KEY, frozen) is None
    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeGraphContractError):
        validate_knowledge_graph_output(KEY, {}, expected_asset_id="42", snapshot=frozen)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("field", [
    {"type": "string", "$ref": "https://example.com/schema"},
    {"type": "boolean", "enum": ["yes"]},
    {"type": "object", "properties": {}, "additionalProperties": True},
    {"type": "array"},
    {"type": "string", "pattern": "(a+)+"},
])
def test_unsupported_schema_is_rejected_before_execution(field):
    schema = get_knowledge_graph_contract(KEY).output_schema
    schema["properties"]["extra"] = field
    schema["required"].append("extra")
    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeGraphContractError):
        extend_knowledge_graph_contract(KEY, schema)


def test_frozen_new_fields_are_validated_and_exported_without_fake_truth():
    from app.baseline_export import build_rows, export_columns
    contract = extended()
    result = resolve_knowledge_graph_fixture("kg.domestic.whole.v1", KEY, expected_asset_id="42", snapshot=contract.snapshot())
    assert result["review_required"] is False
    invalid = {**result, "review_required": "false"}
    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeGraphContractError):
        validate_knowledge_graph_output(KEY, invalid, expected_asset_id="42", snapshot=contract.snapshot())
    columns = export_columns([KEY], contract.field_order)
    rows, _ = build_rows([{"truth_contract_key": KEY, "output_contract_key": KEY,
                          "output_contract_snapshot": contract.snapshot(), "normalized_result": result,
                          "expected_result": {}}], contract_keys=[KEY])
    assert len(rows[0]) == len(columns)
    assert rows[0][columns.index("review_required（模型）")] == "False"
    assert rows[0][columns.index("review_required（真值）")] == ""


def test_projection_must_match_exact_schema_hash():
    from app.workflow_v3 import _validate_knowledge_graph_bindings
    base = get_knowledge_graph_contract(KEY)
    new = extended()
    errors = []
    _validate_knowledge_graph_bindings(errors, [
        {"kind": "prompt", "config": {"output_contract_key": KEY, "output_contract_snapshot": new.snapshot()}, "output_schema": new.output_schema},
        {"kind": "downstream_projection", "config": {"field_contract_key": KEY, "field_contract_snapshot": base.snapshot()}},
    ])
    assert any(e.code == "workflow_kg_contract_mismatch" for e in errors)

from __future__ import annotations

from copy import deepcopy

import pytest


DOMESTIC_WHOLE = "kg.model_3d_su.domestic.whole.v1"


def _domestic_whole_output() -> dict[str, object]:
    return {
        "ll_id": "asset-42",
        "is_single": 0,
        "space": "客厅",
        "object": "沙发_0.7,茶几_0.5",
        "style": "现代简约,自然主义",
        "material": "木材,织物",
        "structural_features": "",
        "architectural_element": "柱,门洞",
        "soft_decoration": "地毯,抱枕",
        "hard_decoration": "护墙板",
        "color": "米白色",
        "form": "几何,方正",
        "structure": "组合,开放",
    }


def test_catalog_registers_the_four_jiaxin_prompt_output_shapes() -> None:
    from app.knowledge_graph_contracts import (
        KNOWLEDGE_GRAPH_SEMANTIC_FIELDS,
        knowledge_graph_contract_catalog,
    )

    catalog = knowledge_graph_contract_catalog()

    assert tuple(catalog) == (
        "kg.model_3d_su.domestic.whole.v1",
        "kg.model_3d_su.domestic.single.v1",
        "kg.model_3d_su.overseas.whole.v1",
        "kg.model_3d_su.overseas.single.v1",
    )
    assert KNOWLEDGE_GRAPH_SEMANTIC_FIELDS == (
        "space",
        "object",
        "style",
        "material",
        "structural_features",
        "architectural_element",
        "soft_decoration",
        "hard_decoration",
        "color",
        "form",
        "structure",
    )

    domestic = catalog["kg.model_3d_su.domestic.whole.v1"]
    overseas = catalog["kg.model_3d_su.overseas.single.v1"]
    assert domestic.field_order == (
        "ll_id",
        "is_single",
        *KNOWLEDGE_GRAPH_SEMANTIC_FIELDS,
    )
    assert overseas.field_order == (
        "res_id",
        "is_single",
        "title",
        *KNOWLEDGE_GRAPH_SEMANTIC_FIELDS,
    )
    assert domestic.output_schema["required"] == list(domestic.field_order)
    assert domestic.output_schema["additionalProperties"] is False
    assert domestic.output_schema["properties"]["is_single"] == {
        "type": "integer",
        "enum": [0],
        "title": "是否单体",
        "description": "国内整体链路固定为 0",
    }
    # 海外编码：整体=1、单体=2（国内 0/1）；这是海外单体合同
    assert overseas.output_schema["properties"]["is_single"]["enum"] == [2]


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("mutate", "expected_code"),
    [
        (lambda value: value.pop("structure"), "kg_output_fields_missing"),
        (
            lambda value: value.__setitem__("confidence", 0.9),
            "kg_output_fields_unknown",
        ),
        (
            lambda value: value.__setitem__("material", None),
            "kg_output_field_type_invalid",
        ),
        (
            lambda value: value.__setitem__("is_single", 1),
            "kg_output_route_mismatch",
        ),
        (
            lambda value: value.__setitem__("ll_id", "another-asset"),
            "kg_output_identity_mismatch",
        ),
    ],
)
def test_output_validation_rejects_shape_and_route_drift(mutate, expected_code) -> None:
    from app.knowledge_graph_contracts import (
        KnowledgeGraphContractError,
        validate_knowledge_graph_output,
    )

    payload = deepcopy(_domestic_whole_output())
    mutate(payload)

    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeGraphContractError) as captured:
        validate_knowledge_graph_output(
            DOMESTIC_WHOLE,
            payload,
            expected_asset_id="asset-42",
        )

    assert captured.value.code == expected_code


def test_output_adapter_preserves_raw_result_and_builds_structured_candidates() -> None:
    from app.knowledge_graph_contracts import adapt_knowledge_graph_model_output

    adapted = adapt_knowledge_graph_model_output(
        DOMESTIC_WHOLE,
        _domestic_whole_output(),
        expected_asset_id="asset-42",
        evidence_prefix="run:7:prompt-c",
    )

    assert adapted["identity"] == {
        "field": "ll_id",
        "value": "asset-42",
        "is_single": 0,
    }
    assert adapted["raw_output"] == _domestic_whole_output()
    assert adapted["semantic"]["object"] == [
        {
            "value": "沙发",
            "locale": "zh",
            "rank": 1,
            "weight": 0.7,
            "evidence_ref": "run:7:prompt-c#object.0",
        },
        {
            "value": "茶几",
            "locale": "zh",
            "rank": 2,
            "weight": 0.5,
            "evidence_ref": "run:7:prompt-c#object.1",
        },
    ]
    assert adapted["semantic"]["material"][1]["value"] == "织物"
    assert adapted["semantic"]["material"][1]["weight"] is None
    assert "structural_features" not in adapted["semantic"]

def test_title_accepts_free_text_with_commas_and_spaces() -> None:
    """title 是自由文本标题，不能按受控词表的逗号规则切。

    提示词要求 Title Case 英文短句，正常英文写作就会出现「逗号+空格」。
    run158 十条全灭里有九条是 title 被当多值字段校验切死的。
    """

    from app.knowledge_graph_contracts import validate_knowledge_graph_output

    for title in (
        "Modern Minimal Living Room, Warm Wood",
        "Sofa, Table and Lamp Set",
        "A, B, C, D, E",
    ):
        validated = validate_knowledge_graph_output(
            OVERSEAS_WHOLE,
            _overseas_whole_output(title),
            expected_asset_id="asset-77",
        )
        assert validated["title"] == title


def test_title_still_rejects_newline_and_overlong_text() -> None:
    from app.knowledge_graph_contracts import (
        KnowledgeGraphContractError,
        validate_knowledge_graph_output,
    )

    for bad in ("line one\nline two", "x" * 201):
        with pytest.raises(KnowledgeGraphContractError) as captured:
            validate_knowledge_graph_output(
                OVERSEAS_WHOLE,
                _overseas_whole_output(bad),
                expected_asset_id="asset-77",
            )
        assert captured.value.code == "kg_output_value_invalid"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_knowledge_graph_is_single_codes.py =====
"""海外 is_single 编码 1/2（国内 0/1）：合同、真值自愈、旧版本预检三层一致。"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import Base
from app.knowledge_graph_contracts import (
    IS_SINGLE_CODES,
    asset_scope_from_is_single,
    get_knowledge_graph_contract,
    is_single_code,
    knowledge_graph_route_issues,
    normalize_is_single_for_contract,
)
from app.models import Asset, BaselineSet, BaselineSetItem
from app.baseline_truth import BaselineTruthError, resolve_effective_baseline_truth
from app.native_workflow_migration import _KNOWLEDGE_GRAPH_ROUTES


def test_codes_are_site_specific_and_contracts_follow() -> None:
    assert dict(IS_SINGLE_CODES["domestic"]) == {"whole": 0, "single": 1}
    assert dict(IS_SINGLE_CODES["overseas"]) == {"whole": 1, "single": 2}
    assert is_single_code("overseas", "whole") == 1
    assert asset_scope_from_is_single("overseas", 1) == "whole"
    assert asset_scope_from_is_single("overseas", 0) is None
    assert asset_scope_from_is_single("domestic", 2) is None
    assert asset_scope_from_is_single("overseas", True) is None
    for category in ("knowledge_graph", "model_3d_su"):
        for site, scopes in IS_SINGLE_CODES.items():
            for scope, code in scopes.items():
                contract = get_knowledge_graph_contract(f"kg.{category}.{site}.{scope}.v1")
                assert contract is not None and contract.is_single == code
                assert contract.output_schema["properties"]["is_single"]["enum"] == [code]
    # 工作流模板的四条路线与合同编码一致
    assert {(r["site_scope"], r["asset_scope"], r["is_single"]) for r in _KNOWLEDGE_GRAPH_ROUTES} == {
        ("domestic", "whole", 0), ("domestic", "single", 1), ("overseas", "whole", 1), ("overseas", "single", 2),
    }


def test_legacy_overseas_codes_heal_by_contract_key_only() -> None:
    whole = get_knowledge_graph_contract("kg.knowledge_graph.overseas.whole.v1")
    single = get_knowledge_graph_contract("kg.knowledge_graph.overseas.single.v1")
    domestic_single = get_knowledge_graph_contract("kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.single.v1")
    assert normalize_is_single_for_contract(whole, 0) == 1  # 历史别名
    assert normalize_is_single_for_contract(whole, 1) == 1
    assert normalize_is_single_for_contract(single, 1) == 2  # 历史别名
    assert normalize_is_single_for_contract(single, 2) == 2
    assert normalize_is_single_for_contract(whole, 2) is None
    assert normalize_is_single_for_contract(domestic_single, 2) is None
    assert normalize_is_single_for_contract(domestic_single, 1) == 1


_SEQ = iter(range(1, 1000))


def _legacy_overseas_item(db: Session, *, scope: str, legacy_code: int) -> BaselineSetItem:
    seq = next(_SEQ)
    asset = Asset(
        original_name=f"国外_{'整体' if scope == 'whole' else '单体'}_1_77.jpg",
        stored_name=f"kg-legacy-{scope}-{seq}.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=10,
        sha256=f"{seq:064x}",
        category_key="knowledge_graph",
        status="uploaded",
    )
    db.add(asset)
    db.flush()
    baseline = BaselineSet(category_key="knowledge_graph", name=f"legacy-{scope}-{seq}", description="", default_expected_level="L3", fingerprint=f"{seq:064x}", created_by="tests")
    db.add(baseline)
    db.flush()
    contract_key = f"kg.knowledge_graph.overseas.{scope}.v1"
    item = BaselineSetItem(
        baseline_set_id=baseline.id,
        asset_id=asset.id,
        expected_level=None,
        expected_result_json=json.dumps({"res_id": "77", "is_single": legacy_code, "title": "", "space": ""}),
        truth_contract_key=contract_key,
        asset_snapshot_json=json.dumps({
            "schema_version": "baseline-asset-v2",
            "asset_id": asset.id,
            "name": asset.original_name,
            "sha256": asset.sha256,
            "mime_type": asset.mime_type,
            "size_bytes": 10,
            "width": None,
            "height": None,
            "source_package_id": None,
            "created_at": "2026-08-27T00:00:00",
            "workflow_input": {
                "source": "overseas",
                "is_single": legacy_code,
                "res_id": "77",
                "fixture_key": f"kg.overseas.{scope}.v1",
            },
        }),
    )
    db.add(item)
    db.flush()
    return item


def test_reading_legacy_overseas_truth_heals_to_new_codes(tmp_path) -> None:
    """基准集 16 那批海外条目存的是旧编码（整体 0/单体 1），读出来必须是 1/2，且不报错。"""

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'kg-legacy.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            whole = _legacy_overseas_item(db, scope="whole", legacy_code=0)
            single = _legacy_overseas_item(db, scope="single", legacy_code=1)
            healed_whole = resolve_effective_baseline_truth(db, whole)
            healed_single = resolve_effective_baseline_truth(db, single)
            assert healed_whole.workflow_input["is_single"] == 1
            assert healed_whole.expected_result["is_single"] == 1
            assert healed_single.workflow_input["is_single"] == 2
            assert healed_single.expected_result["is_single"] == 2
            # 与路线无关的值仍然拒绝
            broken = _legacy_overseas_item(db, scope="whole", legacy_code=2)
            with pytest.raises(BaselineTruthError) as captured:
                resolve_effective_baseline_truth(db, broken)
            assert captured.value.code == "baseline_truth_scope_invalid"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_stale_workflow_versions_are_flagged_before_any_run() -> None:
    contract = get_knowledge_graph_contract("kg.knowledge_graph.overseas.whole.v1")
    fresh = contract.snapshot()
    stale_snapshot = {**fresh, "is_single": 0, "contract_hash": "0" * 64}
    manifest = {
        "nodes": [
            {"node_instance_id": "prompt_overseas_whole", "kind": "prompt", "config": {"output_contract_snapshot": stale_snapshot}},
            {"node_instance_id": "prompt_domestic_whole", "kind": "prompt", "config": {"output_contract_snapshot": get_knowledge_graph_contract("kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1").snapshot()}},
        ],
        "edges": [
            {"from": "route_selector", "to": "prompt_overseas_whole", "branch": "overseas_whole", "condition": {"all": [
                {"op": "eq", "path": "steps.upstream.output.source", "value": "overseas"},
                {"op": "eq", "path": "steps.upstream.output.is_single", "value": 0},
            ]}},
            {"from": "route_selector", "to": "prompt_domestic_whole", "branch": "domestic_whole", "condition": {"all": [
                {"op": "eq", "path": "steps.upstream.output.is_single", "value": 0},
            ]}},
        ],
    }
    issues = knowledge_graph_route_issues(manifest)
    assert len(issues) == 2
    assert any("overseas_whole" in issue and "当前编码应为 1" in issue for issue in issues)
    assert any("prompt_overseas_whole" in issue and "已升级" in issue for issue in issues)

    current = {**manifest, "nodes": [manifest["nodes"][1]], "edges": [manifest["edges"][1]]}
    assert knowledge_graph_route_issues(current) == []


def test_prompt_text_demanding_old_code_is_flagged() -> None:
    """kg-native-v2 曾复用旧海外提示词（always output the number 0）：正文与合同编码不一致要在发起前拦住。"""

    contract = get_knowledge_graph_contract("kg.knowledge_graph.overseas.whole.v1")
    def node(system_prompt: str, user_prompt: str = "") -> dict:
        return {
            "node_instance_id": "prompt_overseas_whole",
            "kind": "prompt",
            "config": {
                "output_contract_snapshot": contract.snapshot(),
                "component_snapshot": {"content": json.dumps({"system_prompt": system_prompt, "user_prompt": user_prompt})},
            },
        }
    stale = {"nodes": [node("●res_id: copy.●is_single: always output the number 0.●title: x")], "edges": []}
    assert any("仍要求输出 is_single=0" in issue for issue in knowledge_graph_route_issues(stale))
    fresh = {"nodes": [node("●is_single: always output the number 1.", '{"res_id":"{{ASSET_ID}}","is_single":1,"title":""}')], "edges": []}
    assert knowledge_graph_route_issues(fresh) == []
    conflicting = {"nodes": [node("●is_single: always output the number 1.", '{"is_single":0}')], "edges": []}
    # 正文自相矛盾时读不出明确值，不误报（由合同校验在执行时兜底）
    assert knowledge_graph_route_issues(conflicting) == []
    domestic = get_knowledge_graph_contract("kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.single.v1")
    zh = {"nodes": [{"node_instance_id": "prompt_domestic_single", "kind": "prompt", "config": {"output_contract_snapshot": domestic.snapshot(), "component_snapshot": {"content": {"system_prompt": "●is_single：固定为数字 1。", "user_prompt": ""}}}}], "edges": []}
    assert knowledge_graph_route_issues(zh) == []

===== FILE backend/tests/test_knowledge_graph_native_workflow.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker

from app.database import Base
from app.models import (
    CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding,
    ProductionRun,
    ProductionStepAttempt,
    WorkflowVersion,
)
from app.seed import seed_defaults
from app.workflow_registry import persisted_workflow_manifest


# is_single 是站点相关编码：国内 整体=0/单体=1，海外 整体=1/单体=2
ROUTES = (
    ("domestic", 0, "domestic_whole", "ll_id", "ll-whole-1"),
    ("domestic", 1, "domestic_single", "ll_id", "ll-single-1"),
    ("overseas", 1, "overseas_whole", "res_id", "res-whole-1"),
    ("overseas", 2, "overseas_single", "res_id", "res-single-1"),
)


def _seed_knowledge_graph(db: Session) -> WorkflowVersion:
    from app.native_workflow_migration import ensure_knowledge_graph_workflow

    seed_defaults(db)
    first = ensure_knowledge_graph_workflow(
        db,
        actor="system:kg-native-test",
        category_key="knowledge_graph",
    )
    second = ensure_knowledge_graph_workflow(
        db,
        actor="system:kg-native-test",
        category_key="knowledge_graph",
    )
    assert second.id == first.id
    return first


def test_frozen_knowledge_graph_projection_drift_warns_without_startup_error(
    tmp_path,
    caplog: pytest.LogCaptureFixture,
) -> None:
    """已冻结投影与当前配置不同时应告警，但不能污染启动错误门禁。"""
    from app.native_workflow_migration import ensure_knowledge_graph_workflow

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'kg-projection-drift.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            workflow = _seed_knowledge_graph(db)
            workflow.step_manifest_json = "[]"
            db.flush()

            caplog.clear()
            with caplog.at_level(
                logging.WARNING,
                logger="app.native_workflow_migration",
            ):
                preserved = ensure_knowledge_graph_workflow(
                    db,
                    actor="system:kg-drift-test",
                    category_key="knowledge_graph",
                )

            assert preserved.id == workflow.id
            drift_records = [
                record
                for record in caplog.records
                if "知识图谱投影" in record.getMessage()
            ]
            assert len(drift_records) == 1
            assert drift_records[0].levelno == logging.WARNING
            assert not [record for record in caplog.records if record.levelno >= logging.ERROR]
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_updated_prompt_source_does_not_rewrite_or_block_frozen_kg_version(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    caplog: pytest.LogCaptureFixture,
) -> None:
    from app import native_workflow_migration
    from app.native_workflow_migration import ensure_knowledge_graph_workflow

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'kg-prompt-source-drift.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            workflow = _seed_knowledge_graph(db)
            original_hash = workflow.canonical_hash
            original_prompt_ids = [
                node["config"]["prompt_version_id"]
                for node in persisted_workflow_manifest(workflow)["nodes"]
                if node["kind"] == "prompt"
            ]
            original = native_workflow_migration._knowledge_graph_prompt_sources()
            monkeypatch.setattr(
                native_workflow_migration,
                "_knowledge_graph_prompt_sources",
                lambda: {
                    key: f"{value}\n新的候选正文只允许进入新版本。"
                    for key, value in original.items()
                },
            )

            with caplog.at_level(
                logging.WARNING,
                logger="app.native_workflow_migration",
            ):
                preserved = ensure_knowledge_graph_workflow(
                    db,
                    actor="system:kg-prompt-source-drift",
                    category_key="knowledge_graph",
                )

            assert preserved.id == workflow.id
            assert preserved.canonical_hash == original_hash
            assert [
                node["config"]["prompt_version_id"]
                for node in persisted_workflow_manifest(preserved)["nodes"]
                if node["kind"] == "prompt"
            ] == original_prompt_ids
            assert any(
                "保留已冻结版本" in record.getMessage()
                for record in caplog.records
            )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_knowledge_graph_workflow_belongs_only_to_knowledge_graph_category(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    from app.baseline_regression import (
        StockMechanismError,
        resolve_stock_mechanism_selection,
    )
    from app.knowledge_graph_category_seed import ensure_knowledge_graph_category
    from app.native_workflow_migration import ensure_knowledge_graph_workflow

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'kg-category-separation.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            seed_defaults(db)
            ensure_knowledge_graph_category(
                db,
                actor="system:kg-separation-test",
            )
            workflow = ensure_knowledge_graph_workflow(
                db,
                actor="system:kg-separation-test",
                category_key="knowledge_graph",
            )
            manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(workflow)
            assert manifest["category_key"] == "knowledge_graph"
            assert workflow.definition.workflow_key == (
                "knowledge_graph.evaluation"
            )
            assert workflow.definition.name == "知识图谱打标评测工作流"
            assert all(
                node["config"].get("output_contract_key", "").startswith(
                    "kg.knowledge_graph."
                )
                for node in manifest["nodes"]
                if node["kind"] == "prompt"
            )
            selected = resolve_stock_mechanism_selection(
                db,
                category_key="knowledge_graph",
                workflow_version_id=workflow.id,
            )
            assert selected.version.id == workflow.id

            legacy = ensure_knowledge_graph_workflow(
                db,
                actor="system:kg-separation-test",
                category_key="model_3d_su",
            )
            with pytest.raises(StockMechanismError) as ineligible:
                resolve_stock_mechanism_selection(
                    db,
                    category_key="model_3d_su",
                    workflow_version_id=legacy.id,
                )
            assert ineligible.value.code == "stock_workflow_lane_ineligible"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_knowledge_graph_manifest_is_one_four_route_native_workflow(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'kg-native-manifest.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            workflow = _seed_knowledge_graph(db)
            manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(workflow)

            assert workflow.workflow_schema_version == "workflow-v3"
            assert workflow.status == "active"
            assert manifest["category_key"] == "knowledge_graph"
            assert manifest["lane_key"] == "knowledge_graph"
            assert {
                "labeling",
                "evaluation",
                "prompt_regression",
                "stock_regression",
            } <= set(manifest["run_modes"])
            assert db.scalar(select(CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding)) is None

            nodes = {
                node["node_instance_id"]: node for node in manifest["nodes"]
            }
            assert [node["kind"] for node in manifest["nodes"]].count(
                "upstream_connector"
            ) == 1
            assert [node["kind"] for node in manifest["nodes"]].count("branch") == 1
            assert [node["kind"] for node in manifest["nodes"]].count("prompt") == 4
            assert [node["kind"] for node in manifest["nodes"]].count(
                "downstream_projection"
            ) == 4
            assert [node["kind"] for node in manifest["nodes"]].count("join") == 1
            assert nodes["route_selector"]["config"] == {
                "branch_key": "knowledge_graph_route",
                "branches": [route[2] for route in ROUTES],
                "mode": "exclusive",
            }
            assert nodes["route_selector"]["error_policy"] == {
                "kind": "manual_review"
            }
            assert nodes["route_join"]["config"] == {
                "join_for": "knowledge_graph_route",
                "mode": "all",
            }

            prompt_nodes = [
                node for node in manifest["nodes"] if node["kind"] == "prompt"
            ]
            assert {node["config"]["asset_key"] for node in prompt_nodes} == {
                f"kg_{route_key}_prompt" for _, _, route_key, _, _ in ROUTES
            }
            assert {
                node["config"]["output_contract_key"] for node in prompt_nodes
            } == {
                f"kg.knowledge_graph.{site}.{route_key.split('_')[1]}.v1"
                for site, _, route_key, _, _ in ROUTES
            }
            assert all(
                isinstance(node["config"].get("prompt_version_id"), int)
                and isinstance(node["config"].get("model_registry_id"), int)
                and node["config"]["max_tokens"] >= 128
                and node["config"]["thinking_level"] == "none"
                and node["config"]["thinking_enabled"] is False
                and len(node["config"]["component_snapshot"]["content_hash"])
                == 64
                and len(node["config"]["model_snapshot"]["snapshot_hash"]) == 64
                for node in prompt_nodes
            )

            projection_nodes = [
                node
                for node in manifest["nodes"]
                if node["kind"] == "downstream_projection"
            ]
            assert {
                node["config"]["logical_target"] for node in projection_nodes
            } == {"knowledge_graph_domestic", "knowledge_graph_overseas"}
            assert {
                node["config"]["field_contract_key"] for node in projection_nodes
            } == {
                node["config"]["output_contract_key"] for node in prompt_nodes
            }

            branch_edges = [
                edge for edge in manifest["edges"] if edge["from"] == "route_selector"
            ]
            assert [edge["branch"] for edge in branch_edges] == [
                route[2] for route in ROUTES
            ]
            assert all(
                isinstance(edge["condition"].get("all"), list)
                and len(edge["condition"]["all"]) == 2
                for edge in branch_edges
            )
            assert all(
                edge["to"] == f"prompt_{edge['branch']}"
                for edge in branch_edges
            )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("site_scope", "is_single", "route_key", "identity_field", "identity"),
    ROUTES,
)
def test_knowledge_graph_route_runs_only_one_prompt_and_projection(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
    site_scope: str,
    is_single: int,
    route_key: str,
    identity_field: str,
    identity: str,
) -> None:
    from app.workflow_fixture_executor import process_runtime_step_once
    from app.workflow_runtime import ProductionRunRequest, create_production_run

    engine = create_engine(
        f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / f'kg-native-{route_key}.db'}"
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            workflow = _seed_knowledge_graph(db)
            input_manifest = {
                "source": site_scope,
                "is_single": is_single,
                identity_field: identity,
                "fixture_key": f"kg.{site_scope}.{route_key.split('_')[1]}.v1",
            }
            run = create_production_run(
                db,
                ProductionRunRequest(
                    workflow_version_id=workflow.id,
                    idempotency_key=f"kg-native-route-{route_key}",
                    category_key="knowledge_graph",
                    queue_class="validation",
                    runtime_context={"run_mode": "evaluation"},
                    input_manifest=input_manifest,
                    owner="knowledge-graph",
                    reason="知识图谱四路原生 dry-run",
                    environment="dry_run",
                ),
                actor="ops",
            )
            run_id = run.id
            db.commit()

        import app.workflow_fixture_executor as fixture_executor

        monkeypatch.setattr(fixture_executor, "SessionLocal", sessions)
        processed = 0
        while process_runtime_step_once(f"kg-{route_key}-worker"):
            processed += 1
            assert processed <= 6

        with sessions() as db:
            run = db.get(ProductionRun, run_id)
            assert run is not None and run.status == "succeeded"
            attempts = {
                item.step_key: item
                for item in db.scalars(
                    select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                        ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run_id
                    )
                ).all()
            }
            active_prompt = f"prompt_{route_key}"
            active_projection = f"project_{route_key}"
            assert attempts[active_prompt].status == "succeeded"
            assert attempts[active_projection].status == "succeeded"
            for _, _, other_route, _, _ in ROUTES:
                if other_route == route_key:
                    continue
                assert attempts[f"prompt_{other_route}"].status == "skipped"
                assert attempts[f"project_{other_route}"].status == "skipped"
            prompt_output = json.loads(attempts[active_prompt].output_manifest_json)[
                "output"
            ]
            assert prompt_output[identity_field] == identity
            assert prompt_output["is_single"] == is_single
            projection = json.loads(
                attempts[active_projection].output_manifest_json
            )["output"]
            assert projection["logical_target"] == (
                "knowledge_graph_domestic"
                if site_scope == "domestic"
                else "knowledge_graph_overseas"
            )
            assert projection["payload"][identity_field] == identity
            assert projection["payload"]["is_single"] == is_single
            assert projection["row_count"] == 1
            assert projection["read_back"] == {
                "matched": True,
                "payload_hash": projection["payload_hash"],
            }
            join = json.loads(attempts["route_join"].output_manifest_json)[
                "output"
            ]
            assert join["successful_predecessors"] == [active_projection]
            assert len(join["skipped_predecessors"]) == 3
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("input_manifest", "expected_code"),
    [
        (
            {
                "is_single": 0,
                "ll_id": "ll-1",
                "fixture_key": "kg.domestic.whole.v1",
            },
            "kg_input_source_invalid",
        ),
        (
            {
                "source": "domestic",

                "ll_id": "ll-1",
                "res_id": "res-1",
                "fixture_key": "kg.domestic.whole.v1",
            },
            "kg_input_identity_conflict",
        ),
        (
            {
                "source": "domestic",
                "ll_id": "ll-1",
                "fixture_key": "kg.domestic.whole.v1",
            },
            "kg_input_is_single_invalid",
        ),
        (
            {
                "source": "domestic",
                "is_single": True,
                "ll_id": "ll-1",
                "fixture_key": "kg.domestic.single.v1",
            },
            "kg_input_is_single_invalid",
        ),
        (
            {"source": "domestic", "is_single": 0, "ll_id": "ll-1"},
            "kg_fixture_missing",
        ),
        (
            {
                "source": "domestic",
                "is_single": 0,
                "ll_id": "ll-1",
                "fixture_key": "kg.overseas.whole.v1",
            },
            "kg_fixture_route_mismatch",
        ),
    ],
)
def test_knowledge_graph_invalid_upstream_facts_stop_for_manual_review(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
    input_manifest: dict,
    expected_code: str,
) -> None:
    from app.workflow_fixture_executor import process_runtime_step_once
    from app.workflow_runtime import ProductionRunRequest, create_production_run

    engine = create_engine(
        f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / f'kg-invalid-{expected_code}.db'}"
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            workflow = _seed_knowledge_graph(db)
            run = create_production_run(
                db,
                ProductionRunRequest(
                    workflow_version_id=workflow.id,
                    idempotency_key=f"kg-invalid-{expected_code}",
                    category_key="knowledge_graph",
                    queue_class="validation",
                    runtime_context={"run_mode": "evaluation"},
                    input_manifest=input_manifest,
                    owner="knowledge-graph",
                    reason="验证知识图谱输入失败关闭",
                    environment="dry_run",
                ),
                actor="ops",
            )
            run_id = run.id
            db.commit()

        import app.workflow_fixture_executor as fixture_executor

        monkeypatch.setattr(fixture_executor, "SessionLocal", sessions)
        processed = 0
        while process_runtime_step_once(f"kg-invalid-{expected_code}"):
            processed += 1
            assert processed <= 2

        with sessions() as db:
            run = db.get(ProductionRun, run_id)
            assert run is not None
            assert run.status == "blocked"
            assert run.error_code == expected_code
            attempts = db.scalars(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                    ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run_id
                )
            ).all()
            upstream = next(item for item in attempts if item.step_key == "upstream")
            assert upstream.status == "blocked"
            assert upstream.last_error_code == expected_code
            assert not any(
                item.status == "succeeded" and item.step_key.startswith("prompt_")
                for item in attempts
            )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("mutation", "expected_code"),
    [
        (lambda payload: payload.__setitem__("extra", "forbidden"), "kg_output_fields_unknown"),
        (lambda payload: payload.__setitem__("ll_id", "wrong-id"), "kg_output_identity_mismatch"),
    ],
)
def test_knowledge_graph_projection_rejects_model_output_drift(
    tmp_path,
    mutation,
    expected_code: str,
) -> None:
    from app.knowledge_graph_contracts import resolve_knowledge_graph_fixture
    from app.workflow_node_executors import (
        NodeExecutionContext,
        NodeExecutorError,
        execute_native_node,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / f'kg-output-{expected_code}.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            workflow = _seed_knowledge_graph(db)
            manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(workflow)
        node = next(
            item
            for item in manifest["nodes"]
            if item["node_instance_id"] == "project_domestic_whole"
        )
        frozen_input = {
            "source": "domestic",
            "is_single": 0,
            "ll_id": "ll-1",
            "fixture_key": "kg.domestic.whole.v1",
        }
        payload = resolve_knowledge_graph_fixture(
            "kg.domestic.whole.v1",
            "kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1",
            expected_asset_id="ll-1",
        )
        mutation(payload)
        with pytest.raises(NodeExecutorError) as captured:
            execute_native_node(
                NodeExecutionContext(
                    run_snapshot={
                        "workflow": {"manifest": manifest},
                        "runtime_context": {"input_manifest": frozen_input},
                    },
                    node=node,
                    input_manifest={
                        "upstream": {
                            "prompt_domestic_whole": {"output": payload}
                        }
                    },
                    raw_fixture_output={},
                    attempt_no=1,
                    started_at=None,
                )
            )
        assert captured.value.code == expected_code
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_knowledge_graph_projection_failure_keeps_rejected_model_output(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    """校验失败必须留下被拒的模型原始输出。

    run158 排查时 61 个合同节点全是 null，运营和排查者都看不到模型
    到底输出了什么，只能翻代码反推。失败证据要随异常带出去落 checkpoint。
    """

    from app.knowledge_graph_contracts import resolve_knowledge_graph_fixture
    from app.workflow_node_executors import (
        NodeExecutionContext,
        NodeExecutorError,
        execute_native_node,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'kg-evidence.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            workflow = _seed_knowledge_graph(db)
            manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(workflow)
        node = next(
            item
            for item in manifest["nodes"]
            if item["node_instance_id"] == "project_domestic_whole"
        )
        payload = resolve_knowledge_graph_fixture(
            "kg.domestic.whole.v1",
            "kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1",
            expected_asset_id="ll-1",
        )
        payload["style"] = "现代简约,自然主义,侘寂"  # 超出 style 上限 2

        with pytest.raises(NodeExecutorError) as captured:
            execute_native_node(
                NodeExecutionContext(
                    run_snapshot={
                        "workflow": {"manifest": manifest},
                        "runtime_context": {
                            "input_manifest": {
                                "source": "domestic",
                                "is_single": 0,
                                "ll_id": "ll-1",
                                "fixture_key": "kg.domestic.whole.v1",
                            }
                        },
                    },
                    node=node,
                    input_manifest={
                        "upstream": {
                            "prompt_domestic_whole": {"output": payload}
                        }
                    },
                    raw_fixture_output={},
                    attempt_no=1,
                    started_at=None,
                )
            )

        assert captured.value.code == "kg_output_value_invalid"
        evidence = captured.value.evidence
        assert evidence["contract_key"] == "kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1"
        rejected = evidence["rejected_model_output"]
        assert rejected["style"] == "现代简约,自然主义,侘寂"
        assert rejected["ll_id"] == "ll-1"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_fixture_error_carries_node_executor_evidence() -> None:
    """执行器异常带的证据必须沿 FixtureExecutionError 传到 checkpoint 落库口。"""

    from app.workflow_fixture_executor import FixtureExecutionError
    from app.workflow_node_executors import NodeExecutorError

    inner = NodeExecutorError(
        "kg_output_value_invalid",
        "style 最多允许 2 个值",
        evidence={"rejected_model_output": {"style": "a,b,c"}},
    )
    outer = FixtureExecutionError(
        inner.code,
        str(inner),
        error_policy_kind="fail",
        evidence=inner.evidence,
    )
    assert outer.evidence["rejected_model_output"] == {"style": "a,b,c"}

===== FILE backend/tests/test_launcher.py =====
import json

from app import launcher


class FakeResponse:
    def __init__(self, payload: dict[str, str]) -> None:
        self.payload = payload

    def __enter__(self) -> "FakeResponse":
        return self

    def __exit__(self, *_args: object) -> None:
        return None

    def read(self) -> bytes:
        return json.dumps(self.payload).encode("utf-8")


def test_existing_3d66_service_is_detected(monkeypatch) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        launcher,
        "urlopen",
        lambda *_args, **_kwargs: FakeResponse({"service": "3d66-label-system"}),
    )
    assert launcher._service_is_running(8080) is True


def test_other_http_service_is_not_mistaken_for_3d66(monkeypatch) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        launcher,
        "urlopen",
        lambda *_args, **_kwargs: FakeResponse({"service": "something-else"}),
    )
    assert launcher._service_is_running(8080) is False


def test_worker_stops_when_launcher_process_is_gone(monkeypatch) -> None:
    class DeadParent:
        def is_alive(self) -> bool:
            return False

    captured: dict[str, object] = {}

    def fake_run_forever(**kwargs: object) -> None:
        captured.update(kwargs)

    monkeypatch.setattr(launcher.mp, "parent_process", lambda: DeadParent())
    monkeypatch.setattr(launcher, "run_forever", fake_run_forever)

    launcher._worker_entry()

    should_continue = captured["should_continue"]
    assert callable(should_continue)
    assert should_continue() is False


def test_worker_sigterm_requests_graceful_stop(monkeypatch) -> None:
    class LiveParent:
        def is_alive(self) -> bool:
            return True

    handlers: dict[int, object] = {}

    def install_handler(signum: int, handler: object) -> None:
        handlers[signum] = handler

    captured: dict[str, object] = {}

    def fake_run_forever(**kwargs: object) -> None:
        captured.update(kwargs)

    monkeypatch.setattr(launcher.mp, "parent_process", lambda: LiveParent())
    monkeypatch.setattr(launcher.signal, "signal", install_handler)
    monkeypatch.setattr(launcher, "run_forever", fake_run_forever)

    launcher._worker_entry()

    should_continue = captured["should_continue"]
    assert callable(should_continue)
    assert should_continue() is True
    handler = handlers[launcher.signal.SIGTERM]
    assert callable(handler)
    handler(launcher.signal.SIGTERM, None)
    assert should_continue() is False


def test_worker_discards_parent_database_pool_before_consuming(monkeypatch) -> None:
    class LiveParent:
        def is_alive(self) -> bool:
            return True

    events: list[str] = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(launcher.mp, "parent_process", lambda: LiveParent())
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        launcher,
        "dispose_inherited_database_pool",
        lambda: events.append("dispose"),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        launcher,
        "run_forever",
        lambda **_kwargs: events.append("consume"),
    )

    launcher._worker_entry()

    assert events == ["dispose", "consume"]


def test_launcher_worker_reuses_parent_database_initialization(monkeypatch) -> None:
    class LiveParent:
        def is_alive(self) -> bool:
            return True

    captured: dict[str, object] = {}
    monkeypatch.setattr(launcher.mp, "parent_process", lambda: LiveParent())
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        launcher,
        "dispose_inherited_database_pool",
        lambda: None,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        launcher,
        "run_forever",
        lambda **kwargs: captured.update(kwargs),
    )

    launcher._worker_entry(database_initialized=True)

    assert captured["initialize_database"] is False
    assert captured["run_auxiliary_ticks"] is True


def test_launcher_starts_one_dedicated_auxiliary_and_keeps_all_evaluation_workers(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    captured: list[dict[str, object]] = []

    class FakeProcess:
        def __init__(self, **kwargs: object) -> None:
            captured.append(kwargs)

    monkeypatch.setattr(launcher.mp, "Process", FakeProcess)

    workers = launcher._build_worker_processes(port=18080, worker_count=4)

    assert len(workers) == 7
    assert [entry["name"] for entry in captured] == [
        "3d66-auxiliary",
        "3d66-optimizer",
        "3d66-projector",
        "3d66-worker-1",
        "3d66-worker-2",
        "3d66-worker-3",
        "3d66-worker-4",
    ]
    assert [entry["args"] for entry in captured] == [
        (18080, True, True, False, True, "auxiliary", False, False),
        (18080, True, False, False, True, "optimization", True, False),
        (18080, True, False, False, True, "projection", False, True),
        (18080, True, False, True, True, "evaluation", False, False),
        (18080, True, False, True, True, "evaluation", False, False),
        (18080, True, False, True, True, "evaluation", False, False),
        (18080, True, False, True, True, "evaluation", False, False),
    ]


def test_launcher_isolates_long_optimizer_from_auxiliary_scheduler() -> None:
    specs = launcher._worker_process_specs(port=18080, worker_count=2)
