        True,
        False,
        True,
        "auxiliary",
        False,
        False,
    )
    assert specs[1][1] == (
        18080,
        True,
        False,
        False,
        True,
        "optimization",
        True,
        False,
    )
    assert specs[2][1] == (
        18080,
        True,
        False,
        False,
        True,
        "projection",
        False,
        True,
    )


def test_launcher_replaces_a_dead_auxiliary_without_reducing_evaluation_capacity(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    class FakeProcess:
        def __init__(
            self,
            *,
            target,
            args: tuple[object, ...],
            name: str,
            daemon: bool,
        ) -> None:
            del target, daemon
            self.args = args
            self.name = name
            self.alive = True
            self.started = False
            self.joined = False

        def start(self) -> None:
            self.started = True

        def is_alive(self) -> bool:
            return self.alive

        def join(self, timeout: float | None = None) -> None:
            del timeout
            self.joined = True

    monkeypatch.setattr(launcher.mp, "Process", FakeProcess)
    workers = launcher._build_worker_processes(port=18080, worker_count=2)
    for process in workers:
        process.start()
    dead_auxiliary = workers[0]
    dead_auxiliary.alive = False

    replaced = launcher._replace_dead_worker_processes(
        workers,
        port=18080,
        worker_count=2,
    )

    assert dead_auxiliary.joined is True
    assert replaced[0] is not dead_auxiliary
    assert replaced[0].name == "3d66-auxiliary"
    assert replaced[0].args == (
        18080,
        True,
        True,
        False,
        True,
        "auxiliary",
        False,
        False,
    )
    assert replaced[0].started is True
    assert replaced[1:] == workers[1:]


def test_worker_waits_for_web_startup_before_consuming(monkeypatch) -> None:
    class LiveParent:
        def is_alive(self) -> bool:
            return True

    readiness = iter([False, False, True])
    consumed: list[bool] = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(launcher.mp, "parent_process", lambda: LiveParent())
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        launcher,
        "_service_is_running",
        lambda _port: next(readiness),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(launcher.time, "sleep", lambda _seconds: None)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        launcher,
        "run_forever",
        lambda **_kwargs: consumed.append(True),
    )

    launcher._worker_entry(18080)

    assert consumed == [True]


def test_worker_entry_restarts_after_unhandled_failure(monkeypatch) -> None:
    class LiveParent:
        def is_alive(self) -> bool:
            return True

    calls: list[str] = []

    def flaky_run_forever(**_kwargs: object) -> None:
        calls.append("run")
        if len(calls) == 1:
            raise RuntimeError("claim loop failed")

    monkeypatch.setattr(launcher.mp, "parent_process", lambda: LiveParent())
    monkeypatch.setattr(launcher, "run_forever", flaky_run_forever)
    monkeypatch.setattr(launcher.time, "sleep", lambda _seconds: None)

    launcher._worker_entry()

    assert calls == ["run", "run"]


def test_worker_entry_does_not_restart_after_clean_return(monkeypatch) -> None:
    class LiveParent:
        def is_alive(self) -> bool:
            return True

    calls: list[str] = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(launcher.mp, "parent_process", lambda: LiveParent())
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        launcher,
        "run_forever",
        lambda **_kwargs: calls.append("run"),
    )

    launcher._worker_entry()

    assert calls == ["run"]

===== FILE backend/tests/test_legacy_native_projection.py =====
"""Legacy evaluation projection must publish evidence, never a placeholder."""
import json
import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker
from app.workflow_node_executors import NodeExecutionContext, NodeExecutorError, execute_native_node


def projection(category='model_3d_su', config=None, outputs=None, enabled_levels=None):
    node={'node_instance_id':'downstream','kind':'downstream_projection','config':config or {'connector_key':'logical.test.output'},'output_schema':{'type':'object'}}
    manifest={'schema_version':'workflow-v3','category_key':category,'nodes':[
        {'node_instance_id':'prompt_a','kind':'prompt','config':{}},node],
        'edges':[{'from':'prompt_a','to':'downstream'}],
        'resource_policy':{'legacy_semantics':'documented_php_chain'}}
    if enabled_levels is not None:
        manifest['resource_policy']['enabled_levels']=enabled_levels
    return execute_native_node(NodeExecutionContext(run_snapshot={'workflow':{'manifest':manifest},'runtime_context':{'model_execution_mode':'real'}},node=node,
        input_manifest={'upstream':outputs or {}},raw_fixture_output={},attempt_no=1,started_at=None))


@pytest.mark.parametrize('category',['model_3d_su','inspiration_image'])
@pytest.mark.parametrize('level',['L2','L3'])
def test_projection_preserves_authoritative_short_circuit(category,level):
    decision={'schema_version':'short-circuit-decision-v1','rule_version':'short-circuit-rule-v1','rule_key':'tested-rule',
        'target_level':level,'skipped_nodes':['prompt_b'],'evidence':{'priority':900}}
    result=projection(category,outputs={'gate':{'status':'succeeded','output':{'target_level':level},'short_circuit_decision':decision}})
    assert result['output']['schema_version']=='native-evaluation-projection-v1'
    assert result['output']['normalized_result']['level']==level
    assert result['output']['normalized_result']['decision_source']=='frozen_short_circuit'


def test_documented_legacy_prompt_result_is_preserved_without_fabrication():
    actual={'level':'L4','score':73,'reason':'observed fixture fact'}
    result=projection(outputs={'prompt_a':{'status':'succeeded','output':actual}})
    assert result['output']['normalized_result']==actual


@pytest.mark.parametrize('actual',[{'score':73},{'level':'L99'},{'level':None},{'level':True}])
def test_partial_or_invalid_grade_is_rejected(actual):
    with pytest.raises(NodeExecutorError) as error:
        projection(outputs={'prompt_a':{'status':'succeeded','output':actual}})
    assert error.value.code=='native_projection_invalid'


def test_projection_uses_frozen_enabled_levels():
    with pytest.raises(NodeExecutorError) as error:
        projection(outputs={'prompt_a':{'status':'succeeded','output':{'level':'L4'}}},enabled_levels=['L2','L3'])
    assert error.value.code=='native_projection_invalid'


def test_missing_evidence_fails_instead_of_returning_placeholder():
    with pytest.raises(NodeExecutorError) as error:
        projection()
    assert error.value.code=='native_projection_invalid'


@pytest.mark.parametrize('category,config',[
    ('knowledge_graph',{'connector_key':'logical.test.output'}),
    ('model_3d_su',{'connector_key':'logical.test.output','mapping':{'schema_version':'custom-v1'}}),
])
def test_other_categories_and_explicit_mapping_preserve_existing_behavior(category,config):
    assert projection(category,config)['output']['fixture']=='logical_projection'


def test_explicit_field_contract_keeps_existing_validation():
    with pytest.raises(NodeExecutorError) as error:
        projection(config={'field_contract_snapshot':{'schema_version':'invalid'}})
    assert error.value.code=='kg_projection_contract_snapshot_invalid'


@pytest.mark.parametrize('missing_evidence',[False,True,'partial','invalid'])
def test_native_panorama_projection_outbox(tmp_path,monkeypatch,missing_evidence):
    from tests.test_strict_prompt_release import _prepare_database
    from app.native_workflow_migration import ensure_native_category_workflows
    from app.model_3d_su_upstream_gate_release import attach_model_3d_su_upstream_gate
    from app.workflow_working_copies import get_or_create_workflow_working_copy,editable_workflow_template
    from app.models import WorkflowDefinition,WorkflowVersion,ProductionRun,ProductionStepAttempt,WorkflowModelInvocation
    from app.workflow_runtime import ProductionRunRequest,create_production_run
    import app.workflow_fixture_executor as worker
    engine=_prepare_database(tmp_path)
    sessions=sessionmaker(bind=engine,expire_on_commit=False)
    with sessions() as db:
        ensure_native_category_workflows(db,'model_3d_su',actor='test')
        definition=db.scalar(select(WorkflowDefinition).where(WorkflowDefinition.workflow_key=='model_3d_su.evaluation'))
        template=editable_workflow_template(db,definition.id)
        get_or_create_workflow_working_copy(db,workflow_definition_id=definition.id,initial_manifest=template.manifest,actor='test')
        attach_model_3d_su_upstream_gate(db,actor='test',apply=True)
        from app.model_3d_su_strict_release import attach_model_3d_su_strict_contracts
        receipt=attach_model_3d_su_strict_contracts(db,actor='test',apply=True)
        version=db.get(WorkflowVersion,receipt['workflow_version_id'])
        # Seed fixtures predate execution roles; create an explicit fixture version.
        from app.workflow_registry import persisted_workflow_manifest,create_workflow_version
        manifest=persisted_workflow_manifest(version)
        manifest['run_modes']=['evaluation','incremental']
        for node in manifest['nodes']:
            if node['node_instance_id'] not in {'upstream','upstream_type_short_circuit_0','downstream'}:
                node['config']['execution_role']='grading'
        if missing_evidence:
            manifest['nodes']=[n for n in manifest['nodes'] if n['kind']=='downstream_projection']
            manifest['edges']=[]
        version=create_workflow_version(db,definition=definition,version='panorama-projection-fixture',manifest=manifest,created_by='test')
        version.status='active'
        identity=(version.canonical_hash,version.step_manifest_json)
        from app.connector_contracts import create_connector_contract_version
        from app.connector_intake import IntakeRequest,ingest_upstream_event
        from app.workflow_connector_executor import create_incremental_run_from_intake
        for node in manifest['nodes']:
            if node['kind'] not in {'upstream_connector','downstream_projection'}:continue
            direction='upstream' if node['kind']=='upstream_connector' else 'downstream'
            contract=create_connector_contract_version(db,contract_key=node['config']['connector_key'],direction=direction,
                category_key='model_3d_su',logical_locator='logical.fixture.'+direction,adapter_key='api-json-v1',
                schema_fingerprint='a'*64,field_mapping={'content_id':'content_id'},
                cursor_contract={'field':'sequence','kind':'integer','ordering':'ascending','out_of_order':'delay'} if direction=='upstream' else {},
                idempotency_contract={'field':'event_id'},batch_size=10,timeout_seconds=30,owner='test',status='active',created_by='test')
            if direction=='upstream':upstream_contract=contract
        if missing_evidence:
            run=create_production_run(db,ProductionRunRequest(workflow_version_id=version.id,idempotency_key='empty-projection',
                category_key='model_3d_su',queue_class='validation',input_manifest={},runtime_context={'run_mode':'incremental'},owner='test'),actor='test')
        else:
            intake=ingest_upstream_event(db,upstream_contract,IntakeRequest(source_event_id='panorama-1',source_cursor='1',source_occurred_at=None,
                category_key='model_3d_su',mechanism_key='',payload={'material_type':'全景图','content_id':'panorama-1','sequence':1},actor='test'))
            run=create_incremental_run_from_intake(db,event_id=intake.event.id,workflow_version_id=version.id,actor='test')
        run_id=run.id;db.commit()
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker,'SessionLocal',sessions)
    if missing_evidence in {'partial','invalid'}:
        import app.workflow_result_finalizer as finalizer
        partial={'score':73} if missing_evidence=='partial' else {'level':'L99'}
        monkeypatch.setattr(finalizer,'reduce_native_workflow_result',lambda *args,**kwargs:partial)
    def forbidden(*args,**kwargs):raise AssertionError('model intent forbidden')
    for _ in range(40):
        if not worker.process_runtime_step_once('projection-test',global_limit=2,model_client_type=forbidden):break
    with sessions() as db:
        run=db.get(ProductionRun,run_id)
        attempts=list(db.scalars(select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id==run_id)))
        if missing_evidence:
            from app.models import RuntimeOutboxEvent
            assert run.status=='failed'
            assert next(a for a in attempts if a.step_key=='downstream').last_error_code=='native_projection_invalid'
            assert not list(db.scalars(select(RuntimeOutboxEvent)))
            return
        assert run.status=='succeeded',[(a.step_key,a.status,a.last_error_code) for a in attempts]
        down=next(a for a in attempts if a.step_key=='downstream')
        assert json.loads(down.output_manifest_json)['output']['normalized_result']['level']=='L2'
        assert json.loads(down.input_manifest_json)['skipped_upstream']
        assert all(a.status=='skipped' for a in attempts if a.step_key in {'prompt_a','prompt_b'})
        assert not list(db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation)))
        from app.models import RuntimeOutboxEvent
        from app.workflow_snapshot_store import load_snapshot_blob
        outbox=db.scalar(select(RuntimeOutboxEvent))
        assert load_snapshot_blob(outbox.payload_blob)['result']['output']['normalized_result']['level']=='L2'
        version=db.get(WorkflowVersion,version.id)
        assert (version.canonical_hash,version.step_manifest_json)==identity

===== FILE backend/tests/test_legacy_workflow_release.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json

import pytest
import shutil

from sqlalchemy import func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker

from app.models import (
    Asset,
    BaselineRegressionRun,
    BaselineSet,
    BaselineSetItem,
    BaselineTruthRevision,
    EvaluationResult,
    EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy,
    EvaluationCategoryProfile,
    PromptVersion,
    RuleModuleVersion,
    WorkflowDefinition,
    WorkflowNodeAsset,
    WorkflowForeachItem,
    ProductionStepAttempt,
    WorkflowVersion,
)
from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.workflow_registry import persisted_workflow_manifest
from tests.test_strict_prompt_release import _prepare_database


WORKFLOW_KEYS = {
    "inspiration_image.legacy_doubao_review",
    "inspiration_image.latest_ab_comparison",
    "inspiration_image.latest_single_comparison",
    "design_case.legacy_doubao_review",
    "model_3d_su.legacy_doubao_max",
    "model_3d_su.legacy_doubao_su",
}

IMAGE_FIELDS = {
    "title",
    "seo_title",
    "category",
    "sub_category",
    "style",
    "tags",
    "cons",
    "design",
    "score",
    "reason",
    "image_defects",
    "trait",
}
CASE_FIELDS = {
    "score",
    "reason",
    "seo_title",
    "category_one",
    "category_two",
    "style",
    "matcraft",
    "designtactic",
    "spaceshape",
    "clientInfo",
    "projattr",
    "designer",
    "region_type",
    "location_detail",
    "scale",
    "dtime",
    "btime",
}


def _counts(db: Session) -> tuple[int, ...]:
    return tuple(
        db.scalar(select(func.count(model.id))) or 0
        for model in (
            EvaluationCategoryProfile,
            WorkflowDefinition,
            WorkflowVersion,
            WorkflowNodeAsset,
            EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy,
            PromptVersion,
            RuleModuleVersion,
        )
    )


def _workflow(db: Session, workflow_key: str) -> WorkflowVersion:
    definition = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowDefinition).where(
            WorkflowDefinition.workflow_key == workflow_key
        )
    )
    assert definition is not None
    rows = db.scalars(
        select(WorkflowVersion).where(
            WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id == definition.id
        )
    ).all()
    assert len(rows) == 1
    assert rows[0].status == "draft"
    return rows[0]


def test_legacy_workflow_preview_is_zero_write(tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.legacy_workflow_release import materialize_legacy_workflows

    engine = _prepare_database(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            before = _counts(db)
            receipt = materialize_legacy_workflows(
                db, actor="system:legacy-release-test", apply=False
            )

            assert receipt.mode == "preview"
            assert {item.workflow_key for item in receipt.workflows} == WORKFLOW_KEYS
            assert all(item.workflow_version_id is None for item in receipt.workflows)
            assert _counts(db) == before
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_apply_is_idempotent_and_never_mutates_or_activates_source_workflows(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    from app.legacy_workflow_release import materialize_legacy_workflows

    engine = _prepare_database(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            source_hashes = {
                row.id: row.canonical_hash
                for row in db.scalars(
                    select(WorkflowVersion).where(WorkflowVersion.status == "active")
                ).all()
            }
            first = materialize_legacy_workflows(
                db, actor="system:legacy-release-test", apply=True
            )
            db.commit()
            after_first = _counts(db)
            second = materialize_legacy_workflows(
                db, actor="system:legacy-release-test", apply=True
            )
            db.commit()

            assert first == second
            assert _counts(db) == after_first
            assert {
                workflow_id: db.get(WorkflowVersion, workflow_id).canonical_hash
                for workflow_id in source_hashes
            } == source_hashes
            assert {item.workflow_key for item in first.workflows} == WORKFLOW_KEYS
            assert all(item.status == "draft" for item in first.workflows)
            assert all(item.workflow_version_id for item in first.workflows)
            for workflow_key in WORKFLOW_KEYS:
                _workflow(db, workflow_key)
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_latest_ab_and_single_are_isolated_comparable_workflows(tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.legacy_workflow_release import materialize_legacy_workflows

    engine = _prepare_database(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            materialize_legacy_workflows(
                db, actor="system:legacy-release-test", apply=True
            )
            db.commit()
            ab = persisted_workflow_manifest(
                _workflow(db, "inspiration_image.latest_ab_comparison")
            )
            single = persisted_workflow_manifest(
                _workflow(db, "inspiration_image.latest_single_comparison")
            )

            ab_prompts = [node for node in ab["nodes"] if node["kind"] == "prompt"]
            single_prompts = [
                node for node in single["nodes"] if node["kind"] == "prompt"
            ]
            assert len(ab_prompts) == 2
            assert len(single_prompts) == 1
            assert {
                node["config"]["model_registry_id"] for node in ab_prompts
            } == {single_prompts[0]["config"]["model_registry_id"]}
            assert all(
                node["config"]["thinking_enabled"] is False
                and node["config"]["thinking_level"] == "none"
                for node in ab_prompts + single_prompts
            )

            ab_contract_fields = set().union(
                *(
                    set(node["config"]["output_contract"]["schema"]["properties"])
                    for node in ab_prompts
                )
            )
            single_contract = single_prompts[0]["config"]["output_contract"]
            assert set(single_contract["schema"]["properties"]) == ab_contract_fields
            assert set(single_contract["schema"]["required"]) == ab_contract_fields
            assert single_contract["schema"]["additionalProperties"] is False
            assert single_contract["strict"] is True

            prompt = db.get(
                PromptVersion,
                single_prompts[0]["config"]["prompt_version_id"],
            )
            assert prompt is not None
            assert "additionalProperties" not in prompt.system_prompt
            assert "```" not in prompt.system_prompt

            rules = single_prompts[0]["config"]["short_circuit_rules"]
            assert {rule["target_level"] for rule in rules} == {"L3", "L4", "L5"}
            assert ab["resource_policy"]["activated"] is False
            assert single["resource_policy"]["activated"] is False
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_changed_prompt_source_adds_version_without_rewriting_existing(
    tmp_path, monkeypatch
) -> None:
    import app.legacy_workflow_release as release

    engine = _prepare_database(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            first = release.materialize_legacy_workflows(
                db, actor="system:legacy-release-test", apply=True
            )
            db.commit()
            original = next(
                item
                for item in first.workflows
                if item.workflow_key == "inspiration_image.legacy_doubao_review"
            )

            changed_prompts = tmp_path / "changed-prompts"
            shutil.copytree(release._PROMPT_ROOT, changed_prompts)
            legacy_prompt = changed_prompts / "inspiration_legacy_single_v1.txt"
            legacy_prompt.write_text(
                legacy_prompt.read_text(encoding="utf-8") + "\n仅用于版本漂移测试。\n",
                encoding="utf-8",
            )
            monkeypatch.setattr(release, "_PROMPT_ROOT", changed_prompts)

            second = release.materialize_legacy_workflows(
                db, actor="system:legacy-release-test", apply=True
            )
            db.commit()
            updated = next(
                item
                for item in second.workflows
                if item.workflow_key == "inspiration_image.legacy_doubao_review"
            )
            assert updated.workflow_version_id != original.workflow_version_id
            assert updated.source_fingerprint != original.source_fingerprint

            definition = db.scalar(
                select(WorkflowDefinition).where(
                    WorkflowDefinition.workflow_key
                    == "inspiration_image.legacy_doubao_review"
                )
            )
            versions = db.scalars(
                select(WorkflowVersion).where(
                    WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id == definition.id
                )
            ).all()
            assert len(versions) == 2
            original_row = db.get(WorkflowVersion, original.workflow_version_id)
            assert original_row is not None
            assert original_row.canonical_hash == original.canonical_hash
            assert original_row.status == "draft"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_legacy_inspiration_and_native_design_case_contracts(tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.legacy_workflow_release import materialize_legacy_workflows
    from app.workflow_foreach_executor import materialize_foreach_items

    engine = _prepare_database(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            materialize_legacy_workflows(
                db, actor="system:legacy-release-test", apply=True
            )
            db.commit()

            legacy = persisted_workflow_manifest(
                _workflow(db, "inspiration_image.legacy_doubao_review")
            )
            legacy_prompt = next(
                node for node in legacy["nodes"] if node["kind"] == "prompt"
            )
            policy = legacy_prompt["config"]["vision_input_policy"]
            legacy_schema = legacy_prompt["config"]["output_contract"]["schema"]
            assert set(legacy_schema["properties"]) == {
                "title",
                "seo_title",
                "category",
                "style",
                "tags",
                "cons",
                "design",
                "score",
                "reason",
                "trait",
                "image_defects",
            }
            assert set(legacy_schema["required"]) == set(
                legacy_schema["properties"]
            )
            assert legacy_schema["additionalProperties"] is False
            assert legacy_schema["properties"]["reason"]["enum"] == [
                "是截图",
                "有大面积文字说明",
                "是多拼图",
                "有二维码",
                "是随手拍",
                "是颠倒图",
                "",
            ]
            assert legacy_schema["properties"]["image_defects"]["enum"] == [
                "有水印",
                "",
            ]
            assert policy["roles"]["target"] == {
                "fidelity": {"mode": "detail", "value": "high"},
                "preprocess": {"mode": "short_edge", "pixels": 600},
            }
            assert policy["transport"]["mode"] == "lossless"
            assert legacy_prompt["config"]["thinking_enabled"] is False

            profile = db.scalar(
                select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(
                    EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key == "design_case"
                )
            )
            assert profile is not None and profile.status == "active"
            case = persisted_workflow_manifest(
                _workflow(db, "design_case.legacy_doubao_review")
            )
            assert [node["kind"] for node in case["nodes"]] == [
                "upstream_connector",
                "foreach_image",
                "aggregate_case",
                "prompt",
                "rule_module",
                "downstream_projection",
            ]
            foreach = case["nodes"][1]
            aggregate = case["nodes"][2]
            text_prompt = case["nodes"][3]
            final_score = case["nodes"][4]
            image_schema = foreach["config"]["item_executor"]["output_contract"][
                "schema"
            ]
            text_schema = text_prompt["config"]["output_contract"]["schema"]
            assert set(image_schema["properties"]) == IMAGE_FIELDS
            assert set(image_schema["required"]) == IMAGE_FIELDS
            assert image_schema["additionalProperties"] is False
            assert set(text_schema["properties"]) == CASE_FIELDS
            assert set(text_schema["required"]) == CASE_FIELDS
            assert text_schema["additionalProperties"] is False
            assert aggregate["config"]["foreach_node_id"] == "foreach_images"
            assert foreach["output_schema"]["additionalProperties"] is False
            assert set(foreach["output_schema"]["required"]) == {
                "schema_version",
                "node_instance_id",
                "source_hash",
                "item_contract_hash",
                "counts",
                "items",
            }
            assert foreach["output_schema"]["properties"]["items"]["items"][
                "additionalProperties"
            ] is False
            assert aggregate["output_schema"]["additionalProperties"] is False
            assert set(aggregate["output_schema"]["required"]) == {
                "schema_version",
                "foreach_node_id",
                "source_hash",
                "item_contract_hash",
                "counts",
                "score",
                "features",
                "results",
                "failures",
            }
            assert aggregate["output_schema"]["properties"]["results"][
                "items"
            ]["properties"]["output"] == image_schema
            assert foreach["config"]["items_path"] == (
                "$.upstream.case_source.output.case.images"
            )
            assert foreach["config"]["item_identity_path"] == "$.cloud_id"
            image_input_schema = case["input_schema"]["properties"]["case"][
                "properties"
            ]["images"]["items"]
            assert set(image_input_schema["required"]) == {
                "cloud_id",
                "oss_url",
                "asset_id",
                "asset_sha256",
            }

            # Exercise the materialized release node with the exact downstream
            # envelope produced by workflow_runtime after case_source succeeds.
            rows = materialize_foreach_items(
                db,
                production_run_id=9001,
                parent_step_attempt_id=9002,
                node=foreach,
                input_manifest={
                    "upstream": {
                        "case_source": {
                            "schema_version": "workflow-node-result-v1",
                            "node_instance_id": "case_source",
                            "output": {
                                "case": {
                                    "cloud_id": "case-42",
                                    "images": [
                                        {
                                            "cloud_id": "image-a",
                                            "oss_url": "https://example.invalid/a.jpg",
                                            "asset_id": 101,
                                            "asset_sha256": "a" * 64,
                                        },
                                        {
                                            "cloud_id": "image-b",
                                            "oss_url": "https://example.invalid/b.jpg",
                                            "asset_id": 102,
                                            "asset_sha256": "b" * 64,
                                        },
                                    ],
                                }
                            },
                        }
                    }
                },
            )
            assert [row.item_key for row in rows] == ["image-a", "image-b"]

            content = json.loads(
                final_score["config"]["component_snapshot"]["content_json"]
            )
            formula = content["case_score_formula"]
            assert formula["comparison"] == "strict_gt"
            assert formula["deletion_mode"] == "recommend_only"
            assert final_score["output_schema"]["properties"][
                "side_effect_executed"
            ] == {"type": "boolean", "const": False}
            assert final_score["output_schema"]["properties"]["case_review"][
                "x-correction-editable"
            ] is False
            assert "recommended_image_ids" in final_score["output_schema"][
                "required"
            ]

            # The final-score node carries an immutable review envelope so the
            # downstream projection remains linear and resumable.  Operators
            # correct the original prompt fields only; the embedded copy must
            # never become a second editable source of truth.
            from app.correction_contract import freeze_contract_from_workflow_snapshot

            correction_contract = freeze_contract_from_workflow_snapshot(
                {
                    "workflow": {
                        "version": "1",
                        "manifest": case,
                    }
                }
            )
            correction_keys = {
                str(node["node_key"])
                for node in correction_contract["nodes"]
                if isinstance(node, dict)
            }
            assert any(
                key.startswith("workflow.case_text_prompt.")
                for key in correction_keys
            )
            assert not any(
                key.startswith("workflow.case_final_score.case_review")
                for key in correction_keys
            )
            assert case["resource_policy"]["physical_delete_enabled"] is False
            assert case["resource_policy"]["activated"] is False
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_materialized_component_assets_have_operator_working_copies(tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.legacy_workflow_release import materialize_legacy_workflows

    engine = _prepare_database(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            materialize_legacy_workflows(
                db, actor="system:legacy-release-test", apply=True
            )
            db.commit()

            expected_assets = {
                "legacy_doubao_single_prompt",
                "latest_single_prompt",
                "case_image_review",
                "case_text_review",
                "case_final_score",
            }
            assets = db.scalars(
                select(WorkflowNodeAsset).where(
                    WorkflowNodeAsset.asset_key.in_(expected_assets)
                )
            ).all()
            assert {asset.asset_key for asset in assets} == expected_assets

            for asset in assets:
                working = db.scalar(
                    select(EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy).where(
                        EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy.asset_id == asset.id
                    )
                )
                assert working is not None, asset.asset_key
                content = json.loads(working.content_json)
                if asset.kind == "prompt":
                    assert content["system_prompt"]
                    assert content["stage"]
                    assert working.base_prompt_version_id is not None
                else:
                    assert (
                        content["case_score_formula"]["deletion_mode"]
                        == "recommend_only"
                    )
                    assert working.base_rule_module_version_id is not None
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_materialized_design_case_simulation_runs_two_images_and_replays_truth(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    """The released graph itself must run and preserve append-only case truth."""

    from app.baseline_truth import DESIGN_CASE_TRUTH_CONTRACT
    from app.legacy_workflow_release import materialize_legacy_workflows
    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run
    from app.workflow_correction import append_node_correction_and_replay
    from app.workflow_fixture_executor import process_runtime_step_once

    engine = _prepare_database(tmp_path)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            materialize_legacy_workflows(
                db, actor="system:design-case-e2e", apply=True
            )
            workflow = _workflow(db, "design_case.legacy_doubao_review")
            workflow.status = "active"
            first = Asset(
                original_name="case-a.jpg",
                stored_name="case-a.jpg",
                mime_type="image/jpeg",
                size_bytes=100,
                sha256="a" * 64,
                category_key="design_case",
            )
            second = Asset(
                original_name="case-b.jpg",
                stored_name="case-b.jpg",
                mime_type="image/jpeg",
                size_bytes=100,
                sha256="b" * 64,
                category_key="design_case",
            )
            baseline = BaselineSet(
                category_key="design_case",
                name="案例双图发布图回归",
                description="",
                default_expected_level="L5",
                fingerprint="c" * 64,
                created_by="ops",
            )
            db.add_all([first, second, baseline])
            db.flush()
            workflow_input = {
                "case": {
                    "cloud_id": "case-42",
                    "project_id": "project-42",
                    "case_name": "双图案例",
                    "case_text": "用于完整发布图回归",
                    "quality_score": 10,
                    "time_score": 5,
                    "filter_threshold": 20,
                    "images": [
                        {
                            "cloud_id": "image-a",
                            "oss_url": "https://example.invalid/a.jpg",
                            "asset_id": first.id,
                            "asset_sha256": first.sha256,
                        },
                        {
                            "cloud_id": "image-b",
                            "oss_url": "https://example.invalid/b.jpg",
                            "asset_id": second.id,
                            "asset_sha256": second.sha256,
                        },
                    ],
                }
            }
            item = BaselineSetItem(
                baseline_set_id=baseline.id,
                asset_id=first.id,
                expected_level=None,
                expected_result_json=canonical_json(
                    {"case_cloud_id": "case-42", "level": "L5"}
                ),
                truth_contract_key=DESIGN_CASE_TRUTH_CONTRACT,
                asset_snapshot_json=canonical_json(
                    {
                        "asset_id": first.id,
                        "sha256": first.sha256,
                        "workflow_input": workflow_input,
                    }
                ),
            )
            db.add(item)
            db.flush()
            aggregate = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="released-design-case-simulated",
            )
            aggregate_id = aggregate.id
            production_id = aggregate.items[0].production_run_id
            item_id = item.id
            db.commit()

        import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor

        monkeypatch.setattr(executor, "SessionLocal", sessions)
        parent_lease_tokens: list[str] = []
        original_heartbeat = executor.heartbeat_runtime_step

        def record_parent_heartbeat(*args, **kwargs):
            lease_token = kwargs.get("lease_token")
            if lease_token is None and len(args) >= 3:
                lease_token = args[2]
            parent_lease_tokens.append(str(lease_token or ""))
            return original_heartbeat(*args, **kwargs)

        monkeypatch.setattr(
            executor,
            "heartbeat_runtime_step",
            record_parent_heartbeat,
        )
        processed = 0
        while process_runtime_step_once("released-design-case-sim-worker"):
            processed += 1
            assert processed <= 10
        assert len(parent_lease_tokens) >= 2
        assert all(parent_lease_tokens)

        with sessions() as db:
            aggregate = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, aggregate_id)
            assert aggregate is not None and aggregate.status == "completed"
            attempts = db.scalars(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt)
                .where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == production_id)
                .order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.sequence)
            ).all()
            assert [row.step_key for row in attempts] == [
                "case_source",
                "foreach_images",
                "aggregate_images",
                "case_text_prompt",
                "case_final_score",
                "downstream",
            ]
            assert all(row.status == "succeeded" for row in attempts)
            children = db.scalars(
                select(WorkflowForeachItem)
                .where(WorkflowForeachItem.production_run_id == production_id)
                .order_by(WorkflowForeachItem.ordinal)
            ).all()
            assert [(row.item_key, row.status) for row in children] == [
                ("image-a", "succeeded"),
                ("image-b", "succeeded"),
            ]
            production_item = aggregate.items[0]
            normalized = json.loads(production_item.result_snapshot_json)[
                "normalized_result"
            ]
            assert normalized["case_cloud_id"] == "case-42"
            assert normalized["side_effect_executed"] is False
            assert normalized["deletion_mode"] == "recommend_only"
            contract = json.loads(aggregate.correction_contract_json)
            foreach_contract_nodes = [
                node
                for node in contract["nodes"]
                if str(node.get("node_key", "")).startswith(
                    "workflow.foreach_images."
                )
            ]
            assert foreach_contract_nodes
            assert all(
                node["metadata"].get("paid_rerun_required") is not True
                for node in foreach_contract_nodes
            )

            image_replay = append_node_correction_and_replay(
                db,
                run_id=production_id,
                node_instance_id="foreach_images",
                field_path="score",
                value=90,
                reason="运营确认首张图片分数",
                actor="运营甲",
                correction_key="released-case-image-score-1",
                foreach_item_key="image-a",
                foreach_item_field_path="score",
            )
            assert "case_text_prompt" in image_replay.affected_nodes
            assert image_replay.effective_next_run is False
            evaluation = db.scalar(
                select(EvaluationResult).where(
                    EvaluationResult.production_run_id == production_id
                )
            )
            assert evaluation is not None
            replayed_outputs = json.loads(
                evaluation.workflow_node_outputs_json
            )
            corrected_image = next(
                item
                for item in replayed_outputs["foreach_images"]["output"][
                    "items"
                ]
                if item["item_key"] == "image-a"
            )
            assert corrected_image["output"]["score"] == 90

            style_replay = append_node_correction_and_replay(
                db,
                run_id=production_id,
                node_instance_id="case_text_prompt",
                field_path="style",
                value="现代简约",
                reason="运营确认案例综合风格",
                actor="运营甲",
                correction_key="released-case-style-1",
            )
            assert style_replay.normalized_result["case_review"]["style"] == (
                "现代简约"
            )
            revisions = db.scalars(
                select(BaselineTruthRevision)
                .where(BaselineTruthRevision.baseline_set_item_id == item_id)
                .order_by(BaselineTruthRevision.revision)
            ).all()
            assert len(revisions) == 1
            assert json.loads(revisions[-1].expected_result_json)["case_review"][
                "style"
            ] == "现代简约"

            level_replay = append_node_correction_and_replay(
                db,
                run_id=production_id,
                node_instance_id="case_final_score",
                field_path="level",
                value="L4",
                reason="运营确认最终等级",
                actor="运营甲",
                correction_key="released-case-level-1",
            )
            assert level_replay.normalized_result["level"] == "L4"
            revisions = db.scalars(
                select(BaselineTruthRevision)
                .where(BaselineTruthRevision.baseline_set_item_id == item_id)
                .order_by(BaselineTruthRevision.revision)
            ).all()
            assert len(revisions) == 2
            assert json.loads(revisions[-1].expected_result_json)["level"] == "L4"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()



def test_prompt_nodes_get_one_automatic_retry_in_released_manifests() -> None:
    # 提示词节点从来源清单深拷贝（max_attempts=1），发布清单必须统一覆盖成可重试；
    # 规则节点保持来源策略。
    from app.legacy_workflow_release import (
        PROMPT_NODE_RETRY_POLICY,
        _apply_prompt_retry_policy,
        _latest_ab_manifest,
    )

    base = {
        "nodes": [
            {"node_instance_id": "prompt_a", "kind": "prompt",
             "retry_policy": {"kind": "fixed", "max_attempts": 1, "delay_seconds": 0}},
            {"node_instance_id": "rule_1", "kind": "rule_module",
             "retry_policy": {"kind": "fixed", "max_attempts": 1, "delay_seconds": 0}},
        ],
        "edges": [],
    }
    manifest = _latest_ab_manifest(base, fingerprint="fp")
    by_id = {n["node_instance_id"]: n for n in manifest["nodes"]}
    assert by_id["prompt_a"]["retry_policy"] == PROMPT_NODE_RETRY_POLICY
    assert by_id["prompt_a"]["retry_policy"]["max_attempts"] == 2
    assert by_id["rule_1"]["retry_policy"]["max_attempts"] == 1
    assert _apply_prompt_retry_policy({"nodes": []}) == {"nodes": []}


def test_legacy_chain_manifests_give_prompt_nodes_one_retry() -> None:
    # 旧豆包链路两条清单的提示词节点同样要拿到一次自动重试（方舟 strict 实测仍会回类型不符字段）。
    from app.legacy_workflow_release import PROMPT_NODE_RETRY_POLICY, _apply_prompt_retry_policy

    manifest = {
        "nodes": [
            {"node_instance_id": "upstream", "kind": "upstream_connector",
             "retry_policy": {"kind": "fixed", "max_attempts": 1, "delay_seconds": 0}},
            {"node_instance_id": "prompt_single", "kind": "prompt",
             "retry_policy": {"kind": "fixed", "max_attempts": 1, "delay_seconds": 0}},
            {"node_instance_id": "foreach_images", "kind": "foreach_image",
             "retry_policy": {"kind": "fixed", "max_attempts": 1, "delay_seconds": 0}},
        ]
    }
    _apply_prompt_retry_policy(manifest)
    by_id = {n["node_instance_id"]: n for n in manifest["nodes"]}
    assert by_id["prompt_single"]["retry_policy"] == PROMPT_NODE_RETRY_POLICY
    # foreach 逐图 attempt 上限读的是 foreach 节点自己的 retry_policy
    assert by_id["foreach_images"]["retry_policy"] == PROMPT_NODE_RETRY_POLICY
    assert by_id["upstream"]["retry_policy"]["max_attempts"] == 1


def test_legacy_3d_su_chains_match_online_prompt_and_schema(tmp_path) -> None:
    """Max/SU 两条旧链路：提示词与线上 MD5 一致，合同枚举逐字对齐，图片原样上传。"""

    import hashlib

    from app.legacy_workflow_release import (
        LEGACY_3D_SU_VARIANTS,
        _prompt_text,
        materialize_legacy_workflows,
    )

    engine = _prepare_database(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            materialize_legacy_workflows(db, actor="system:3d-su-test", apply=True)
            db.commit()
            expected_md5 = {"max": "4f737ce1c20359c6a97122febd9727ec", "su": "19d7a4532c26be8e56b28070af12a826"}
            for variant, spec in LEGACY_3D_SU_VARIANTS.items():
                prompt_text = _prompt_text(spec["prompt_file"])
                assert hashlib.md5(prompt_text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest() == expected_md5[variant]
                workflow = _workflow(db, spec["workflow_key"])
                manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(workflow)
                assert manifest["category_key"] == "model_3d_su"
                assert manifest["run_modes"] == ["evaluation", "prompt_regression", "stock_regression"]
                assert manifest["resource_policy"]["activated"] is False
                assert manifest["resource_policy"]["legacy_source"]["res_type"] == spec["res_type"]
                prompt_node = next(node for node in manifest["nodes"] if node["kind"] == "prompt")
                config = prompt_node["config"]
                assert config["thinking_level"] == "none" and config["structured_output"] is True
                assert config["vision_input_policy"]["roles"]["target"]["preprocess"] == {"mode": "original"}
                schema = config["output_contract"]["schema"]
                expected_fields = {
                    "title", "seotitle", "category", "style", "tags", "cons", "score", "design", "reason", "hwreason",
                }
                assert set(schema["properties"]) == expected_fields
                assert set(schema["required"]) == expected_fields
                assert schema["additionalProperties"] is False
                assert schema["properties"]["reason"]["enum"] == spec["reason_enum"]
                assert schema["properties"]["score"] == {"type": "integer", "description": "<0-100的数字>", "minimum": 0, "maximum": 100}
                assert len(schema["properties"]["hwreason"]["enum"]) == 11
                prompt = db.get(PromptVersion, config["prompt_version_id"])
                assert prompt.category_key == "model_3d_su" and prompt.system_prompt == prompt_text
                assert prompt.status == "draft"
            assert LEGACY_3D_SU_VARIANTS["su"]["reason_enum"][1] == "是未渲染模型图"
            assert "是未渲染模型图" not in LEGACY_3D_SU_VARIANTS["max"]["reason_enum"]
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_3d_su_subset_release_uses_legacy_inspiration_base_without_strict_source(tmp_path, monkeypatch) -> None:
    """运营把灵感图评测切到 LEGACY 提示词后，严格候选取源失败；3D/SU 仍可单独物化。"""

    from app.legacy_workflow_release import (
        LEGACY_3D_SU_WORKFLOW_KEYS,
        LegacyWorkflowReleaseError,
        materialize_legacy_workflows,
    )

    engine = _prepare_database(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            # 先整体发布一次，得到灵感图旧链路作为基座
            full = materialize_legacy_workflows(db, actor="system:3d-su-subset", apply=True)
            db.commit()
            full_ids = {item.workflow_key: item.workflow_version_id for item in full.workflows}
            preview = materialize_legacy_workflows(
                db, actor="system:3d-su-subset", apply=False, workflow_keys=list(LEGACY_3D_SU_WORKFLOW_KEYS)
            )
            assert preview.mode == "preview" and {item.workflow_key for item in preview.workflows} == set(LEGACY_3D_SU_WORKFLOW_KEYS)
            # 让严格候选发布不可用（服务器上灵感图评测已切到 LEGACY 提示词时就是这种状态）
            from app import legacy_workflow_release as module
            from app.strict_prompt_release import StrictPromptReleaseError

            def _refuse(*_args, **_kwargs):
                raise StrictPromptReleaseError("strict_release_prompt_stage_unsupported", "LEGACY 不在候选范围")

            monkeypatch.setattr(module, "materialize_strict_vision_candidates", _refuse)
            with pytest.raises(StrictPromptReleaseError):
                materialize_legacy_workflows(db, actor="x", apply=False)
            subset = materialize_legacy_workflows(
                db, actor="system:3d-su-subset", apply=True, workflow_keys=list(LEGACY_3D_SU_WORKFLOW_KEYS)
            )
            db.commit()
            assert subset.mode == "apply"
            for item in subset.workflows:
                # 指纹不依赖严格候选，因此与整体发布得到的是同一版本，幂等不新增
                assert item.workflow_version_id == full_ids[item.workflow_key]
            with pytest.raises(LegacyWorkflowReleaseError):
                materialize_legacy_workflows(db, actor="x", apply=False, workflow_keys=["inspiration_image.legacy_doubao_review"])
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_3d_su_subset_release_can_override_model_registry(tmp_path) -> None:
    """--model-id 把 3D/SU 候选的提示词节点切到指定登记模型，并生成独立版本。"""

    from app.legacy_workflow_release import (
        LEGACY_3D_SU_WORKFLOW_KEYS,
        LegacyWorkflowReleaseError,
        materialize_legacy_workflows,
    )
    from app.models import ModelConfig, ModelRegistryEntry

    engine = _prepare_database(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = materialize_legacy_workflows(db, actor="system:3d-su-model", apply=True)
            db.commit()
            base_ids = {item.workflow_key: item.workflow_version_id for item in baseline.workflows}
            source = db.scalar(select(ModelConfig).where(ModelConfig.active.is_(True)))
            legacy_config = ModelConfig(
                name="豆包 1.8 对照", provider=source.provider, protocol=source.protocol,
                base_url=source.base_url, api_path=source.api_path, model_id="doubao-seed-1-8-251228",
                encrypted_api_key=source.encrypted_api_key, benchmark_enabled=True, active=True,
                capabilities_json=source.capabilities_json,
                vision_capability_profile_json=source.vision_capability_profile_json,
                pricing_contract_json=source.pricing_contract_json,
                input_micros_per_million_tokens=source.input_micros_per_million_tokens,
                output_micros_per_million_tokens=source.output_micros_per_million_tokens,
                max_input_tokens=source.max_input_tokens,
            )
            db.add(legacy_config)
            db.flush()
            registry = ModelRegistryEntry(
                role="main", name="豆包 1.8 对照", provider=source.provider, protocol=source.protocol,
                base_url=source.base_url, api_path=source.api_path, model_id="doubao-seed-1-8-251228",
                capabilities_json=source.capabilities_json,
                vision_capability_profile_json=source.vision_capability_profile_json,
                pricing_contract_json=source.pricing_contract_json,
                source_model_config_id=legacy_config.id, active=True,
            )
            db.add(registry)
            db.commit()
            with pytest.raises(LegacyWorkflowReleaseError):
                materialize_legacy_workflows(db, actor="x", apply=True, workflow_keys=list(LEGACY_3D_SU_WORKFLOW_KEYS), model_id="doubao-not-registered")
            db.rollback()
            switched = materialize_legacy_workflows(
                db, actor="system:3d-su-model", apply=True,
                workflow_keys=list(LEGACY_3D_SU_WORKFLOW_KEYS), model_id="doubao-seed-1-8-251228",
            )
            db.commit()
            for item in switched.workflows:
                assert item.workflow_version_id != base_ids[item.workflow_key]
                manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(db.get(WorkflowVersion, item.workflow_version_id))
                prompt_node = next(node for node in manifest["nodes"] if node["kind"] == "prompt")
                assert prompt_node["config"]["model_registry_id"] == registry.id
                assert manifest["resource_policy"]["legacy_source"]["model_override"] == "doubao-seed-1-8-251228"
            again = materialize_legacy_workflows(
                db, actor="system:3d-su-model", apply=True,
                workflow_keys=list(LEGACY_3D_SU_WORKFLOW_KEYS), model_id="doubao-seed-1-8-251228",
            )
            assert {item.workflow_version_id for item in again.workflows} == {item.workflow_version_id for item in switched.workflows}
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_level_scale.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from app.level_scale import (
    DEFAULT_LEVEL_SCALE_VERSION,
    LevelScaleError,
    assert_level_enabled,
    is_level_enabled,
    resolve_level_scale,
    score_to_level,
    to_threshold_table,
)


def _four_level_scale() -> dict:
    return {
        "version": DEFAULT_LEVEL_SCALE_VERSION,
        "levels": [
            {"level": "L1", "enabled": True, "min_score": 80, "display_name": "优选"},
            {"level": "L2", "enabled": True, "min_score": 60, "display_name": "良好"},
            {"level": "L3", "enabled": True, "min_score": 40, "display_name": "常规"},
            {"level": "L4", "enabled": True, "min_score": 0, "display_name": "过滤"},
            {"level": "L5", "enabled": False, "display_name": "停用"},
        ],
    }


def test_missing_scale_keeps_default_five_levels() -> None:
    resolved = resolve_level_scale({})
    assert resolved["enabled_levels"] == ["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"]
    assert resolved["thresholds"][-1] == {"min_score": 0, "level": "L5"}
    assert score_to_level(0, resolved) == "L5"


def test_category_can_disable_l5_and_make_l4_the_catch_all() -> None:
    resolved = resolve_level_scale({"level_scale": _four_level_scale()})
    assert resolved["enabled_levels"] == ["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4"]
    assert resolved["disabled_levels"] == ["L5"]
    assert resolved["thresholds"][-1] == {"min_score": 0, "level": "L4"}
    assert score_to_level(0, resolved) == "L4"
    assert score_to_level(39, resolved) == "L4"
    assert "L5" not in {score_to_level(score, resolved) for score in range(101)}


def test_category_can_disable_a_middle_level_without_reversing_direction() -> None:
    scale = _four_level_scale()
    scale["levels"][1] = {"level": "L2", "enabled": False, "display_name": "停用"}
    scale["levels"][2]["min_score"] = 60
    resolved = resolve_level_scale({"level_scale": scale})
    assert resolved["enabled_levels"] == ["L1", "L3", "L4"]
    assert [item["level"] for item in resolved["thresholds"]] == ["L1", "L3", "L4"]


def test_legacy_level_thresholds_remain_read_compatible() -> None:
    resolved = resolve_level_scale(
        {
            "level_thresholds": [
                {"min_score": 75, "level": "L1"},
                {"min_score": 45, "level": "L2"},
                {"min_score": 0, "level": "L3"},
            ]
        }
    )
    assert resolved["source"] == "legacy_level_thresholds"
    assert resolved["enabled_levels"] == ["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"]
    assert resolved["disabled_levels"] == []
    assert score_to_level(44, resolved) == "L3"


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("mutate", "code"),
    [
        (lambda scale: scale.update(version="unknown"), "version_unsupported"),
        (lambda scale: scale.update(levels=[]), "levels_invalid"),
        (
            lambda scale: scale["levels"].append(
                {"level": "L1", "enabled": True, "min_score": 10}
            ),
            "level_duplicate",
        ),
        (
            lambda scale: scale["levels"][4].update(enabled=True, min_score=10),
            "threshold_order_invalid",
        ),
        (
            lambda scale: scale["levels"][3].update(min_score=1),
            "catch_all_missing",
        ),
        (
            lambda scale: scale["levels"][4].update(min_score=0),
            "disabled_level_threshold_present",
        ),
    ],
)
def test_invalid_scale_fails_closed(mutate, code: str) -> None:
    scale = _four_level_scale()
    mutate(scale)
    with pytest.raises(LevelScaleError) as excinfo:
        resolve_level_scale({"level_scale": scale})
    assert excinfo.value.code == code


def test_level_scale_and_legacy_thresholds_cannot_coexist() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(LevelScaleError) as excinfo:
        resolve_level_scale(
            {
                "level_scale": _four_level_scale(),
                "level_thresholds": [{"min_score": 0, "level": "L1"}],
            }
        )
    assert excinfo.value.code == "ambiguous_configuration"


def test_enabled_level_display_names_are_preserved() -> None:
    resolved = resolve_level_scale({"level_scale": _four_level_scale()})
    assert resolved["display_names"] == {
        "L1": "优选",
        "L2": "良好",
        "L3": "常规",
        "L4": "过滤",
    }


def test_disabled_level_reference_fails_closed() -> None:
    resolved = resolve_level_scale({"level_scale": _four_level_scale()})
    assert assert_level_enabled("L4", resolved) == "L4"
    assert is_level_enabled("L5", resolved) is False
    with pytest.raises(LevelScaleError) as excinfo:
        assert_level_enabled("L5", resolved)
    assert excinfo.value.code == "level_not_enabled"


def test_threshold_projection_excludes_disabled_levels() -> None:
    resolved = resolve_level_scale({"level_scale": _four_level_scale()})
    assert to_threshold_table(resolved) == [
        {"min_score": 80, "level": "L1"},
        {"min_score": 60, "level": "L2"},
        {"min_score": 40, "level": "L3"},
        {"min_score": 0, "level": "L4"},
    ]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("bad", [None, "50", 1.5, True])
def test_score_to_level_rejects_non_integer_score(bad: object) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(LevelScaleError) as excinfo:
        score_to_level(bad, resolve_level_scale({}))
    assert excinfo.value.code == "score_invalid"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_level_semantics.py =====
"""Unified level-semantics tests.

Covers the pure ``level_semantics`` helpers and migration 54:
- the sole production constant reuses the aggregator's.
- ``describe_level_semantics`` returns the unified direction and
  fail-closed ``known=False`` for unknown versions (no raise).
- migration 54 is idempotent and table-guarded (missing table does not crash).
- adding ``level_semantics_version`` leaves authoritative fields intact.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Iterator

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, text
from sqlalchemy.engine import Connection
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.category_evaluation_aggregator import LEVEL_SEMANTICS_VERSION
from app.database import Base
from app.level_semantics import (
    UNIFIED_LEVEL_SEMANTICS_VERSION,
    describe_level_semantics,
)
import app.level_semantics as level_semantics
from app.migrations import run_migrations
from app.migrations.runner import (
    _migration_054_add_evaluation_result_level_semantics,
)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# constants
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #


def test_unified_constant_reuses_aggregator() -> None:
    assert UNIFIED_LEVEL_SEMANTICS_VERSION == "doc-l5-worst-v1"
    assert UNIFIED_LEVEL_SEMANTICS_VERSION == LEVEL_SEMANTICS_VERSION


def test_removed_v1_l5_best_constant_is_not_exposed() -> None:
    assert not hasattr(level_semantics, "LEVEL_SEMANTICS_V1_L5_BEST")


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# describe_level_semantics
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #


def test_describe_unified_l1_best() -> None:
    desc = describe_level_semantics(UNIFIED_LEVEL_SEMANTICS_VERSION)
    assert desc["known"] is True
    assert desc["best_level"] == "L1"
    assert desc["worst_level"] == "L5"
    assert desc["levels"]["L1"] == "best"
    assert desc["levels"]["L5"] == "worst"
@pytest.mark.parametrize("bad", ["", "l5-best", "v1-l5-best", "v2", "unknown"])
def test_describe_unknown_version_is_fail_closed(bad: str) -> None:
    desc = describe_level_semantics(bad)
    assert desc["known"] is False
    assert desc["best_level"] is None
    assert desc["worst_level"] is None
    assert desc["levels"] == {}


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# migration 54 idempotency + table guard
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #


@pytest.fixture
def connection() -> Iterator[Connection]:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    with engine.begin() as conn:
        yield conn
    engine.dispose()


def _columns(conn: Connection, table: str) -> set[str]:
    return {row[1] for row in conn.exec_driver_sql(f"PRAGMA table_info({table})")}


def test_migration_54_missing_table_does_not_crash(connection: Connection) -> None:
    # No evaluation_results table at all → guard returns early, no exception.
    _migration_054_add_evaluation_result_level_semantics(connection)
    assert "evaluation_results" not in {
        row[0]
        for row in connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'"
        )
    }


def test_migration_54_adds_column_idempotently(connection: Connection) -> None:
    connection.exec_driver_sql(
        "CREATE TABLE evaluation_results (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, level VARCHAR(10))"
    )
    _migration_054_add_evaluation_result_level_semantics(connection)
    assert "level_semantics_version" in _columns(connection, "evaluation_results")
    # Second run is a no-op (must not raise on duplicate ALTER).
    _migration_054_add_evaluation_result_level_semantics(connection)
    assert "level_semantics_version" in _columns(connection, "evaluation_results")


def test_full_migrations_install_column() -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with engine.begin() as conn:
        run_migrations(conn)
        cols = {
            row[1] for row in conn.exec_driver_sql("PRAGMA table_info(evaluation_results)")
        }
    engine.dispose()
    assert "level_semantics_version" in cols
    # The scaffold never touches the authoritative score/level columns.
    assert {"score", "level"}.issubset(cols)


def test_new_column_is_nullable_default_none() -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with engine.begin() as conn:
        run_migrations(conn)
        info = {
            row[1]: row
            for row in conn.exec_driver_sql("PRAGMA table_info(evaluation_results)")
        }
    engine.dispose()
    row = info["level_semantics_version"]
    # PRAGMA table_info: col[3] = notnull flag (0 → nullable), col[4] = default.
    assert row[3] == 0
    assert row[4] is None

from typing import cast

def test_mysql_local_connector_e2e_routes_and_reconciles_all_targets(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
) -> None:

    assert report["external_network_calls"] == 0

        "shadow_kg_domestic": 1,

        "shadow_labels": 2,

    scenarios = report["scenarios"]

        "knowledge_graph_domestic",
        "knowledge_graph_overseas",
        "inspiration_image",
        "model_3d_su",

            item["outbox_contract_version_id"]
